# VNB structure-grouped index

Auto-generated by `(catalog)`.  48 structures (26 shape + 22 refinement/instance), 21 views.

Each section lists a structure's defining predicate, the
theorems quantifying over it, and the view-as declarations
into/out of it.  Every structure is declared by
`declare-structure`.  A *shape* structure declares slots
(`(carriers ...)`, `(op ...)`, `(constant ...)`) and has a
slot listing here; a *refinement* declares
`(same-shape-as PARENT)` and laws, inherits the parent's
slots, and notes its parent instead.  Anchors are
lower-case-kebab: `#monoid`, `#ring-additive-ag`, etc.
Flat theorem listing in `THEOREMS.md`.

## Structures

### abelian-group
<a id="abelian-group"></a>

*Declared in* [`structure-library/abelian-group.scm`](../structure-library/abelian-group.scm).

*Kind.* Shape structure — `declare-structure` with slot clauses.

*Slots* (4): `carr` carrier; `opr` operation cartesian(carr, carr) -> carr; `iden` constant in carr; `inv` operation carr -> carr.

*Default arrows* (`is-hom-abelian-group-def`): forall([a, b, f], is-hom-abelian-group(a, b, f) iff is-abelian-group(a) and is-abelian-group(b) and f in fun(carr(a), carr(b)) and forall([x1_ in carr(a), x2_ in carr(a)], f((opr(a))(x1_, x2_)) = (opr(b))(f(x1_), f(x2_))) and f(iden(a)) = iden(b) and forall([x1_ in carr(a)], f((inv(a))(x1_)) = (inv(b))(f(x1_))))

*Declaration.*

```scheme
(declare-structure abelian-group
  (carriers carr)
  (op opr (cartesian carr carr) carr)
  (constant iden carr)
  (op inv carr carr)
  (property is-associative opr carr)
  (property is-identity opr iden carr)
  (property has-inverses opr iden inv carr)
  (property is-commutative opr carr))
```

*Defining predicate* (as stored):

- `is-abelian-group` — forall([s], is-abelian-group(s) iff length(s) = 4 and carr(s) in set and opr(s) in fun(cartesian(carr(s), carr(s)), carr(s)) and iden(s) in carr(s) and inv(s) in fun(carr(s), carr(s)) and is-associative(opr(s), carr(s)) and is-identity(opr(s), iden(s), carr(s)) and has-inverses(opr(s), iden(s), inv(s), carr(s)) and is-commutative(opr(s), carr(s)))

*Theorems quantifying over `is-abelian-group`.*

- `abelian-group-as-monoid-is-monoid` — forall([r], is-abelian-group(r) implies is-monoid(abelian-group-as-monoid(r)))
- `abelian-group-assoc` — forall([s], is-abelian-group(s) implies forall([a in carr(s), b in carr(s), c in carr(s)], (opr(s))((opr(s))(a, b), c) = (opr(s))(a, (opr(s))(b, c))))
- `abelian-group-assoc-rev` — forall([s], is-abelian-group(s) implies forall([a in carr(s), b in carr(s), c in carr(s)], (opr(s))(a, (opr(s))(b, c)) = (opr(s))((opr(s))(a, b), c)))
- `abelian-group-idempotent-is-id` — forall([s], is-abelian-group(s) implies forall([a in carr(s)], (opr(s))(a, a) = a implies a = iden(s)))
- `abelian-group-idempotent-is-id-rev` — forall([s], is-abelian-group(s) implies forall([a in carr(s)], (opr(s))(a, a) = a implies iden(s) = a))
- `abelian-group-inv-iden` — forall([g], is-abelian-group(g) implies (inv(g))(iden(g)) = iden(g))
- `abelian-group-inv-iden-rev` — forall([g], is-abelian-group(g) implies iden(g) = (inv(g))(iden(g)))
- `abelian-group-inv-inv` — forall([s], is-abelian-group(s) implies forall([a in carr(s)], (inv(s))((inv(s))(a)) = a))
- `abelian-group-inv-inv-rev` — forall([s], is-abelian-group(s) implies forall([a in carr(s)], a = (inv(s))((inv(s))(a))))
- `abelian-group-inv-opr` — forall([s], is-abelian-group(s) implies forall([a in carr(s), b in carr(s)], (inv(s))((opr(s))(a, b)) = (opr(s))((inv(s))(a), (inv(s))(b))))
- `abelian-group-inv-opr-rev` — forall([s], is-abelian-group(s) implies forall([a in carr(s), b in carr(s)], (opr(s))((inv(s))(a), (inv(s))(b)) = (inv(s))((opr(s))(a, b))))
- `abelian-group-inverse-unique` — forall([s], is-abelian-group(s) implies forall([a in carr(s), b in carr(s)], (opr(s))(a, b) = iden(s) implies b = (inv(s))(a)))
- `abelian-group-inverse-unique-rev` — forall([s], is-abelian-group(s) implies forall([a in carr(s), b in carr(s)], (opr(s))(a, b) = iden(s) implies (inv(s))(a) = b))
- `abelian-group-is-group` — forall([s], is-abelian-group(s) implies is-group(s))
- `abelian-group-opr-comm` — forall([s], is-abelian-group(s) implies forall([a in carr(s), b in carr(s)], (opr(s))(a, b) = (opr(s))(b, a)))
- `abelian-group-opr-comm-rev` — forall([s], is-abelian-group(s) implies forall([a in carr(s), b in carr(s)], (opr(s))(b, a) = (opr(s))(a, b)))
- `abelian-group-opr-interchange` — forall([s], is-abelian-group(s) implies forall([a in carr(s), b in carr(s), c in carr(s), d in carr(s)], (opr(s))((opr(s))(a, b), (opr(s))(c, d)) = (opr(s))((opr(s))(a, c), (opr(s))(b, d))))

- `abelian-group-right-id` — forall([s], is-abelian-group(s) implies forall([a in carr(s)], (opr(s))(a, iden(s)) = a))
- `abelian-group-right-id-rev` — forall([s], is-abelian-group(s) implies forall([a in carr(s)], a = (opr(s))(a, iden(s))))

- `finsum-in-subset` — forall([ag], is-abelian-group(ag) implies forall([sm], iden(ag) in sm implies forall([x_ in sm, y_ in sm], (opr(ag))(x_, y_) in sm) implies forall([s in set], card(s) in nn implies forall([f in fun(s, carr(ag))], forall([z in s], f(z) in sm) implies finsum(ag, f, s) in sm))))
- `finsum-insert-ag` — forall([ag], is-abelian-group(ag) implies forall([x in set], card(x) in nn implies forall([k in set], not(k in x) implies forall([f in fun(union(x, pair(k, k)), carr(ag))], finsum(ag, f, union(x, pair(k, k))) = (opr(ag))(finsum(ag, f, x), f(k))))))
- `finsum-insert-ag-rev` — forall([ag], is-abelian-group(ag) implies forall([x in set], card(x) in nn implies forall([k in set], not(k in x) implies forall([f in fun(union(x, pair(k, k)), carr(ag))], (opr(ag))(finsum(ag, f, x), f(k)) = finsum(ag, f, union(x, pair(k, k)))))))
- `finsum-insert-ptwise` — forall([ag], is-abelian-group(ag) implies forall([s_ in set], card(s_) in nn implies forall([k_ in set], not(k_ in s_) implies forall([f_], forall([z_ in union(s_, pair(k_, k_))], f_(z_) in carr(ag)) implies finsum(ag, f_, union(s_, pair(k_, k_))) = (opr(ag))(finsum(ag, f_, s_), f_(k_))))))
- `finsum-insert-ptwise-rev` — forall([ag], is-abelian-group(ag) implies forall([s_ in set], card(s_) in nn implies forall([k_ in set], not(k_ in s_) implies forall([f_], forall([z_ in union(s_, pair(k_, k_))], f_(z_) in carr(ag)) implies (opr(ag))(finsum(ag, f_, s_), f_(k_)) = finsum(ag, f_, union(s_, pair(k_, k_)))))))
- `finsum-interval-peel` — forall([ag], is-abelian-group(ag) implies forall([n in nn, f in fun(interval(1, succ(n)), carr(ag))], finsum(ag, f, interval(1, succ(n))) = (opr(ag))(finsum(ag, f, interval(1, n)), f(succ(n)))))
- `finsum-interval-peel-rev` — forall([ag], is-abelian-group(ag) implies forall([n in nn, f in fun(interval(1, succ(n)), carr(ag))], (opr(ag))(finsum(ag, f, interval(1, n)), f(succ(n))) = finsum(ag, f, interval(1, succ(n)))))
- `finsum-interval-shift` — forall([ag], is-abelian-group(ag) implies forall([n in nn, f in fun(interval(1, succ(n)), carr(ag))], finsum(ag, f, interval(1, succ(n))) = (opr(ag))(f(1), finsum(ag, vnb-lambda(z, interval(1, n), f(succ(z))), interval(1, n)))))
- `finsum-interval-shift-rev` — forall([ag], is-abelian-group(ag) implies forall([n in nn, f in fun(interval(1, succ(n)), carr(ag))], (opr(ag))(f(1), finsum(ag, vnb-lambda(z, interval(1, n), f(succ(z))), interval(1, n))) = finsum(ag, f, interval(1, succ(n)))))
- `finsum-ord-peel` — forall([ag], is-abelian-group(ag) implies forall([n in nn, f in fun(ord-segment(succ(n)), carr(ag))], finsum(ag, f, ord-segment(succ(n))) = (opr(ag))(finsum(ag, f, ord-segment(n)), f(n))))
- `finsum-ord-peel-rev` — forall([ag], is-abelian-group(ag) implies forall([n in nn, f in fun(ord-segment(succ(n)), carr(ag))], (opr(ag))(finsum(ag, f, ord-segment(n)), f(n)) = finsum(ag, f, ord-segment(succ(n)))))
- `finsum-reindex-ag` — forall([ag], is-abelian-group(ag) implies forall([s in set], card(s) in nn implies forall([t], t in set and card(t) in nn implies forall([phi in bijection(t, s), f in fun(s, carr(ag))], finsum(ag, f, s) = finsum(ag, vnb-lambda(z, t, f(phi(z))), t)))))
- `finsum-reindex-ag-rev` — forall([ag], is-abelian-group(ag) implies forall([s in set], card(s) in nn implies forall([t], t in set and card(t) in nn implies forall([phi in bijection(t, s), f in fun(s, carr(ag))], finsum(ag, vnb-lambda(z, t, f(phi(z))), t) = finsum(ag, f, s)))))
- `finsum-reindex-inverse` — forall([ag], is-abelian-group(ag) implies forall([s in set], card(s) in nn implies forall([t], t in set and card(t) in nn implies forall([phi in fun(t, s), psi in fun(s, t)], forall([u_ in t], psi(phi(u_)) = u_) implies forall([v_ in s], phi(psi(v_)) = v_) implies forall([f in fun(s, carr(ag))], finsum(ag, f, s) = finsum(ag, vnb-lambda(z, t, f(phi(z))), t))))))
- `finsum-reindex-inverse-ptwise` — forall([ag], is-abelian-group(ag) implies forall([s in set], card(s) in nn implies forall([t], t in set and card(t) in nn implies forall([phi in fun(t, s), psi in fun(s, t)], forall([u_ in t], psi(phi(u_)) = u_) implies forall([v_ in s], phi(psi(v_)) = v_) implies forall([ff], forall([z_ in s], ff(z_) in carr(ag)) implies finsum(ag, ff, s) = finsum(ag, vnb-lambda(z, t, ff(phi(z))), t))))))
- `finsum-reindex-inverse-ptwise-rev` — forall([ag], is-abelian-group(ag) implies forall([s in set], card(s) in nn implies forall([t], t in set and card(t) in nn implies forall([phi in fun(t, s), psi in fun(s, t)], forall([u_ in t], psi(phi(u_)) = u_) implies forall([v_ in s], phi(psi(v_)) = v_) implies forall([ff], forall([z_ in s], ff(z_) in carr(ag)) implies finsum(ag, vnb-lambda(z, t, ff(phi(z))), t) = finsum(ag, ff, s))))))
- `finsum-reindex-inverse-rev` — forall([ag], is-abelian-group(ag) implies forall([s in set], card(s) in nn implies forall([t], t in set and card(t) in nn implies forall([phi in fun(t, s), psi in fun(s, t)], forall([u_ in t], psi(phi(u_)) = u_) implies forall([v_ in s], phi(psi(v_)) = v_) implies forall([f in fun(s, carr(ag))], finsum(ag, vnb-lambda(z, t, f(phi(z))), t) = finsum(ag, f, s))))))
- `finsum-single-support` — forall([ag], is-abelian-group(ag) implies forall([s in set], card(s) in nn implies forall([f in fun(s, carr(ag)), i0 in s], forall([j in s], not(j = i0) implies f(j) = iden(ag)) implies finsum(ag, f, s) = f(i0))))
- `finsum-single-support-rev` — forall([ag], is-abelian-group(ag) implies forall([s in set], card(s) in nn implies forall([f in fun(s, carr(ag)), i0 in s], forall([j in s], not(j = i0) implies f(j) = iden(ag)) implies f(i0) = finsum(ag, f, s))))
- `finsum-singleton` — forall([ag], is-abelian-group(ag) implies forall([x in set, f in fun(pair(x, x), carr(ag))], finsum(ag, f, pair(x, x)) = f(x)))
- `finsum-singleton-ptwise` — forall([ag], is-abelian-group(ag) implies forall([x_ in set, f_], f_(x_) in carr(ag) implies finsum(ag, f_, pair(x_, x_)) = f_(x_)))
- `finsum-singleton-ptwise-rev` — forall([ag], is-abelian-group(ag) implies forall([x_ in set, f_], f_(x_) in carr(ag) implies f_(x_) = finsum(ag, f_, pair(x_, x_))))
- `finsum-singleton-rev` — forall([ag], is-abelian-group(ag) implies forall([x in set, f in fun(pair(x, x), carr(ag))], f(x) = finsum(ag, f, pair(x, x))))
- `finsum-two-support` — forall([ag], is-abelian-group(ag) implies forall([s in set], card(s) in nn implies forall([f in fun(s, carr(ag)), i0 in s, i1 in s], not(i0 = i1) implies forall([j in s], not(j = i0) implies not(j = i1) implies f(j) = iden(ag)) implies finsum(ag, f, s) = (opr(ag))(f(i0), f(i1)))))
- `finsum-two-support-rev` — forall([ag], is-abelian-group(ag) implies forall([s in set], card(s) in nn implies forall([f in fun(s, carr(ag)), i0 in s, i1 in s], not(i0 = i1) implies forall([j in s], not(j = i0) implies not(j = i1) implies f(j) = iden(ag)) implies (opr(ag))(f(i0), f(i1)) = finsum(ag, f, s))))
- `finsum-type` — forall([ag], is-abelian-group(ag) implies forall([s in set], card(s) in nn implies forall([f in fun(s, carr(ag))], finsum(ag, f, s) in carr(ag))))
- `finsum-type-ptwise` — forall([ag], is-abelian-group(ag) implies forall([s in set], card(s) in nn implies forall([f], forall([z in s], f(z) in carr(ag)) implies finsum(ag, f, s) in carr(ag))))
- `finsum-union-disjoint` — forall([ag], is-abelian-group(ag) implies forall([s_ in set], card(s_) in nn implies forall([t_ in set], card(t_) in nn implies forall([z_ in t_], not(z_ in s_)) implies forall([f_], forall([z_ in union(s_, t_)], f_(z_) in carr(ag)) implies finsum(ag, f_, union(s_, t_)) = (opr(ag))(finsum(ag, f_, s_), finsum(ag, f_, t_))))))
- `finsum-union-disjoint-fun` — forall([ag], is-abelian-group(ag) implies forall([s_ in set], card(s_) in nn implies forall([t_ in set], card(t_) in nn implies intersection(s_, t_) = empty-set implies forall([f_ in fun(union(s_, t_), carr(ag))], finsum(ag, f_, union(s_, t_)) = (opr(ag))(finsum(ag, f_, s_), finsum(ag, f_, t_))))))
- `finsum-union-disjoint-fun-rev` — forall([ag], is-abelian-group(ag) implies forall([s_ in set], card(s_) in nn implies forall([t_ in set], card(t_) in nn implies intersection(s_, t_) = empty-set implies forall([f_ in fun(union(s_, t_), carr(ag))], (opr(ag))(finsum(ag, f_, s_), finsum(ag, f_, t_)) = finsum(ag, f_, union(s_, t_))))))
- `finsum-union-disjoint-rev` — forall([ag], is-abelian-group(ag) implies forall([s_ in set], card(s_) in nn implies forall([t_ in set], card(t_) in nn implies forall([z_ in t_], not(z_ in s_)) implies forall([f_], forall([z_ in union(s_, t_)], f_(z_) in carr(ag)) implies (opr(ag))(finsum(ag, f_, s_), finsum(ag, f_, t_)) = finsum(ag, f_, union(s_, t_))))))
- `hom-abelian-group-id` — forall([a], is-abelian-group(a) implies is-hom-abelian-group(a, a, id-fun(carr(a))))
- `mpow-add-abelian-group-as-monoid` — forall([r], is-abelian-group(r) implies forall([x in carr(r), j in nn, k in nn], mpow(abelian-group-as-monoid(r), x, j + k) = (opr(r))(mpow(abelian-group-as-monoid(r), x, j), mpow(abelian-group-as-monoid(r), x, k))))
- `mpow-add-abelian-group-as-monoid-rev` — forall([r], is-abelian-group(r) implies forall([x in carr(r), j in nn, k in nn], (opr(r))(mpow(abelian-group-as-monoid(r), x, j), mpow(abelian-group-as-monoid(r), x, k)) = mpow(abelian-group-as-monoid(r), x, j + k)))
- `mpow-one-abelian-group-as-monoid` — forall([r], is-abelian-group(r) implies forall([x in carr(r)], mpow(abelian-group-as-monoid(r), x, 1) = x))
- `mpow-one-abelian-group-as-monoid-rev` — forall([r], is-abelian-group(r) implies forall([x in carr(r)], x = mpow(abelian-group-as-monoid(r), x, 1)))
- `rb4-finsum-ag-pad-ptwise` — forall([ag], is-abelian-group(ag) implies forall([t in set], card(t) in nn implies forall([r], forall([z_ in r], z_ in t) implies forall([g], forall([z_ in t], g(z_) in carr(ag)) implies forall([z_ in t], not(z_ in r) implies g(z_) = iden(ag)) implies finsum(ag, g, t) = finsum(ag, g, r)))))
- `rb4-finsum-ag-pad-ptwise-rev` — forall([ag], is-abelian-group(ag) implies forall([t in set], card(t) in nn implies forall([r], forall([z_ in r], z_ in t) implies forall([g], forall([z_ in t], g(z_) in carr(ag)) implies forall([z_ in t], not(z_ in r) implies g(z_) = iden(ag)) implies finsum(ag, g, r) = finsum(ag, g, t)))))
- `sum-ag-as-reduce` — forall([ag], is-abelian-group(ag) implies forall([f in fun(nn, carr(ag)), n], n in nn and 1 <= n implies sum-ag(ag, f, n) = reduce(opr(ag), f, n)))
- `sum-ag-as-reduce-rev` — forall([ag], is-abelian-group(ag) implies forall([f in fun(nn, carr(ag)), n], n in nn and 1 <= n implies reduce(opr(ag), f, n) = sum-ag(ag, f, n)))
- `sum-ag-singleton` — forall([ag], is-abelian-group(ag) implies forall([f in fun(nn, carr(ag))], sum-ag(ag, f, 1) = f(0)))
- `sum-ag-singleton-rev` — forall([ag], is-abelian-group(ag) implies forall([f in fun(nn, carr(ag))], f(0) = sum-ag(ag, f, 1)))
- `sum-ag-type` — forall([ag], is-abelian-group(ag) implies forall([f in fun(nn, carr(ag)), n in nn], sum-ag(ag, f, n) in carr(ag)))
- `zz-act-add` — forall([g], is-abelian-group(g) implies forall([j in zz, k in zz, a in carr(g)], zz-act(g, j + k, a) = (opr(g))(zz-act(g, j, a), zz-act(g, k, a))))
- `zz-act-add-rev` — forall([g], is-abelian-group(g) implies forall([j in zz, k in zz, a in carr(g)], (opr(g))(zz-act(g, j, a), zz-act(g, k, a)) = zz-act(g, j + k, a)))
- `zz-act-assoc` — forall([g], is-abelian-group(g) implies forall([j in zz, k in zz, a in carr(g)], zz-act(g, j * k, a) = zz-act(g, j, zz-act(g, k, a))))
- `zz-act-assoc-rev` — forall([g], is-abelian-group(g) implies forall([j in zz, k in zz, a in carr(g)], zz-act(g, j, zz-act(g, k, a)) = zz-act(g, j * k, a)))
- `zz-act-distrib` — forall([g], is-abelian-group(g) implies forall([k in zz, a in carr(g), b in carr(g)], zz-act(g, k, (opr(g))(a, b)) = (opr(g))(zz-act(g, k, a), zz-act(g, k, b))))
- `zz-act-distrib-rev` — forall([g], is-abelian-group(g) implies forall([k in zz, a in carr(g), b in carr(g)], (opr(g))(zz-act(g, k, a), zz-act(g, k, b)) = zz-act(g, k, (opr(g))(a, b))))
- `zz-act-neg` — forall([g], is-abelian-group(g) implies forall([k in nn, a in carr(g)], zz-act(g, -k, a) = (inv(g))(mpow(abelian-group-as-monoid(g), a, k))))
- `zz-act-neg-rev` — forall([g], is-abelian-group(g) implies forall([k in nn, a in carr(g)], (inv(g))(mpow(abelian-group-as-monoid(g), a, k)) = zz-act(g, -k, a)))
- `zz-act-neg-sign` — forall([g], is-abelian-group(g) implies forall([k in zz, a in carr(g)], zz-act(g, -k, a) = (inv(g))(zz-act(g, k, a))))
- `zz-act-neg-sign-rev` — forall([g], is-abelian-group(g) implies forall([k in zz, a in carr(g)], (inv(g))(zz-act(g, k, a)) = zz-act(g, -k, a)))
- `zz-act-nonneg` — forall([g], is-abelian-group(g) implies forall([k in nn, a in carr(g)], zz-act(g, k, a) = mpow(abelian-group-as-monoid(g), a, k)))
- `zz-act-nonneg-rev` — forall([g], is-abelian-group(g) implies forall([k in nn, a in carr(g)], mpow(abelian-group-as-monoid(g), a, k) = zz-act(g, k, a)))
- `zz-act-one` — forall([g], is-abelian-group(g) implies forall([a in carr(g)], zz-act(g, 1, a) = a))
- `zz-act-one-rev` — forall([g], is-abelian-group(g) implies forall([a in carr(g)], a = zz-act(g, 1, a)))
- `zz-act-type` — forall([g], is-abelian-group(g) implies forall([k in zz, a in carr(g)], zz-act(g, k, a) in carr(g)))
- `zz-act-zero` — forall([g], is-abelian-group(g) implies forall([a in carr(g)], zz-act(g, 0, a) = iden(g)))
- `zz-act-zero-rev` — forall([g], is-abelian-group(g) implies forall([a in carr(g)], iden(g) = zz-act(g, 0, a)))

*Views into `abelian-group`.*

- `ring-additive-ag` — from `ring`: (carr add zero neg) ↦ (carr opr iden inv)
- `commutative-ring-additive-ag` — from `commutative-ring`: (carr add zero neg) ↦ (carr opr iden inv)
- `normed-field-additive-ag` — from `normed-field`: (carr add zero neg) ↦ (carr opr iden inv)
- `module-vector-ag` — from `module`: (vec vadd vzero vneg) ↦ (carr opr iden inv)
- `field-additive-ag` — from `field`: (carr add zero neg) ↦ (carr opr iden inv)
- `normed-ag-as-abelian-group` — from `normed-ag`: (carr opr iden inv) ↦ (carr opr iden inv)

*Views from `abelian-group`.*

- `abelian-group-as-monoid` — into `monoid`: (carr opr iden) ↦ (carr opr iden)

### bundle
<a id="bundle"></a>

*Declared in* [`structure-library/strand-spaces.scm`](../structure-library/strand-spaces.scm).

*Kind.* Shape structure — `declare-structure` with slot clauses.

*Slots* (3): `bspace` substructure, a strand-space; `bnodes` carrier; `bsend` relation on bnodes.

*Default arrows* (`is-hom-bundle-def`): forall([a, b, f], is-hom-bundle(a, b, f) iff is-bundle(a) and is-bundle(b) and f in fun(bnodes(a), bnodes(b)) and bspace(a) = bspace(b) and forall([x1_ in bnodes(a), x2_ in bnodes(a)], [x1_, x2_] in bsend(a) implies [f(x1_), f(x2_)] in bsend(b)))

*Declaration.*

```scheme
(declare-structure bundle
  (instance-var s)
  (substructure bspace strand-space)
  (carriers bnodes)
  (relation bsend bnodes)
  (law (forall ssx_ (implies (in ssx_ (bnodes s)) (in ssx_ (nodes (bspace s))))))
  (law (in (card (bnodes s)) nn))
  (law (forall ssx_ (forall ssy_ (implies (in (list ssx_ ssy_) (bsend s)) (sends-to (bspace s) ssx_ ssy_)))))
  (law (forall ssy_ (implies (in ssy_ (bnodes s)) (implies (is-received (bspace s) ssy_) (forsome ssx_ (and (in (list ssx_ ssy_) (bsend s)) (forall ssz_ (implies (in (list ssz_ ssy_) (bsend s)) (= ssz_ ssx_)))))))))
  (law (forall ssy_ (implies (in ssy_ (bnodes s)) (forall ssx_ (implies (same-strand-before (bspace s) ssx_ ssy_) (in ssx_ (bnodes s)))))))
  (law (forall ssx_ (not (in (list ssx_ ssx_) (prec-strict (bspace s) (bnodes s) (bsend s)))))))
```

*Defining predicate* (as stored):

- `is-bundle` — forall([s], is-bundle(s) iff length(s) = 3 and is-strand-space(bspace(s)) and bnodes(s) in set and bsend(s) in power(cartesian(bnodes(s), bnodes(s))) and forall([ssx_ in bnodes(s)], ssx_ in nodes(bspace(s))) and card(bnodes(s)) in nn and forall([ssx_, ssy_], [ssx_, ssy_] in bsend(s) implies sends-to(bspace(s), ssx_, ssy_)) and forall([ssy_ in bnodes(s)], is-received(bspace(s), ssy_) implies forsome([ssx_], [ssx_, ssy_] in bsend(s) and forall([ssz_], [ssz_, ssy_] in bsend(s) implies ssz_ = ssx_))) and forall([ssy_ in bnodes(s), ssx_], same-strand-before(bspace(s), ssx_, ssy_) implies ssx_ in bnodes(s)) and forall([ssx_], not([ssx_, ssx_] in prec-strict(bspace(s), bnodes(s), bsend(s)))))

*Theorems quantifying over `is-bundle`.*

- `bundle-acyclic` — forall([c], is-bundle(c) implies forall([ssx_], not([ssx_, ssx_] in prec-strict(bspace(c), bnodes(c), bsend(c)))))
- `bundle-finite` — forall([c], is-bundle(c) implies card(bnodes(c)) in nn)
- `bundle-nodes-in-space` — forall([c], is-bundle(c) implies forall([ssx_ in bnodes(c)], ssx_ in nodes(bspace(c))))
- `bundle-nodes-set` — forall([c], is-bundle(c) implies bnodes(c) in set)
- `bundle-received-sender` — forall([c], is-bundle(c) implies forall([ssy_ in bnodes(c)], is-received(bspace(c), ssy_) implies forsome([ssx_], [ssx_, ssy_] in bsend(c) and forall([ssz_], [ssz_, ssy_] in bsend(c) implies ssz_ = ssx_))))
- `bundle-send-sends` — forall([c], is-bundle(c) implies forall([ssx_, ssy_], [ssx_, ssy_] in bsend(c) implies sends-to(bspace(c), ssx_, ssy_)))
- `bundle-send-typing` — forall([c], is-bundle(c) implies bsend(c) in power(cartesian(bnodes(c), bnodes(c))))
- `bundle-space` — forall([c], is-bundle(c) implies is-strand-space(bspace(c)))
- `bundle-ssb-closed` — forall([c], is-bundle(c) implies forall([ssy_ in bnodes(c), ssx_], same-strand-before(bspace(c), ssx_, ssy_) implies ssx_ in bnodes(c)))
- `precedes-partial-order` — forall([c], is-bundle(c) implies is-partial-order(bnodes(c), precedes(c)))
- `precedes-set` — forall([c], is-bundle(c) implies precedes(c) in set)

### c-metric-space
<a id="c-metric-space"></a>

*Declared in* [`structure-library/c-metric-space.scm`](../structure-library/c-metric-space.scm).

*Kind.* Shape structure — `declare-structure` with slot clauses.

*Slots* (2): `pts` carrier; `dists` operation nn -> fun(cartesian(pts, pts), rr).

*Default arrows* (`is-hom-c-metric-space-def`): forall([a, b, f], is-hom-c-metric-space(a, b, f) iff is-c-metric-space(a) and is-c-metric-space(b) and f in fun(pts(a), pts(b)) and forall([x1_ in nn], (dists(a))(x1_) = (dists(b))(x1_)))

*Declaration.*

```scheme
(declare-structure c-metric-space
  (instance-var s)
  (carriers pts)
  (op dists nn (fun (cartesian pts pts) rr))
  (law "forall([k_ in nn], is-pseudometric(dists(s)(k_), pts(s)))")
  (law "forall([u in pts(s), v in pts(s)],
          forall([k_ in nn], dists(s)(k_)(u, v) = 0) implies u = v)"))
```

*Defining predicate* (as stored):

### comm-monoid
<a id="comm-monoid"></a>

*Declared in* [`structure-library/monoid.scm`](../structure-library/monoid.scm).

*Kind.* Shape structure — `declare-structure` with slot clauses.

*Slots* (3): `carr` carrier; `opr` operation cartesian(carr, carr) -> carr; `iden` constant in carr.

*Default arrows* (`is-hom-comm-monoid-def`): forall([a, b, f], is-hom-comm-monoid(a, b, f) iff is-comm-monoid(a) and is-comm-monoid(b) and f in fun(carr(a), carr(b)) and forall([x1_ in carr(a), x2_ in carr(a)], f((opr(a))(x1_, x2_)) = (opr(b))(f(x1_), f(x2_))) and f(iden(a)) = iden(b))

*Declaration.*

```scheme
(declare-structure comm-monoid
  (carriers carr)
  (op opr (cartesian carr carr) carr)
  (constant iden carr)
  (property is-associative opr carr)
  (property is-identity opr iden carr)
  (property is-commutative opr carr))
```

*Defining predicate* (as stored):

- `is-comm-monoid` — forall([s], is-comm-monoid(s) iff length(s) = 3 and carr(s) in set and opr(s) in fun(cartesian(carr(s), carr(s)), carr(s)) and iden(s) in carr(s) and is-associative(opr(s), carr(s)) and is-identity(opr(s), iden(s), carr(s)) and is-commutative(opr(s), carr(s)))

*Theorems quantifying over `is-comm-monoid`.*

- `comm-monoid-assoc` — forall([s], is-comm-monoid(s) implies forall([a in carr(s), b in carr(s), c in carr(s)], (opr(s))((opr(s))(a, b), c) = (opr(s))(a, (opr(s))(b, c))))
- `comm-monoid-assoc-rev` — forall([s], is-comm-monoid(s) implies forall([a in carr(s), b in carr(s), c in carr(s)], (opr(s))(a, (opr(s))(b, c)) = (opr(s))((opr(s))(a, b), c)))
- `comm-monoid-carrier-closed-opr` — forall([s], is-comm-monoid(s) implies forall([a in carr(s), b in carr(s)], (opr(s))(a, b) in carr(s)))
- `comm-monoid-identity-in-carr` — forall([s], is-comm-monoid(s) implies iden(s) in carr(s))
- `comm-monoid-is-monoid` — forall([s], is-comm-monoid(s) implies is-monoid(s))
- `comm-monoid-left-id` — forall([s], is-comm-monoid(s) implies forall([a in carr(s)], (opr(s))(iden(s), a) = a))
- `comm-monoid-left-id-rev` — forall([s], is-comm-monoid(s) implies forall([a in carr(s)], a = (opr(s))(iden(s), a)))
- `comm-monoid-opr-comm` — forall([s], is-comm-monoid(s) implies forall([a in carr(s), b in carr(s)], (opr(s))(a, b) = (opr(s))(b, a)))
- `comm-monoid-opr-comm-rev` — forall([s], is-comm-monoid(s) implies forall([a in carr(s), b in carr(s)], (opr(s))(b, a) = (opr(s))(a, b)))
- `comm-monoid-opr-interchange` — forall([s], is-comm-monoid(s) implies forall([a in carr(s), b in carr(s), c in carr(s), d in carr(s)], (opr(s))((opr(s))(a, b), (opr(s))(c, d)) = (opr(s))((opr(s))(a, c), (opr(s))(b, d))))
- `comm-monoid-opr-interchange-rev` — forall([s], is-comm-monoid(s) implies forall([a in carr(s), b in carr(s), c in carr(s), d in carr(s)], (opr(s))((opr(s))(a, c), (opr(s))(b, d)) = (opr(s))((opr(s))(a, b), (opr(s))(c, d))))
- `finsum-add` — forall([m], is-comm-monoid(m) implies forall([s in set], card(s) in nn implies forall([f in fun(s, carr(m)), h in fun(s, carr(m))], finsum(m, vnb-lambda(z, s, (opr(m))(f(z), h(z))), s) = (opr(m))(finsum(m, f, s), finsum(m, h, s)))))
- `finsum-add-rev` — forall([m], is-comm-monoid(m) implies forall([s in set], card(s) in nn implies forall([f in fun(s, carr(m)), h in fun(s, carr(m))], (opr(m))(finsum(m, f, s), finsum(m, h, s)) = finsum(m, vnb-lambda(z, s, (opr(m))(f(z), h(z))), s))))
- `finsum-cm-add-ptwise` — forall([m], is-comm-monoid(m) implies forall([s in set], card(s) in nn implies forall([f, g, h], forall([z_ in s], f(z_) in carr(m)) implies forall([z_ in s], g(z_) in carr(m)) implies forall([z_ in s], h(z_) = (opr(m))(f(z_), g(z_))) implies finsum(m, h, s) = (opr(m))(finsum(m, f, s), finsum(m, g, s)))))
- `finsum-cm-add-ptwise-rev` — forall([m], is-comm-monoid(m) implies forall([s in set], card(s) in nn implies forall([f, g, h], forall([z_ in s], f(z_) in carr(m)) implies forall([z_ in s], g(z_) in carr(m)) implies forall([z_ in s], h(z_) = (opr(m))(f(z_), g(z_))) implies (opr(m))(finsum(m, f, s), finsum(m, g, s)) = finsum(m, h, s))))
- `finsum-cm-all-iden-ptwise` — forall([m], is-comm-monoid(m) implies forall([s in set], card(s) in nn implies forall([f], forall([z_ in s], f(z_) = iden(m)) implies finsum(m, f, s) = iden(m))))
- `finsum-cm-all-iden-ptwise-rev` — forall([m], is-comm-monoid(m) implies forall([s in set], card(s) in nn implies forall([f], forall([z_ in s], f(z_) = iden(m)) implies iden(m) = finsum(m, f, s))))
- `finsum-cm-congruence-q` — forall([m], is-comm-monoid(m) implies forall([s in set], card(s) in nn implies forall([f, g], forall([z in s], f(z) = g(z)) implies finsum(m, f, s) == finsum(m, g, s))))
- `finsum-cm-congruence-q-rev` — forall([m], is-comm-monoid(m) implies forall([s in set], card(s) in nn implies forall([f, g], forall([z in s], f(z) = g(z)) implies finsum(m, g, s) == finsum(m, f, s))))
- `finsum-cm-fiber-ptwise` — forall([m], is-comm-monoid(m) implies forall([s in set], card(s) in nn implies forall([t in set], card(t) in nn implies forall([phi, f], forall([z_ in s], phi(z_) in t) implies forall([z_ in s], f(z_) in carr(m)) implies forall([gq], forall([z_ in t], gq(z_) == finsum(m, f, {fw_ in s: phi(fw_) = z_})) implies finsum(m, f, s) = finsum(m, gq, t))))))

- `finsum-cm-insert-ptwise` — forall([m], is-comm-monoid(m) implies forall([s_ in set], card(s_) in nn implies forall([k_ in set], not(k_ in s_) implies forall([f_], forall([z_ in union(s_, pair(k_, k_))], f_(z_) in carr(m)) implies finsum(m, f_, union(s_, pair(k_, k_))) = (opr(m))(finsum(m, f_, s_), f_(k_))))))

### commutative-ring
<a id="commutative-ring"></a>

*Declared in* [`structure-library/commutative-ring.scm`](../structure-library/commutative-ring.scm).

*Kind.* Refinement — `declare-structure` with `(same-shape-as ring)`; its `IS-X <=> IS-PARENT and <laws>` axiom is generated, not hand-written.  Subtype of [`ring`](#ring).  Shape inherited from `ring` — slots (carr add mul neg zero one).

*Declaration.*

```scheme
(declare-structure commutative-ring
  (instance-var s)
  (same-shape-as ring)
  (law "forall([a in carr(s), b in carr(s)], mul(s)(a, b) = mul(s)(b, a))"))
```

*Defining predicate* (as stored):

- `is-commutative-ring-def` — forall([s], is-commutative-ring(s) iff is-ring(s) and forall([a in carr(s), b in carr(s)], (mul(s))(a, b) = (mul(s))(b, a)))

### complex-inner-product-space
<a id="complex-inner-product-space"></a>

*Declared in* [`structure-library/complex-inner-product.scm`](../structure-library/complex-inner-product.scm).

*Kind.* Shape structure — `declare-structure` with slot clauses.

*Slots* (7): `scal` substructure, a ring; `vec` carrier; `vadd` operation cartesian(vec, vec) -> vec; `vzero` constant in vec; `vneg` operation vec -> vec; `act` operation cartesian(carr(scal), vec) -> vec; `ip` operation cartesian(vec, vec) -> cc.

*Default arrows* (`is-hom-complex-inner-product-space-def`): forall([a, b, f], is-hom-complex-inner-product-space(a, b, f) iff is-complex-inner-product-space(a) and is-complex-inner-product-space(b) and f in fun(vec(a), vec(b)) and scal(a) = scal(b) and forall([x1_ in vec(a), x2_ in vec(a)], f((vadd(a))(x1_, x2_)) = (vadd(b))(f(x1_), f(x2_))) and f(vzero(a)) = vzero(b) and forall([x1_ in vec(a)], f((vneg(a))(x1_)) = (vneg(b))(f(x1_))) and forall([x1_ in carr(scal(a)), x2_ in vec(a)], f((act(a))(x1_, x2_)) = (act(b))(x1_, f(x2_))) and forall([x1_ in vec(a), x2_ in vec(a)], (ip(a))(x1_, x2_) = (ip(b))(f(x1_), f(x2_))))

*Declaration.*

```scheme
(declare-structure complex-inner-product-space
  (instance-var s)
  (substructure scal ring)
  (carriers vec)
  (op vadd (cartesian vec vec) vec)
  (constant vzero vec)
  (op vneg vec vec)
  (op act (cartesian (carr scal) vec) vec)
  (op ip (cartesian vec vec) cc)
  (property is-associative vadd vec)
  (property is-commutative vadd vec)
  (property is-identity vadd vzero vec)
  (property has-inverses vadd vzero vneg vec)
  (law "scal(s) = normed-field-as-commutative-ring(cc-normed-field)")
  (law "carr(scal(s)) = cc")
  (law "forall([r_ in carr(scal(s)), x_ in vec(s), y_ in vec(s)],
          act(s)(r_, vadd(s)(x_, y_)) = vadd(s)(act(s)(r_, x_), act(s)(r_, y_)))")
  (law "forall([r_ in carr(scal(s)), s_ in carr(scal(s)), x_ in vec(s)],
          act(s)(add(scal(s))(r_, s_), x_) = vadd(s)(act(s)(r_, x_), act(s)(s_, x_)))")
  (law "forall([r_ in carr(scal(s)), s_ in carr(scal(s)), x_ in vec(s)],
          act(s)(mul(scal(s))(r_, s_), x_) = act(s)(r_, act(s)(s_, x_)))")
  (law "forall([x_ in vec(s)], act(s)(one(scal(s)), x_) = x_)")
  (law "forall([x_ in vec(s), y_ in vec(s), z_ in vec(s)],
          ip(s)(vadd(s)(x_, y_), z_) = ip(s)(x_, z_) + ip(s)(y_, z_))")
  (law "forall([a_ in cc, x_ in vec(s), y_ in vec(s)],
          ip(s)(act(s)(a_, x_), y_) = a_ * ip(s)(x_, y_))")
  (law "forall([x_ in vec(s), y_ in vec(s)],
          ip(s)(y_, x_) = conjugate(ip(s)(x_, y_)))")
  (law "forall([x_ in vec(s)], ip(s)(x_, x_) in rr)")
  (law "forall([x_ in vec(s)], 0 <= ip(s)(x_, x_))")
  (law "forall([x_ in vec(s)], (ip(s)(x_, x_) = 0) iff (x_ = vzero(s)))"))
```

*Defining predicate* (as stored):

- `is-complex-inner-product-space` — forall([s], is-complex-inner-product-space(s) iff length(s) = 7 and is-ring(scal(s)) and vec(s) in set and vadd(s) in fun(cartesian(vec(s), vec(s)), vec(s)) and vzero(s) in vec(s) and vneg(s) in fun(vec(s), vec(s)) and act(s) in fun(cartesian(carr(scal(s)), vec(s)), vec(s)) and ip(s) in fun(cartesian(vec(s), vec(s)), cc) and is-associative(vadd(s), vec(s)) and is-commutative(vadd(s), vec(s)) and is-identity(vadd(s), vzero(s), vec(s)) and has-inverses(vadd(s), vzero(s), vneg(s), vec(s)) and scal(s) = normed-field-as-commutative-ring(cc-normed-field) and carr(scal(s)) = cc and forall([r_ in carr(scal(s)), x_ in vec(s), y_ in vec(s)], (act(s))(r_, (vadd(s))(x_, y_)) = (vadd(s))((act(s))(r_, x_), (act(s))(r_, y_))) and forall([r_ in carr(scal(s)), s_ in carr(scal(s)), x_ in vec(s)], (act(s))((add(scal(s)))(r_, s_), x_) = (vadd(s))((act(s))(r_, x_), (act(s))(s_, x_))) and forall([r_ in carr(scal(s)), s_ in carr(scal(s)), x_ in vec(s)], (act(s))((mul(scal(s)))(r_, s_), x_) = (act(s))(r_, (act(s))(s_, x_))) and forall([x_ in vec(s)], (act(s))(one(scal(s)), x_) = x_) and forall([x_ in vec(s), y_ in vec(s), z_ in vec(s)], (ip(s))((vadd(s))(x_, y_), z_) = (ip(s))(x_, z_) + (ip(s))(y_, z_)) and forall([a_ in cc, x_ in vec(s), y_ in vec(s)], (ip(s))((act(s))(a_, x_), y_) = a_ * (ip(s))(x_, y_)) and forall([x_ in vec(s), y_ in vec(s)], (ip(s))(y_, x_) = conjugate((ip(s))(x_, y_))) and forall([x_ in vec(s)], (ip(s))(x_, x_) in rr) and forall([x_ in vec(s)], 0 <= (ip(s))(x_, x_)) and forall([x_ in vec(s)], (ip(s))(x_, x_) = 0 iff x_ = vzero(s)))

### complex-semi-inner-product-space
<a id="complex-semi-inner-product-space"></a>

*Declared in* [`structure-library/sip-complex-vocab.scm`](../structure-library/sip-complex-vocab.scm).

*Kind.* Shape structure — `declare-structure` with slot clauses.

*Slots* (8): `scal` substructure, a ring; `vec` carrier; `vadd` operation cartesian(vec, vec) -> vec; `vzero` constant in vec; `vneg` operation vec -> vec; `act` operation cartesian(carr(scal), vec) -> vec; `rip` operation cartesian(vec, vec) -> rr; `jop` operation vec -> vec.

*Default arrows* (`is-hom-complex-semi-inner-product-space-def`): forall([a, b, f], is-hom-complex-semi-inner-product-space(a, b, f) iff is-complex-semi-inner-product-space(a) and is-complex-semi-inner-product-space(b) and f in fun(vec(a), vec(b)) and scal(a) = scal(b) and forall([x1_ in vec(a), x2_ in vec(a)], f((vadd(a))(x1_, x2_)) = (vadd(b))(f(x1_), f(x2_))) and f(vzero(a)) = vzero(b) and forall([x1_ in vec(a)], f((vneg(a))(x1_)) = (vneg(b))(f(x1_))) and forall([x1_ in carr(scal(a)), x2_ in vec(a)], f((act(a))(x1_, x2_)) = (act(b))(x1_, f(x2_))) and forall([x1_ in vec(a), x2_ in vec(a)], (rip(a))(x1_, x2_) = (rip(b))(f(x1_), f(x2_))) and forall([x1_ in vec(a)], f((jop(a))(x1_)) = (jop(b))(f(x1_))))

*Declaration.*

```scheme
(declare-structure complex-semi-inner-product-space
  (instance-var s)
  (substructure scal ring)
  (carriers vec)
  (op vadd (cartesian vec vec) vec)
  (constant vzero vec)
  (op vneg vec vec)
  (op act (cartesian (carr scal) vec) vec)
  (op rip (cartesian vec vec) rr)
  (op jop vec vec)
  (law "is-semi-inner-product-space([scal(s), vec(s), vadd(s), vzero(s), vneg(s), act(s), rip(s)])")
  (law "is-real-linear-op(s, jop(s))")
  (law "is-square-minus-id(s, jop(s))")
  (law "is-antisymmetric-op(s, jop(s))"))
```

*Defining predicate* (as stored):

- `is-complex-semi-inner-product-space` — forall([s], is-complex-semi-inner-product-space(s) iff length(s) = 8 and is-ring(scal(s)) and vec(s) in set and vadd(s) in fun(cartesian(vec(s), vec(s)), vec(s)) and vzero(s) in vec(s) and vneg(s) in fun(vec(s), vec(s)) and act(s) in fun(cartesian(carr(scal(s)), vec(s)), vec(s)) and rip(s) in fun(cartesian(vec(s), vec(s)), rr) and jop(s) in fun(vec(s), vec(s)) and is-semi-inner-product-space([scal(s), vec(s), vadd(s), vzero(s), vneg(s), act(s), rip(s)]) and is-real-linear-op(s, jop(s)) and is-square-minus-id(s, jop(s)) and is-antisymmetric-op(s, jop(s)))

- `abelian-group-assoc-module-vector-ag-rev-sip-as-module-csip-as-sip` — forall([r], is-complex-semi-inner-product-space(r) implies forall([a in vec(r), b in vec(r), c in vec(r)], (vadd(r))(a, (vadd(r))(b, c)) = (vadd(r))((vadd(r))(a, b), c)))

- `ses-add-left` — forall([v], is-complex-semi-inner-product-space(v) implies forall([sqf], is-sesquilinear-form(v, sqf) implies forall([x_ in vec(v), y_ in vec(v), z_ in vec(v)], sqf((vadd(v))(x_, y_), z_) = sqf(x_, z_) + sqf(y_, z_))))
- `ses-add-left-rev` — forall([v], is-complex-semi-inner-product-space(v) implies forall([sqf], is-sesquilinear-form(v, sqf) implies forall([x_ in vec(v), y_ in vec(v), z_ in vec(v)], sqf(x_, z_) + sqf(y_, z_) = sqf((vadd(v))(x_, y_), z_))))
- `ses-add-right` — forall([v], is-complex-semi-inner-product-space(v) implies forall([sqf], is-sesquilinear-form(v, sqf) implies forall([sx_ in vec(v), sy_ in vec(v), sz_ in vec(v)], sqf(sx_, (vadd(v))(sy_, sz_)) = sqf(sx_, sy_) + sqf(sx_, sz_))))
- `ses-add-right-rev` — forall([v], is-complex-semi-inner-product-space(v) implies forall([sqf], is-sesquilinear-form(v, sqf) implies forall([sx_ in vec(v), sy_ in vec(v), sz_ in vec(v)], sqf(sx_, sy_) + sqf(sx_, sz_) = sqf(sx_, (vadd(v))(sy_, sz_)))))
- `ses-conj-sym` — forall([v], is-complex-semi-inner-product-space(v) implies forall([sqf], is-sesquilinear-form(v, sqf) implies forall([x_ in vec(v), y_ in vec(v)], sqf(y_, x_) = conjugate(sqf(x_, y_)))))
- `ses-conj-sym-rev` — forall([v], is-complex-semi-inner-product-space(v) implies forall([sqf], is-sesquilinear-form(v, sqf) implies forall([x_ in vec(v), y_ in vec(v)], conjugate(sqf(x_, y_)) = sqf(y_, x_))))
- `ses-expand` — forall([v], is-complex-semi-inner-product-space(v) implies forall([sqf], is-sesquilinear-form(v, sqf) implies forall([sc_ in cc, sx_ in vec(v), sy_ in vec(v)], sqf((vadd(v))(sx_, (cact(v))(sc_, sy_)), (vadd(v))(sx_, (cact(v))(sc_, sy_))) = ((sqf(sx_, sx_) + conjugate(sc_) * sqf(sx_, sy_)) + sc_ * sqf(sy_, sx_)) + (sc_ * conjugate(sc_)) * sqf(sy_, sy_))))
- `ses-expand-rev` — forall([v], is-complex-semi-inner-product-space(v) implies forall([sqf], is-sesquilinear-form(v, sqf) implies forall([sc_ in cc, sx_ in vec(v), sy_ in vec(v)], ((sqf(sx_, sx_) + conjugate(sc_) * sqf(sx_, sy_)) + sc_ * sqf(sy_, sx_)) + (sc_ * conjugate(sc_)) * sqf(sy_, sy_) = sqf((vadd(v))(sx_, (cact(v))(sc_, sy_)), (vadd(v))(sx_, (cact(v))(sc_, sy_))))))
- `ses-homog-left` — forall([v], is-complex-semi-inner-product-space(v) implies forall([sqf], is-sesquilinear-form(v, sqf) implies forall([a_ in cc, x_ in vec(v), y_ in vec(v)], sqf((cact(v))(a_, x_), y_) = a_ * sqf(x_, y_))))
- `ses-homog-left-rev` — forall([v], is-complex-semi-inner-product-space(v) implies forall([sqf], is-sesquilinear-form(v, sqf) implies forall([a_ in cc, x_ in vec(v), y_ in vec(v)], a_ * sqf(x_, y_) = sqf((cact(v))(a_, x_), y_))))
- `ses-homog-right` — forall([v], is-complex-semi-inner-product-space(v) implies forall([sqf], is-sesquilinear-form(v, sqf) implies forall([sk_ in cc, sx_ in vec(v), sy_ in vec(v)], sqf(sx_, (cact(v))(sk_, sy_)) = conjugate(sk_) * sqf(sx_, sy_))))
- `ses-homog-right-rev` — forall([v], is-complex-semi-inner-product-space(v) implies forall([sqf], is-sesquilinear-form(v, sqf) implies forall([sk_ in cc, sx_ in vec(v), sy_ in vec(v)], conjugate(sk_) * sqf(sx_, sy_) = sqf(sx_, (cact(v))(sk_, sy_)))))
- `ses-polarization` — forall([v], is-complex-semi-inner-product-space(v) implies forall([sqf], is-sesquilinear-form(v, sqf) implies forall([sx_ in vec(v), sy_ in vec(v)], 4 * sqf(sx_, sy_) = sqf((vadd(v))(sx_, sy_), (vadd(v))(sx_, sy_)) - sqf((vadd(v))(sx_, (vneg(v))(sy_)), (vadd(v))(sx_, (vneg(v))(sy_))) + 1i * sqf((vadd(v))(sx_, (cact(v))(1i, sy_)), (vadd(v))(sx_, (cact(v))(1i, sy_))) - 1i * sqf((vadd(v))(sx_, (vneg(v))((cact(v))(1i, sy_))), (vadd(v))(sx_, (vneg(v))((cact(v))(1i, sy_)))))))
- `ses-polarization-rev` — forall([v], is-complex-semi-inner-product-space(v) implies forall([sqf], is-sesquilinear-form(v, sqf) implies forall([sx_ in vec(v), sy_ in vec(v)], sqf((vadd(v))(sx_, sy_), (vadd(v))(sx_, sy_)) - sqf((vadd(v))(sx_, (vneg(v))(sy_)), (vadd(v))(sx_, (vneg(v))(sy_))) + 1i * sqf((vadd(v))(sx_, (cact(v))(1i, sy_)), (vadd(v))(sx_, (cact(v))(1i, sy_))) - 1i * sqf((vadd(v))(sx_, (vneg(v))((cact(v))(1i, sy_))), (vadd(v))(sx_, (vneg(v))((cact(v))(1i, sy_)))) = 4 * sqf(sx_, sy_))))
- `ses-type` — forall([v], is-complex-semi-inner-product-space(v) implies forall([sqf], is-sesquilinear-form(v, sqf) implies forall([sx_ in vec(v), sy_ in vec(v)], sqf(sx_, sy_) in cc)))

### euclidean-ring
<a id="euclidean-ring"></a>

*Declared in* [`structure-library/euclidean-ring.scm`](../structure-library/euclidean-ring.scm).

*Kind.* Refinement — `declare-structure` with `(same-shape-as integral-domain)`; its `IS-X <=> IS-PARENT and <laws>` axiom is generated, not hand-written.  Subtype of [`integral-domain`](#integral-domain).  Shape inherited from `integral-domain` — slots (carr add mul neg zero one).

*Declaration.*

```scheme
(declare-structure euclidean-ring
  (instance-var s)
  (same-shape-as integral-domain)
  (law "forsome([dg in fun(carr(s), nn)],
          forall([a in carr(s), b in carr(s)],
            not(b = zero(s)) implies
              forsome([q in carr(s), r in carr(s)],
                a = add(s)(mul(s)(q, b), r)
                and (r = zero(s) or succ(dg(r)) <= dg(b)))))"))
```

*Defining predicate* (as stored):

- `is-euclidean-ring-def` — forall([s], is-euclidean-ring(s) iff is-integral-domain(s) and forsome([dg in fun(carr(s), nn)], forall([a in carr(s), b in carr(s)], not(b = zero(s)) implies forsome([q in carr(s), r in carr(s)], a = (add(s))((mul(s))(q, b), r) and (r = zero(s) or succ(dg(r)) <= dg(b))))))

*Theorems quantifying over `is-euclidean-ring`.*

- `field-as-euclidean-ring` — from `field`

### field
<a id="field"></a>

*Declared in* [`structure-library/field.scm`](../structure-library/field.scm).

*Kind.* Shape structure — `declare-structure` with slot clauses.

*Slots* (8): `carr` carrier; `add` operation cartesian(carr, carr) -> carr; `mul` operation cartesian(carr, carr) -> carr; `neg` operation carr -> carr; `zero` constant in carr; `one` constant in carr; `non-zero` derived carrier, carved out of carr; `mul-inv` operation non-zero -> non-zero.

*Default arrows* (`is-hom-field-def`): forall([a, b, f], is-hom-field(a, b, f) iff is-field(a) and is-field(b) and f in fun(carr(a), carr(b)) and forall([x1_ in carr(a), x2_ in carr(a)], f((add(a))(x1_, x2_)) = (add(b))(f(x1_), f(x2_))) and forall([x1_ in carr(a), x2_ in carr(a)], f((mul(a))(x1_, x2_)) = (mul(b))(f(x1_), f(x2_))) and forall([x1_ in carr(a)], f((neg(a))(x1_)) = (neg(b))(f(x1_))) and f(zero(a)) = zero(b) and f(one(a)) = one(b) and forall([x1_ in non-zero(a)], f((mul-inv(a))(x1_)) = (mul-inv(b))(f(x1_))))

*Declaration.*

```scheme
(declare-structure field
  (instance-var s)
  (carriers carr)
  (op add (cartesian carr carr) carr)
  (op mul (cartesian carr carr) carr)
  (op neg carr carr)
  (constant zero carr)
  (constant one carr)
  (derived non-zero carr (difference carr (singleton zero)))
  (op mul-inv non-zero non-zero)
  (property is-associative add carr)
  (property is-commutative add carr)
  (property is-identity add zero carr)
  (property has-inverses add zero neg carr)
  (property is-associative mul carr)
  (property is-commutative mul carr)
  (property is-identity mul one carr)
  (property is-distributive add mul carr)
  (law "not(zero(s) = one(s))")
  (law "forall([a_ in non-zero(s)], mul(s)(a_, mul-inv(s)(a_)) = one(s))"))
```

*Defining predicate* (as stored):

- `is-field` — forall([s], is-field(s) iff length(s) = 8 and carr(s) in set and add(s) in fun(cartesian(carr(s), carr(s)), carr(s)) and mul(s) in fun(cartesian(carr(s), carr(s)), carr(s)) and neg(s) in fun(carr(s), carr(s)) and zero(s) in carr(s) and one(s) in carr(s) and non-zero(s) in set and non-zero(s) = difference(carr(s), singleton(zero(s))) and mul-inv(s) in fun(non-zero(s), non-zero(s)) and is-associative(add(s), carr(s)) and is-commutative(add(s), carr(s)) and is-identity(add(s), zero(s), carr(s)) and has-inverses(add(s), zero(s), neg(s), carr(s)) and is-associative(mul(s), carr(s)) and is-commutative(mul(s), carr(s)) and is-identity(mul(s), one(s), carr(s)) and is-distributive(add(s), mul(s), carr(s)) and not(zero(s) = one(s)) and forall([a_ in non-zero(s)], (mul(s))(a_, (mul-inv(s))(a_)) = one(s)))

*Theorems quantifying over `is-field`.*

- `abelian-group-as-monoid-is-monoid-field-additive-ag` — forall([r], is-field(r) implies is-monoid(abelian-group-as-monoid(field-additive-ag(r))))
- `field-additive-ag-is-abelian-group` — forall([r], is-field(r) implies is-abelian-group(field-additive-ag(r)))
- `field-as-euclidean-ring-is-euclidean-ring` — forall([r], is-field(r) implies is-euclidean-ring(field-as-euclidean-ring(r)))
- `field-as-integral-domain-is-integral-domain` — forall([r], is-field(r) implies is-integral-domain(field-as-integral-domain(r)))
- `field-id-add` — forall([f_], is-field(f_) implies add(field-as-integral-domain(f_)) == add(f_))
- `field-id-add-rev` — forall([f_], is-field(f_) implies add(f_) == add(field-as-integral-domain(f_)))
- `field-id-carr` — forall([f_], is-field(f_) implies carr(field-as-integral-domain(f_)) == carr(f_))
- `field-id-carr-rev` — forall([f_], is-field(f_) implies carr(f_) == carr(field-as-integral-domain(f_)))
- `field-id-mul` — forall([f_], is-field(f_) implies mul(field-as-integral-domain(f_)) == mul(f_))
- `field-id-mul-rev` — forall([f_], is-field(f_) implies mul(f_) == mul(field-as-integral-domain(f_)))
- `field-id-neg` — forall([f_], is-field(f_) implies neg(field-as-integral-domain(f_)) == neg(f_))
- `field-id-neg-rev` — forall([f_], is-field(f_) implies neg(f_) == neg(field-as-integral-domain(f_)))
- `field-id-one` — forall([f_], is-field(f_) implies one(field-as-integral-domain(f_)) == one(f_))
- `field-id-one-rev` — forall([f_], is-field(f_) implies one(f_) == one(field-as-integral-domain(f_)))
- `field-id-zero` — forall([f_], is-field(f_) implies zero(field-as-integral-domain(f_)) == zero(f_))
- `field-id-zero-rev` — forall([f_], is-field(f_) implies zero(f_) == zero(field-as-integral-domain(f_)))
- `field-is-field-ring` — forall([s_], is-field(s_) implies is-field-ring(field-as-integral-domain(s_)))
- `field-multiplicative-group-is-group` — forall([r], is-field(r) implies is-group(field-multiplicative-group(r)))
- `field-non-zero-carrier` — forall([s], is-field(s) implies non-zero(s) = difference(carr(s), singleton(zero(s))))
- `field-non-zero-carrier-rev` — forall([s], is-field(s) implies difference(carr(s), singleton(zero(s))) = non-zero(s))
- `hom-field-id` — forall([a], is-field(a) implies is-hom-field(a, a, id-fun(carr(a))))

*Views from `field`.*

- `field-as-euclidean-ring` — into `euclidean-ring`: (carr add mul neg zero one) ↦ (carr add mul neg zero one)
- `field-as-integral-domain` — into `integral-domain`: (carr add mul neg zero one) ↦ (carr add mul neg zero one)
- `field-multiplicative-group` — into `group`: (non-zero mul one mul-inv) ↦ (carr opr iden inv)
- `field-additive-ag` — into `abelian-group`: (carr add zero neg) ↦ (carr opr iden inv)

### field-ring
<a id="field-ring"></a>

*Declared in* [`structure-library/field.scm`](../structure-library/field.scm).

*Kind.* Refinement — `declare-structure` with `(same-shape-as commutative-ring)`; its `IS-X <=> IS-PARENT and <laws>` axiom is generated, not hand-written.  Subtype of [`commutative-ring`](#commutative-ring).  Shape inherited from `commutative-ring` — slots (carr add mul neg zero one).

*Declaration.*

```scheme
(declare-structure field-ring
  (instance-var s)
  (same-shape-as commutative-ring)
  (law "not(one(s) = zero(s))")
  (law "forall([a_ in carr(s)],
          not(a_ = zero(s)) implies
            forsome([b_ in carr(s)], mul(s)(a_, b_) = one(s)))"))
```

*Defining predicate* (as stored):

- `is-field-ring-def` — forall([s], is-field-ring(s) iff is-commutative-ring(s) and not(one(s) = zero(s)) and forall([a_ in carr(s)], not(a_ = zero(s)) implies forsome([b_ in carr(s)], (mul(s))(a_, b_) = one(s))))

*Theorems quantifying over `is-field-ring`.*

- `hom-field-ring-id` — forall([a], is-field-ring(a) implies is-hom-field-ring(a, a, id-fun(carr(a))))
- `ladet-field-inverse` — forall([r], is-field-ring(r) implies forall([lfx_ in carr(r)], not(lfx_ = zero(r)) implies forsome([lfb_ in carr(r)], (mul(r))(lfx_, lfb_) = one(r))))
- `ladet-field-one-ne` — forall([r], is-field-ring(r) implies not(one(r) = zero(r)))

### group
<a id="group"></a>

*Declared in* [`structure-library/group.scm`](../structure-library/group.scm).

*Kind.* Shape structure — `declare-structure` with slot clauses.

*Slots* (4): `carr` carrier; `opr` operation cartesian(carr, carr) -> carr; `iden` constant in carr; `inv` operation carr -> carr.

*Default arrows* (`is-hom-group-def`): forall([a, b, f], is-hom-group(a, b, f) iff is-group(a) and is-group(b) and f in fun(carr(a), carr(b)) and forall([x1_ in carr(a), x2_ in carr(a)], f((opr(a))(x1_, x2_)) = (opr(b))(f(x1_), f(x2_))) and f(iden(a)) = iden(b) and forall([x1_ in carr(a)], f((inv(a))(x1_)) = (inv(b))(f(x1_))))

*Declaration.*

```scheme
(declare-structure group
  (carriers carr)
  (op opr (cartesian carr carr) carr)
  (constant iden carr)
  (op inv carr carr)
  (property is-associative opr carr)
  (property is-identity opr iden carr)
  (property has-inverses opr iden inv carr))
```

*Defining predicate* (as stored):

- `is-group` — forall([s], is-group(s) iff length(s) = 4 and carr(s) in set and opr(s) in fun(cartesian(carr(s), carr(s)), carr(s)) and iden(s) in carr(s) and inv(s) in fun(carr(s), carr(s)) and is-associative(opr(s), carr(s)) and is-identity(opr(s), iden(s), carr(s)) and has-inverses(opr(s), iden(s), inv(s), carr(s)))

*Theorems quantifying over `is-group`.*

- `group-assoc` — forall([s], is-group(s) implies forall([a in carr(s), b in carr(s), c in carr(s)], (opr(s))((opr(s))(a, b), c) = (opr(s))(a, (opr(s))(b, c))))
- `group-assoc-rev` — forall([s], is-group(s) implies forall([a in carr(s), b in carr(s), c in carr(s)], (opr(s))(a, (opr(s))(b, c)) = (opr(s))((opr(s))(a, b), c)))
- `group-cancel-left` — forall([s], is-group(s) implies forall([a in carr(s), b in carr(s), c in carr(s)], (opr(s))(c, a) = (opr(s))(c, b) implies a = b))
- `group-cancel-left-rev` — forall([s], is-group(s) implies forall([a in carr(s), b in carr(s), c in carr(s)], (opr(s))(c, a) = (opr(s))(c, b) implies b = a))
- `group-carrier-closed-opr` — forall([s], is-group(s) implies forall([a in carr(s), b in carr(s)], (opr(s))(a, b) in carr(s)))
- `group-identity-in` — forall([s], is-group(s) implies iden(s) in carr(s))
- `group-inv-in` — forall([s], is-group(s) implies forall([a in carr(s)], (inv(s))(a) in carr(s)))
- `group-left-id` — forall([s], is-group(s) implies forall([a in carr(s)], (opr(s))(iden(s), a) = a))
- `group-left-id-rev` — forall([s], is-group(s) implies forall([a in carr(s)], a = (opr(s))(iden(s), a)))
- `group-left-inv` — forall([s], is-group(s) implies forall([a in carr(s)], (opr(s))((inv(s))(a), a) = iden(s)))
- `group-left-inv-rev` — forall([s], is-group(s) implies forall([a in carr(s)], iden(s) = (opr(s))((inv(s))(a), a)))
- `hom-group-id` — forall([a], is-group(a) implies is-hom-group(a, a, id-fun(carr(a))))

*Views into `group`.*

- `field-multiplicative-group` — from `field`: (non-zero mul one mul-inv) ↦ (carr opr iden inv)

### infiltrated-space
<a id="infiltrated-space"></a>

*Declared in* [`structure-library/strand-adversary.scm`](../structure-library/strand-adversary.scm).

*Kind.* Shape structure — `declare-structure` with slot clauses.

*Slots* (3): `ispace` substructure, a strand-space; `adv-ids` carrier; `adv-tokens` carrier.

*Default arrows* (`is-hom-infiltrated-space-def`): forall([a, b, f1, f2], is-hom-infiltrated-space(a, b, f1, f2) iff is-infiltrated-space(a) and is-infiltrated-space(b) and f1 in fun(adv-ids(a), adv-ids(b)) and f2 in fun(adv-tokens(a), adv-tokens(b)) and ispace(a) = ispace(b))

*Declaration.*

```scheme
(declare-structure infiltrated-space
  (instance-var s)
  (substructure ispace strand-space)
  (carriers adv-ids adv-tokens)
  (law (forall sap_ (implies (in sap_ (adv-ids s)) (in sap_ (ids (ispace s))))))
  (law (forall sat_ (implies (in sat_ (adv-tokens s)) (in sat_ (tokens (ispace s))))))
  (law (forall sap_ (implies (in sap_ (adv-ids s)) (is-adversary-trace (ispace s) (adv-tokens s) ((strand-map (ispace s)) sap_))))))
```

*Defining predicate* (as stored):

- `is-infiltrated-space` — forall([s], is-infiltrated-space(s) iff length(s) = 3 and is-strand-space(ispace(s)) and adv-ids(s) in set and adv-tokens(s) in set and forall([sap_ in adv-ids(s)], sap_ in ids(ispace(s))) and forall([sat_ in adv-tokens(s)], sat_ in tokens(ispace(s))) and forall([sap_ in adv-ids(s)], is-adversary-trace(ispace(s), adv-tokens(s), (strand-map(ispace(s)))(sap_))))

### integral-domain
<a id="integral-domain"></a>

*Declared in* [`structure-library/integral-domain.scm`](../structure-library/integral-domain.scm).

*Kind.* Refinement — `declare-structure` with `(same-shape-as commutative-ring)`; its `IS-X <=> IS-PARENT and <laws>` axiom is generated, not hand-written.  Subtype of [`commutative-ring`](#commutative-ring).  Shape inherited from `commutative-ring` — slots (carr add mul neg zero one).

*Declaration.*

```scheme
(declare-structure integral-domain
  (instance-var s)
  (same-shape-as commutative-ring)
  (law "not(one(s) = zero(s))")
  (law "forall([a in carr(s), b in carr(s)],
          mul(s)(a, b) = zero(s) implies (a = zero(s) or b = zero(s)))"))
```

*Defining predicate* (as stored):

- `is-integral-domain-def` — forall([s], is-integral-domain(s) iff is-commutative-ring(s) and not(one(s) = zero(s)) and forall([a in carr(s), b in carr(s)], (mul(s))(a, b) = zero(s) implies a = zero(s) or b = zero(s)))

*Theorems quantifying over `is-integral-domain`.*

- `hom-integral-domain-id` — forall([a], is-integral-domain(a) implies is-hom-integral-domain(a, a, id-fun(carr(a))))
- `integral-domain-cancel-zero` — forall([s], is-integral-domain(s) implies forall([a in carr(s), b in carr(s)], (mul(s))(a, b) = zero(s) implies not(b = zero(s)) implies a = zero(s)))
- `integral-domain-cancel-zero-rev` — forall([s], is-integral-domain(s) implies forall([a in carr(s), b in carr(s)], (mul(s))(a, b) = zero(s) implies not(b = zero(s)) implies zero(s) = a))
- `integral-domain-is-commutative-ring` — forall([s], is-integral-domain(s) implies is-commutative-ring(s))
- `integral-domain-nontrivial` — forall([s], is-integral-domain(s) implies not(one(s) = zero(s)))

*Views into `integral-domain`.*

- `field-as-integral-domain` — from `field`
- `normed-field-as-integral-domain` — from `normed-field`

### lft-group
<a id="lft-group"></a>

*Declared in* [`structure-library/ca-lft-vocab.scm`](../structure-library/ca-lft-vocab.scm).

*Kind.* Instance — a constant tuple (an *element* of the class, not a predicate).  Subtype of [`group`](#group).

*Member of:*

- `is-group` (via `lft-group-is-group`)

*Components* (mapped to inherited `group` slots):

- `carr` = `lfts`
- `opr` = `vnb-lambda([lfg_, lfh_], cartesian(lfts, lfts), compose(lfg_, lfh_))`
- `iden` = `id-fun(cc-hat)`
- `inv` = `vnb-lambda(lfg_, lfts, inverse-bij(lfg_, cc-hat, cc-hat))`

### measurable-space
<a id="measurable-space"></a>

*Declared in* [`structure-library/measurable-space.scm`](../structure-library/measurable-space.scm).

*Kind.* Shape structure — `declare-structure` with slot clauses.

*Slots* (2): `pts` carrier; `sigma` family of subsets of pts.

*Default arrows* (`is-hom-measurable-space-def`): forall([s, t, f], is-hom-measurable-space(s, t, f) iff is-measurable-space(s) and is-measurable-space(t) and f in fun(pts(s), pts(t)) and forall([u in sigma(t)], preimage(s, f, u) in sigma(s)))

*Declaration.*

```scheme
(declare-structure measurable-space
  (instance-var s)
  (carriers pts)
  (family sigma pts)
  (law (is-sigma-algebra (pts s) (sigma s))))
```

*Defining predicate* (as stored):

- `is-measurable-space` — forall([s], is-measurable-space(s) iff length(s) = 2 and pts(s) in set and sigma(s) in power(power(pts(s))) and is-sigma-algebra(pts(s), sigma(s)))

*Theorems quantifying over `is-measurable-space`.*

- `hom-measurable-space-id` — forall([a], is-measurable-space(a) implies is-hom-measurable-space(a, a, id-fun(pts(a))))

*Views into `measurable-space`.*

- `measure-space-as-measurable-space` — from `measure-space`: (pts sigma) ↦ (pts sigma)

### measure-space
<a id="measure-space"></a>

*Declared in* [`structure-library/measurable-space.scm`](../structure-library/measurable-space.scm).

*Kind.* Shape structure — `declare-structure` with slot clauses.

*Slots* (3): `pts` carrier; `sigma` family of subsets of pts; `meas` function on the family sigma into rr-pos-star.

*Default arrows* (`is-hom-measure-space-def`): forall([s, t, f], is-hom-measure-space(s, t, f) iff is-measure-space(s) and is-measure-space(t) and f in fun(pts(s), pts(t)) and forall([u in sigma(t)], preimage(s, f, u) in sigma(s)) and forall([u in sigma(t)], (meas(t))(u) = (meas(s))(preimage(s, f, u))))

*Declaration.*

```scheme
(declare-structure measure-space
  (instance-var s)
  (carriers pts)
  (family sigma pts)
  (family-fun meas sigma rr-pos-star)
  (law (is-measure (pts s) (sigma s) (meas s))))
```

*Defining predicate* (as stored):

- `is-measure-space` — forall([s], is-measure-space(s) iff length(s) = 3 and pts(s) in set and sigma(s) in power(power(pts(s))) and meas(s) in fun(sigma(s), rr-pos-star) and is-measure(pts(s), sigma(s), meas(s)))

*Theorems quantifying over `is-measure-space`.*

- `hom-measure-space-id` — forall([a], is-measure-space(a) implies is-hom-measure-space(a, a, id-fun(pts(a))))
- `measure-space-as-measurable-space-is-measurable-space` — forall([r], is-measure-space(r) implies is-measurable-space(measure-space-as-measurable-space(r)))

*Views from `measure-space`.*

- `measure-space-as-measurable-space` — into `measurable-space`: (pts sigma) ↦ (pts sigma)

### metric-space
<a id="metric-space"></a>

*Declared in* [`structure-library/metric-space.scm`](../structure-library/metric-space.scm).

*Kind.* Shape structure — `declare-structure` with slot clauses.

*Slots* (2): `pts` carrier; `dist` operation cartesian(pts, pts) -> rr.

*Default arrows* (`is-hom-metric-space-def`): forall([a, b, f], is-hom-metric-space(a, b, f) iff is-metric-space(a) and is-metric-space(b) and f in fun(pts(a), pts(b)) and forall([x1_ in pts(a), x2_ in pts(a)], (dist(a))(x1_, x2_) = (dist(b))(f(x1_), f(x2_))))

*Declaration.*

```scheme
(declare-structure metric-space
  (carriers pts)
  (op dist (cartesian pts pts) rr)
  (property is-metric dist pts))
```

*Defining predicate* (as stored):

- `is-metric-space` — forall([s], is-metric-space(s) iff length(s) = 2 and pts(s) in set and dist(s) in fun(cartesian(pts(s), pts(s)), rr) and is-metric(dist(s), pts(s)))

*Theorems quantifying over `is-metric-space`.*

- `ball-2r-triangle` — forall([s], is-metric-space(s) implies forall([x in pts(s), r], r in rr and 0 <= r and not(0 = r) implies forall([y in ball(s, x, r), z in ball(s, x, r)], (dist(s))(y, z) <= r + r and not((dist(s))(y, z) = r + r))))
- `ball-center-in` — forall([s], is-metric-space(s) implies forall([x in pts(s), r], r in rr and 0 <= r and not(0 = r) implies x in ball(s, x, r)))
- `ball-cover-is-open-cover` — forall([s], is-metric-space(s) implies forall([r], r in rr and 0 <= r and not(0 = r) implies is-open-cover(s, ball-cover(s, r))))
- `ball-in-closed-ball` — forall([s], is-metric-space(s) implies forall([c, r], ball(s, c, r) subset closed-ball(s, c, r)))
- `ball-is-open` — forall([s], is-metric-space(s) implies forall([x in pts(s), r], r in rr and 0 <= r and not(0 = r) implies is-open(s, ball(s, x, r))))
- `ball-is-set` — forall([s], is-metric-space(s) implies forall([x, r], ball(s, x, r) in set))
- `ball-mem-from-le` — forall([s], is-metric-space(s) implies forall([x in pts(s), y, d, r], y in pts(s) and d in rr and r in rr and (dist(s))(x, y) <= d and d < r implies y in ball(s, x, r)))
- `bdd-metric-bounded` — forall([s], is-metric-space(s) implies forall([x in pts(s), y in pts(s)], (dist(bdd-metric(s)))(x, y) < 1))
- `bdd-metric-carrier` — forall([s], is-metric-space(s) implies pts(bdd-metric(s)) == pts(s))
- `bdd-metric-carrier-rev` — forall([s], is-metric-space(s) implies pts(s) == pts(bdd-metric(s)))
- `bdd-metric-converges-bwd` — forall([s], is-metric-space(s) implies forall([f in fun(nn, pts(s)), lm_ in pts(s)], converges-to(bdd-metric(s), f, lm_) implies converges-to(s, f, lm_)))
- `bdd-metric-converges-fwd` — forall([s], is-metric-space(s) implies forall([f in fun(nn, pts(s)), lm_ in pts(s)], converges-to(s, f, lm_) implies converges-to(bdd-metric(s), f, lm_)))
- `bdd-metric-converges-iff` — forall([s], is-metric-space(s) implies forall([f in fun(nn, pts(s)), lm_ in pts(s)], converges-to(bdd-metric(s), f, lm_) iff converges-to(s, f, lm_)))
- `bdd-metric-converges-iff-rev` — forall([s], is-metric-space(s) implies forall([f in fun(nn, pts(s)), lm_ in pts(s)], converges-to(s, f, lm_) iff converges-to(bdd-metric(s), f, lm_)))
- `bdd-metric-dist-le` — forall([s], is-metric-space(s) implies forall([x_ in pts(s), y_ in pts(s)], (dist(bdd-metric(s)))(x_, y_) <= (dist(s))(x_, y_)))
- `bdd-metric-dist-reflect` — forall([s], is-metric-space(s) implies forall([x_ in pts(s), y_ in pts(s), e_ in rr], 0 <= e_ implies (dist(bdd-metric(s)))(x_, y_) <= (/)(e_, 1 + e_) implies (dist(s))(x_, y_) <= e_))
- `bdd-metric-distance` — forall([s], is-metric-space(s) implies forall([x in pts(s), y in pts(s)], (dist(bdd-metric(s)))(x, y) = (/)((dist(s))(x, y), 1 + (dist(s))(x, y))))
- `bdd-metric-distance-rev` — forall([s], is-metric-space(s) implies forall([x in pts(s), y in pts(s)], (/)((dist(s))(x, y), 1 + (dist(s))(x, y)) = (dist(bdd-metric(s)))(x, y)))
- `bdd-metric-id-bicontinuous` — forall([s], is-metric-space(s) implies is-continuous(s, bdd-metric(s), vnb-lambda(x, pts(s), x)) and is-continuous(bdd-metric(s), s, vnb-lambda(x, pts(bdd-metric(s)), x)))
- `bdd-metric-is-bounded-metric-space` — forall([s], is-metric-space(s) implies is-bounded-metric-space(bdd-metric(s)))
- `bdd-metric-is-metric-space` — forall([s], is-metric-space(s) implies is-metric-space(bdd-metric(s)))
- `bdd-metric-open-bwd` — forall([s], is-metric-space(s) implies forall([uu], is-open(bdd-metric(s), uu) implies is-open(s, uu)))
- `bdd-metric-open-fwd` — forall([s], is-metric-space(s) implies forall([uu], is-open(s, uu) implies is-open(bdd-metric(s), uu)))
- `bdd-metric-preserves-metric-top` — forall([s], is-metric-space(s) implies metric-top(bdd-metric(s)) == metric-top(s))
- `bdd-metric-preserves-metric-top-rev` — forall([s], is-metric-space(s) implies metric-top(s) == metric-top(bdd-metric(s)))
- `bdd-metric-weight-bound` — forall([s], is-metric-space(s) implies forall([u_ in pts(s), v_ in pts(s), wn in rr], 0 <= wn implies wn * (dist(bdd-metric(s)))(u_, v_) in rr and 0 <= wn * (dist(bdd-metric(s)))(u_, v_) and wn * (dist(bdd-metric(s)))(u_, v_) <= wn))
- `carrier-is-open` — forall([s], is-metric-space(s) implies is-open(s, pts(s)))
- `cauchy-block-estimate` — forall([s], is-metric-space(s) implies forall([c in pts(s), r], pos-rr(r) implies forall([u], u = ball(s, c, r) implies forall([y in u, z in u, d], pos-rr(d) implies forall([eps in rr], r <= d implies d + d = eps implies (dist(s))(y, z) <= eps)))))
- `cauchy-setoid-is-setoid` — forall([m], is-metric-space(m) implies is-setoid(cauchy-setoid(m)))
- `closed-ball-center-in` — forall([s], is-metric-space(s) implies forall([cv_ in pts(s), qv_ in rr], 0 <= qv_ implies cv_ in closed-ball(s, cv_, qv_)))
- `closed-ball-in-ball` — forall([s], is-metric-space(s) implies forall([c in pts(s), r in rr, q in rr], r < q implies closed-ball(s, c, r) subset ball(s, c, q)))
- `closed-ball-in-carrier` — forall([s], is-metric-space(s) implies forall([cv_, qv_], closed-ball(s, cv_, qv_) subset pts(s)))
- `closed-ball-in-power` — forall([s], is-metric-space(s) implies forall([cv_, qv_], closed-ball(s, cv_, qv_) in power(pts(s))))
- `closed-ball-is-closed` — forall([s], is-metric-space(s) implies forall([c in pts(s), r in rr], is-closed(s, closed-ball(s, c, r))))
- `closed-ball-is-set` — forall([s], is-metric-space(s) implies forall([c, r], closed-ball(s, c, r) in set))
- `closed-iff-contains-accumulation-points` — forall([s], is-metric-space(s) implies forall([a], a subset pts(s) implies is-closed(s, a) iff forall([x], is-accumulation-point(s, a, x) implies x in a)))
- `closed-iff-contains-accumulation-points-rev` — forall([s], is-metric-space(s) implies forall([a], a subset pts(s) implies forall([x], is-accumulation-point(s, a, x) implies x in a) iff is-closed(s, a)))
- `closed-limit-in` — forall([s], is-metric-space(s) implies forall([a], is-closed(s, a) implies forall([f in fun(nn, a), l], converges-to(s, f, l) implies l in a)))
- `closure-contains` — forall([s], is-metric-space(s) implies forall([a], a subset pts(s) implies a subset closure(s, a)))
- `closure-in-carrier` — forall([s], is-metric-space(s) implies forall([a], closure(s, a) subset pts(s)))
- `closure-is-closed` — forall([s], is-metric-space(s) implies forall([a], is-closed(s, closure(s, a))))
- `closure-is-set` — forall([s], is-metric-space(s) implies forall([a], closure(s, a) in set))
- `closure-of-ball-in-closed-ball` — forall([s], is-metric-space(s) implies forall([c in pts(s), r in rr], closure(s, ball(s, c, r)) subset closed-ball(s, c, r)))
- `compact-iff-cluster-point` — forall([s], is-metric-space(s) implies is-compact(s) iff forall([f in fun(nn, pts(s))], forsome([x], cluster-point(s, f, x))))
- `compact-iff-cluster-point-rev` — forall([s], is-metric-space(s) implies forall([f in fun(nn, pts(s))], forsome([x], cluster-point(s, f, x))) iff is-compact(s))
- `compact-iff-fip` — forall([s], is-metric-space(s) implies is-compact(s) iff forall([c], has-fip(s, c) implies forsome([p], p in intersection-of(c))))
- `compact-iff-fip-rev` — forall([s], is-metric-space(s) implies forall([c], has-fip(s, c) implies forsome([p], p in intersection-of(c))) iff is-compact(s))
- `compact-iff-seq-compact` — forall([s], is-metric-space(s) implies is-compact(s) iff seq-compact(s))
- `compact-iff-seq-compact-rev` — forall([s], is-metric-space(s) implies seq-compact(s) iff is-compact(s))
- `compact-iff-tb-complete` — forall([s], is-metric-space(s) implies is-compact(s) iff totally-bounded(s) and is-complete(s))
- `compact-iff-tb-complete-rev` — forall([s], is-metric-space(s) implies totally-bounded(s) and is-complete(s) iff is-compact(s))
- `compact-implies-fip` — forall([s], is-metric-space(s) implies is-compact(s) implies forall([c], has-fip(s, c) implies forsome([p], p in intersection-of(c))))
- `compact-implies-seq-compact` — forall([s], is-metric-space(s) implies is-compact(s) implies seq-compact(s))
- `compact-subspace-is-closed` — forall([s], is-metric-space(s) implies forall([a], a subset pts(s) implies is-compact(subspace-ms(s, a)) implies is-closed(s, a)))
- `completion-is-complete` — forall([m], is-metric-space(m) implies is-complete(completion(m)))
- `completion-is-metric-space` — forall([m], is-metric-space(m) implies is-metric-space(completion(m)))
- `converges-dist-null-bwd` — forall([t], is-metric-space(t) implies forall([f in fun(nn, pts(t)), lv in pts(t)], converges-to(rr-ms, vnb-lambda(j_, nn, (dist(t))(f(j_), lv)), 0) implies converges-to(t, f, lv)))
- `converges-dist-null-fwd` — forall([t], is-metric-space(t) implies forall([f in fun(nn, pts(t)), lv in pts(t)], converges-to(t, f, lv) implies converges-to(rr-ms, vnb-lambda(j_, nn, (dist(t))(f(j_), lv)), 0)))
- `converges-iff-dist-null` — forall([t], is-metric-space(t) implies forall([f in fun(nn, pts(t)), lv in pts(t)], converges-to(t, f, lv) iff converges-to(rr-ms, vnb-lambda(j_, nn, (dist(t))(f(j_), lv)), 0)))
- `converges-iff-dist-null-rev` — forall([t], is-metric-space(t) implies forall([f in fun(nn, pts(t)), lv in pts(t)], converges-to(rr-ms, vnb-lambda(j_, nn, (dist(t))(f(j_), lv)), 0) iff converges-to(t, f, lv)))
- `converges-to-transfer` — forall([t], is-metric-space(t) implies forall([f in fun(nn, pts(t)), h in fun(nn, pts(t)), lv in pts(t)], converges-to(t, f, lv) implies forall([j_ in nn], h(j_) = f(j_)) implies converges-to(t, h, lv)))
- `dist-layer-disjoint` — forall([m], is-metric-space(m) implies forall([fcl], fcl subset pts(m) implies forall([n in nn, x in {mex_ in pts(m): recip(n + 1) <= pt-set-dist(m, mex_, fcl)}], not(x in fcl))))
- `dist-layer-gap` — forall([m], is-metric-space(m) implies forall([fcl], fcl subset pts(m) implies forall([j in nn, x, y], x in {mex_ in pts(m): recip(j + 1) <= pt-set-dist(m, mex_, fcl)} implies y in pts(m) implies not(y in {mex_ in pts(m): recip(succ(j) + 1) <= pt-set-dist(m, mex_, fcl)}) implies recip(j + 1) - recip(succ(j) + 1) <= (dist(m))(x, y))))
- `dist-layer-mem-elim` — forall([m], is-metric-space(m) implies forall([fcl], fcl subset pts(m) implies forall([n in nn, x in {mex_ in pts(m): recip(n + 1) <= pt-set-dist(m, mex_, fcl)}], x in pts(m) and forall([mzz_ in fcl], recip(n + 1) <= (dist(m))(x, mzz_)))))
- `dist-layer-mono` — forall([m], is-metric-space(m) implies forall([fcl], fcl subset pts(m) implies forall([k, n], k in nn implies n in nn implies k <= n implies {mex_ in pts(m): recip(k + 1) <= pt-set-dist(m, mex_, fcl)} subset {mex_ in pts(m): recip(n + 1) <= pt-set-dist(m, mex_, fcl)})))
- `dist-layer-set-sep` — forall([m], is-metric-space(m) implies forall([fcl], fcl subset pts(m) implies forall([j in nn, aa, bb], aa subset {mex_ in pts(m): recip(j + 1) <= pt-set-dist(m, mex_, fcl)} implies forall([myy_ in bb], myy_ in pts(m) and not(myy_ in {mex_ in pts(m): recip(succ(j) + 1) <= pt-set-dist(m, mex_, fcl)})) implies not(set-dist(m, aa, bb) = 0) and not(set-dist(m, bb, aa) = 0))))
- `dist-le-implies-in-carrier` — forall([s], is-metric-space(s) implies forall([cc_, pp_, rr_], (dist(s))(cc_, pp_) <= rr_ implies cc_ in pts(s)))
- `dist-seq-in-fun` — forall([t], is-metric-space(t) implies forall([f in fun(nn, pts(t)), lv in pts(t)], vnb-lambda(j_, nn, (dist(t))(f(j_), lv)) in fun(nn, rr)))
- `embed-in-fun` — forall([m], is-metric-space(m) implies embed(m) in fun(pts(m), pts(completion(m))))
- `embed-isometry` — forall([m], is-metric-space(m) implies forall([u in pts(m), v in pts(m)], (dist(completion(m)))((embed(m))(u), (embed(m))(v)) = (dist(m))(u, v)))
- `embed-isometry-rev` — forall([m], is-metric-space(m) implies forall([u in pts(m), v in pts(m)], (dist(m))(u, v) = (dist(completion(m)))((embed(m))(u), (embed(m))(v))))
- `empty-is-open` — forall([s], is-metric-space(s) implies is-open(s, empty-set))
- `finite-ball-subcover-r-net` — forall([s], is-metric-space(s) implies forall([r], r in rr and 0 <= r and not(0 = r) implies forsome([f], f subset ball-cover(s, r) and card(f) in nn and is-open-cover(s, f)) implies forsome([n], card(n) in nn and is-r-net(s, n, pts(s), r))))
- `fip-implies-compact` — forall([s], is-metric-space(s) implies forall([c], has-fip(s, c) implies forsome([p], p in intersection-of(c))) implies is-compact(s))
- `fom-closure-near` — forall([s], is-metric-space(s) implies forall([aa, z in closure(s, aa), rho], pos-rr(rho) implies forsome([pt in aa], (dist(s))(z, pt) < rho)))
- `hom-continuous-id` — forall([a], is-metric-space(a) implies is-continuous-arrow(a, a, id-fun(pts(a))))
- `hom-lipschitz-id` — forall([a], is-metric-space(a) implies is-lipschitz-arrow(a, a, id-fun(pts(a))))
- `hom-metric-space-id` — forall([a], is-metric-space(a) implies is-hom-metric-space(a, a, id-fun(pts(a))))
- `hom-uniformly-continuous-id` — forall([a], is-metric-space(a) implies is-uniformly-continuous-arrow(a, a, id-fun(pts(a))))
- `inter-of-opens-open` — forall([s], is-metric-space(s) implies forall([u, w], is-open(s, u) and is-open(s, w) implies is-open(s, intersection(u, w))))
- `interior-in-carrier` — forall([s], is-metric-space(s) implies forall([a], interior(s, a) subset pts(s)))
- `interior-in-set` — forall([s], is-metric-space(s) implies forall([a], interior(s, a) subset a))
- `interior-is-open` — forall([s], is-metric-space(s) implies forall([a], is-open(s, interior(s, a))))
- `interior-is-set` — forall([s], is-metric-space(s) implies forall([a], interior(s, a) in set))

- `metric-pos` — forall([s], is-metric-space(s) implies forall([x in pts(s), y in pts(s)], 0 <= (dist(s))(x, y)))
- `metric-self-zero` — forall([s], is-metric-space(s) implies forall([x in pts(s)], (dist(s))(x, x) = 0))
- `metric-self-zero-rev` — forall([s], is-metric-space(s) implies forall([x in pts(s)], 0 = (dist(s))(x, x)))
