- `set-dist-union-pos` — forall([m], is-metric-space(m) implies forall([aa, bb, ds], aa subset pts(m) implies bb subset pts(m) implies ds subset pts(m) implies not(set-dist(m, aa, ds) = 0) implies not(set-dist(m, bb, ds) = 0) implies not(set-dist(m, union(aa, bb), ds) = 0)))
- `subspace-ball` — forall([s], is-metric-space(s) implies forall([a], a subset pts(s) implies forall([c in a, r], ball(subspace-ms(s, a), c, r) = intersection(ball(s, c, r), a))))
- `subspace-ball-membership` — forall([s], is-metric-space(s) implies forall([a], a subset pts(s) implies forall([c in a, r, y], y in ball(subspace-ms(s, a), c, r) iff y in ball(s, c, r) and y in a)))
- `subspace-ball-membership-rev` — forall([s], is-metric-space(s) implies forall([a], a subset pts(s) implies forall([c in a, r, y], y in ball(s, c, r) and y in a iff y in ball(subspace-ms(s, a), c, r))))
- `subspace-ball-rev` — forall([s], is-metric-space(s) implies forall([a], a subset pts(s) implies forall([c in a, r], intersection(ball(s, c, r), a) = ball(subspace-ms(s, a), c, r))))
- `subspace-converges-to` — forall([s], is-metric-space(s) implies forall([a], a subset pts(s) implies forall([g in fun(nn, a), l in a], converges-to(subspace-ms(s, a), g, l) iff converges-to(s, g, l))))
- `subspace-converges-to-rev` — forall([s], is-metric-space(s) implies forall([a], a subset pts(s) implies forall([g in fun(nn, a), l in a], converges-to(s, g, l) iff converges-to(subspace-ms(s, a), g, l))))
- `subspace-is-cauchy-seq` — forall([s], is-metric-space(s) implies forall([a], a subset pts(s) implies forall([f in fun(nn, a)], is-cauchy-seq(subspace-ms(s, a), f) iff is-cauchy-seq(s, f))))
- `subspace-is-cauchy-seq-rev` — forall([s], is-metric-space(s) implies forall([a], a subset pts(s) implies forall([f in fun(nn, a)], is-cauchy-seq(s, f) iff is-cauchy-seq(subspace-ms(s, a), f))))
- `subspace-is-metric-space` — forall([s], is-metric-space(s) implies forall([a], a subset pts(s) implies is-metric-space(subspace-ms(s, a))))
- `subspace-open-is-trace` — forall([s], is-metric-space(s) implies forall([a], a subset pts(s) implies forall([vs_], is-open(subspace-ms(s, a), vs_) implies forsome([us_], is-open(s, us_) and vs_ = intersection(us_, a)))))
- `subspace-open-trace` — forall([s], is-metric-space(s) implies forall([a], a subset pts(s) implies forall([u], is-open(s, u) implies is-open(subspace-ms(s, a), intersection(u, a)))))
- `subspace-restrict-continuous-at` — forall([s], is-metric-space(s) implies forall([icu_], icu_ subset pts(s) implies forall([icv_], icv_ subset icu_ implies forall([ict_], is-metric-space(ict_) implies forall([f, icz_ in icv_], is-continuous-at(subspace-ms(s, icu_), ict_, f, icz_) implies is-continuous-at(subspace-ms(s, icv_), ict_, restrict(f, icv_), icz_))))))
- `subspace-whole` — forall([s], is-metric-space(s) implies pts(subspace-ms(s, pts(s))) == pts(s) and forall([u_, v_], u_ in pts(s) implies v_ in pts(s) implies (dist(subspace-ms(s, pts(s))))(u_, v_) == (dist(s))(u_, v_)))
- `trunc-metric-bounded` — forall([s], is-metric-space(s) implies forall([u_, v_], u_ in pts(s) implies v_ in pts(s) implies (dist(trunc-metric(s)))(u_, v_) <= 1))
- `trunc-metric-id-uniformly-continuous` — forall([s], is-metric-space(s) implies is-uniformly-continuous(s, trunc-metric(s), vnb-lambda(a_, pts(s), a_)))
- `trunc-metric-id-uniformly-continuous-back` — forall([s], is-metric-space(s) implies is-uniformly-continuous(trunc-metric(s), s, vnb-lambda(a_, pts(s), a_)))
- `trunc-metric-is-metric-space` — forall([s], is-metric-space(s) implies is-metric-space(trunc-metric(s)))
- `union-of-opens-open` — forall([s], is-metric-space(s) implies forall([a, g], forall([i in a], is-open(s, g(i))) implies is-open(s, big-union(i, a, g(i)))))

### metrizable-top-space
<a id="metrizable-top-space"></a>

*Declared in* [`structure-library/top-space.scm`](../structure-library/top-space.scm).

*Kind.* Refinement — `declare-structure` with `(same-shape-as top-space)`; its `IS-X <=> IS-PARENT and <laws>` axiom is generated, not hand-written.  Subtype of [`top-space`](#top-space).  Shape inherited from `top-space` — slots (pts opens).

*Declaration.*

```scheme
(declare-structure metrizable-top-space
  (instance-var s)
  (same-shape-as top-space)
  (law (forsome md (and (is-metric-space md) (== s (list (pts md) (sep u (power (pts md)) (is-open md u))))))))
```

*Defining predicate* (as stored):

- `is-metrizable-top-space-def` — forall([s], is-metrizable-top-space(s) iff is-top-space(s) and forsome([md], is-metric-space(md) and s == [pts(md), {u in power(pts(md)): is-open(md, u)}]))

*Theorems quantifying over `is-metrizable-top-space`.*

- `hom-metrizable-top-space-id` — forall([s], is-metrizable-top-space(s) implies is-hom-metrizable-top-space(s, s, id-fun(pts(s))))
- `metrizable-has-metric-top` — forall([s], is-metrizable-top-space(s) implies forsome([md], is-metric-space(md) and metric-top(md) == s))
- `rb3-metrizable-hausdorff` — forall([s], is-metrizable-top-space(s) implies is-hausdorff(s))

### module
<a id="module"></a>

*Declared in* [`structure-library/module.scm`](../structure-library/module.scm).

*Kind.* Shape structure — `declare-structure` with slot clauses.

*Slots* (6): `scal` substructure, a ring; `vec` carrier; `vadd` operation cartesian(vec, vec) -> vec; `vzero` constant in vec; `vneg` operation vec -> vec; `act` operation cartesian(carr(scal), vec) -> vec.

*Default arrows* (`is-hom-module-def`): forall([a, b, f], is-hom-module(a, b, f) iff is-module(a) and is-module(b) and f in fun(vec(a), vec(b)) and scal(a) = scal(b) and forall([x1_ in vec(a), x2_ in vec(a)], f((vadd(a))(x1_, x2_)) = (vadd(b))(f(x1_), f(x2_))) and f(vzero(a)) = vzero(b) and forall([x1_ in vec(a)], f((vneg(a))(x1_)) = (vneg(b))(f(x1_))) and forall([x1_ in carr(scal(a)), x2_ in vec(a)], f((act(a))(x1_, x2_)) = (act(b))(x1_, f(x2_))))

*Declaration.*

```scheme
(declare-structure module
  (instance-var s)
  (substructure scal ring)
  (carriers vec)
  (op vadd (cartesian vec vec) vec)
  (constant vzero vec)
  (op vneg vec vec)
  (op act (cartesian (carr scal) vec) vec)
  (property is-associative vadd vec)
  (property is-commutative vadd vec)
  (property is-identity vadd vzero vec)
  (property has-inverses vadd vzero vneg vec)
  (law "forall([r_ in carr(scal(s)), x_ in vec(s), y_ in vec(s)],
          act(s)(r_, vadd(s)(x_, y_)) = vadd(s)(act(s)(r_, x_), act(s)(r_, y_)))")
  (law "forall([r_ in carr(scal(s)), s_ in carr(scal(s)), x_ in vec(s)],
          act(s)(add(scal(s))(r_, s_), x_) = vadd(s)(act(s)(r_, x_), act(s)(s_, x_)))")
  (law "forall([r_ in carr(scal(s)), s_ in carr(scal(s)), x_ in vec(s)],
          act(s)(mul(scal(s))(r_, s_), x_) = act(s)(r_, act(s)(s_, x_)))")
  (law "forall([x_ in vec(s)], act(s)(one(scal(s)), x_) = x_)"))
```

*Defining predicate* (as stored):

- `is-module` — forall([s], is-module(s) iff length(s) = 6 and is-ring(scal(s)) and vec(s) in set and vadd(s) in fun(cartesian(vec(s), vec(s)), vec(s)) and vzero(s) in vec(s) and vneg(s) in fun(vec(s), vec(s)) and act(s) in fun(cartesian(carr(scal(s)), vec(s)), vec(s)) and is-associative(vadd(s), vec(s)) and is-commutative(vadd(s), vec(s)) and is-identity(vadd(s), vzero(s), vec(s)) and has-inverses(vadd(s), vzero(s), vneg(s), vec(s)) and forall([r_ in carr(scal(s)), x_ in vec(s), y_ in vec(s)], (act(s))(r_, (vadd(s))(x_, y_)) = (vadd(s))((act(s))(r_, x_), (act(s))(r_, y_))) and forall([r_ in carr(scal(s)), s_ in carr(scal(s)), x_ in vec(s)], (act(s))((add(scal(s)))(r_, s_), x_) = (vadd(s))((act(s))(r_, x_), (act(s))(s_, x_))) and forall([r_ in carr(scal(s)), s_ in carr(scal(s)), x_ in vec(s)], (act(s))((mul(scal(s)))(r_, s_), x_) = (act(s))(r_, (act(s))(s_, x_))) and forall([x_ in vec(s)], (act(s))(one(scal(s)), x_) = x_))

*Theorems quantifying over `is-module`.*

- `abelian-group-as-monoid-is-monoid-module-vector-ag` — forall([r], is-module(r) implies is-monoid(abelian-group-as-monoid(module-vector-ag(r))))
- `abelian-group-assoc-module-vector-ag` — forall([r], is-module(r) implies forall([a in vec(r), b in vec(r), c in vec(r)], (vadd(r))((vadd(r))(a, b), c) = (vadd(r))(a, (vadd(r))(b, c))))
- `abelian-group-assoc-module-vector-ag-rev` — forall([r], is-module(r) implies forall([a in vec(r), b in vec(r), c in vec(r)], (vadd(r))(a, (vadd(r))(b, c)) = (vadd(r))((vadd(r))(a, b), c)))
- `abelian-group-idempotent-is-id-module-vector-ag` — forall([r], is-module(r) implies forall([a in vec(r)], (vadd(r))(a, a) = a implies a = vzero(r)))
- `abelian-group-idempotent-is-id-module-vector-ag-rev` — forall([r], is-module(r) implies forall([a in vec(r)], (vadd(r))(a, a) = a implies vzero(r) = a))
- `abelian-group-inverse-unique-module-vector-ag` — forall([r], is-module(r) implies forall([a in vec(r), b in vec(r)], (vadd(r))(a, b) = vzero(r) implies b = (vneg(r))(a)))
- `abelian-group-inverse-unique-module-vector-ag-rev` — forall([r], is-module(r) implies forall([a in vec(r), b in vec(r)], (vadd(r))(a, b) = vzero(r) implies (vneg(r))(a) = b))
- `abelian-group-right-id-module-vector-ag` — forall([r], is-module(r) implies forall([a in vec(r)], (vadd(r))(a, vzero(r)) = a))
- `abelian-group-right-id-module-vector-ag-rev` — forall([r], is-module(r) implies forall([a in vec(r)], a = (vadd(r))(a, vzero(r))))
- `descent-remainder` — forall([md], is-module(md) implies is-commutative-ring(scal(md)) implies forall([n in nn, u in mat(succ(n), 1, vec(md)), c in mat(1, succ(n), carr(scal(md))), c0 in mat(1, succ(n), carr(scal(md))), q in carr(scal(md)), b in carr(scal(md))], entry(c0, 1, succ(n)) = b implies entry(c, 1, succ(n)) = (mul(scal(md)))(q, b) implies forsome([y in span(md, n, block(u, n, 1))], lincomb(md, succ(n), c, u) = (vadd(md))(y, (act(md))(q, lincomb(md, succ(n), c0, u))) and y = (vadd(md))(lincomb(md, succ(n), c, u), (act(md))((neg(scal(md)))(q), lincomb(md, succ(n), c0, u))))))
- `fbs-id-linear` — forall([m1], is-module(m1) implies is-linear-map(m1, m1, vnb-lambda(iv_, vec(m1), iv_)))
- `fbs-v-add-sub` — forall([m], is-module(m) implies forall([x in vec(m), y in vec(m)], (vadd(m))((vadd(m))(x, y), (vneg(m))(y)) = x))
- `fbs-v-add-sub-rev` — forall([m], is-module(m) implies forall([x in vec(m), y in vec(m)], x = (vadd(m))((vadd(m))(x, y), (vneg(m))(y))))
- `fbs-v-sub-add` — forall([m], is-module(m) implies forall([x in vec(m), y in vec(m)], (vadd(m))((vadd(m))(x, (vneg(m))(y)), y) = x))
- `fbs-v-sub-add-rev` — forall([m], is-module(m) implies forall([x in vec(m), y in vec(m)], x = (vadd(m))((vadd(m))(x, (vneg(m))(y)), y)))
- `finsum-act-collect-gen` — forall([md], is-module(md) implies forall([x in vec(md), s in set], card(s) in nn implies forall([c in fun(s, carr(scal(md)))], (act(md))(finsum(ring-additive-ag(scal(md)), c, s), x) = finsum(module-vector-ag(md), vnb-lambda(z, s, (act(md))(c(z), x)), s))))
- `finsum-act-collect-gen-rev` — forall([md], is-module(md) implies forall([x in vec(md), s in set], card(s) in nn implies forall([c in fun(s, carr(scal(md)))], finsum(module-vector-ag(md), vnb-lambda(z, s, (act(md))(c(z), x)), s) = (act(md))(finsum(ring-additive-ag(scal(md)), c, s), x))))
- `finsum-act-distrib-gen` — forall([md], is-module(md) implies forall([r in carr(scal(md)), s in set], card(s) in nn implies forall([f in fun(s, carr(module-vector-ag(md)))], (act(md))(r, finsum(module-vector-ag(md), f, s)) = finsum(module-vector-ag(md), vnb-lambda(z, s, (act(md))(r, f(z))), s))))
- `finsum-act-distrib-gen-rev` — forall([md], is-module(md) implies forall([r in carr(scal(md)), s in set], card(s) in nn implies forall([f in fun(s, carr(module-vector-ag(md)))], finsum(module-vector-ag(md), vnb-lambda(z, s, (act(md))(r, f(z))), s) = (act(md))(r, finsum(module-vector-ag(md), f, s)))))
- `fom-v-quad` — forall([m], is-module(m) implies forall([p in vec(m), q in vec(m), a1 in vec(m), a2 in vec(m)], (vadd(m))((vadd(m))(p, (vneg(m))(q)), (vneg(m))((vadd(m))(a1, (vneg(m))(a2)))) = (vadd(m))((vadd(m))(p, (vneg(m))(a1)), (vneg(m))((vadd(m))(q, (vneg(m))(a2))))))
- `fom-v-quad-rev` — forall([m], is-module(m) implies forall([p in vec(m), q in vec(m), a1 in vec(m), a2 in vec(m)], (vadd(m))((vadd(m))(p, (vneg(m))(a1)), (vneg(m))((vadd(m))(q, (vneg(m))(a2)))) = (vadd(m))((vadd(m))(p, (vneg(m))(q)), (vneg(m))((vadd(m))(a1, (vneg(m))(a2))))))
- `fom-v-shift-back` — forall([m], is-module(m) implies forall([w in vec(m), c in vec(m)], (vadd(m))((vadd(m))(w, c), (vneg(m))(c)) = w))
- `fom-v-shift-back-rev` — forall([m], is-module(m) implies forall([w in vec(m), c in vec(m)], w = (vadd(m))((vadd(m))(w, c), (vneg(m))(c))))
- `fom-v-sub-sum` — forall([m], is-module(m) implies forall([y in vec(m), a in vec(m), b in vec(m)], (vadd(m))(y, (vneg(m))((vadd(m))(a, b))) = (vadd(m))((vadd(m))(y, (vneg(m))(b)), (vneg(m))(a))))
- `fom-v-sub-sum-rev` — forall([m], is-module(m) implies forall([y in vec(m), a in vec(m), b in vec(m)], (vadd(m))((vadd(m))(y, (vneg(m))(b)), (vneg(m))(a)) = (vadd(m))(y, (vneg(m))((vadd(m))(a, b)))))
- `free-length-le-generators` — forall([md], is-module(md) implies is-euclidean-ring(scal(md)) implies forall([n, m, u, v], n in nn implies m in nn implies u in mat(n, 1, vec(md)) implies v in mat(m, 1, vec(md)) implies generates(md, n, u) implies rel-free(md, m, v) implies m <= n))
- `free-transport` — forall([md], is-module(md) implies forall([m, v, qm], v in mat(m, 1, vec(md)) implies is-invertible-mat(scal(md), m, qm) implies rel-free(md, m, v) implies rel-free(md, m, matact(md, qm, v))))
- `frm-act-diff` — forall([m], is-module(m) implies forall([r, s, x, y], r in carr(scal(m)) implies s in carr(scal(m)) implies x in vec(m) implies y in vec(m) implies (vadd(m))((act(m))(r, x), (vneg(m))((act(m))(s, y))) = (vadd(m))((act(m))(r, (vadd(m))(x, (vneg(m))(y))), (act(m))((add(scal(m)))(r, (neg(scal(m)))(s)), y))))
- `frm-act-diff-rev` — forall([m], is-module(m) implies forall([r, s, x, y], r in carr(scal(m)) implies s in carr(scal(m)) implies x in vec(m) implies y in vec(m) implies (vadd(m))((act(m))(r, (vadd(m))(x, (vneg(m))(y))), (act(m))((add(scal(m)))(r, (neg(scal(m)))(s)), y)) = (vadd(m))((act(m))(r, x), (vneg(m))((act(m))(s, y)))))
- `frm-act-neg-scal` — forall([m], is-module(m) implies forall([r, x], r in carr(scal(m)) implies x in vec(m) implies (act(m))((neg(scal(m)))(r), x) = (vneg(m))((act(m))(r, x))))
- `frm-act-neg-scal-rev` — forall([m], is-module(m) implies forall([r, x], r in carr(scal(m)) implies x in vec(m) implies (vneg(m))((act(m))(r, x)) = (act(m))((neg(scal(m)))(r), x)))
- `frm-act-neg-vec` — forall([m], is-module(m) implies forall([r, x], r in carr(scal(m)) implies x in vec(m) implies (act(m))(r, (vneg(m))(x)) = (vneg(m))((act(m))(r, x))))
- `frm-act-neg-vec-rev` — forall([m], is-module(m) implies forall([r, x], r in carr(scal(m)) implies x in vec(m) implies (vneg(m))((act(m))(r, x)) = (act(m))(r, (vneg(m))(x))))
- `frm-act-sub-scal` — forall([m], is-module(m) implies forall([r, s, x], r in carr(scal(m)) implies s in carr(scal(m)) implies x in vec(m) implies (act(m))((add(scal(m)))(r, (neg(scal(m)))(s)), x) = (vadd(m))((act(m))(r, x), (vneg(m))((act(m))(s, x)))))
- `frm-act-sub-scal-rev` — forall([m], is-module(m) implies forall([r, s, x], r in carr(scal(m)) implies s in carr(scal(m)) implies x in vec(m) implies (vadd(m))((act(m))(r, x), (vneg(m))((act(m))(s, x))) = (act(m))((add(scal(m)))(r, (neg(scal(m)))(s)), x)))
- `frm-act-sub-vec` — forall([m], is-module(m) implies forall([r, x, y], r in carr(scal(m)) implies x in vec(m) implies y in vec(m) implies (act(m))(r, (vadd(m))(x, (vneg(m))(y))) = (vadd(m))((act(m))(r, x), (vneg(m))((act(m))(r, y)))))
- `frm-act-sub-vec-rev` — forall([m], is-module(m) implies forall([r, x, y], r in carr(scal(m)) implies x in vec(m) implies y in vec(m) implies (vadd(m))((act(m))(r, x), (vneg(m))((act(m))(r, y))) = (act(m))(r, (vadd(m))(x, (vneg(m))(y)))))
- `frm-module-vec-set` — forall([m], is-module(m) implies vec(m) in set)
- `frm-mvag-inv` — forall([md], is-module(md) implies inv(module-vector-ag(md)) = vneg(md))
- `frm-mvag-inv-rev` — forall([md], is-module(md) implies vneg(md) = inv(module-vector-ag(md)))
- `frm-ti-uniform` — forall([m], is-module(m) implies forall([s1, s2], is-metric-space(s1) implies is-metric-space(s2) implies pts(s1) == vec(m) implies pts(s2) == vec(m) implies forall([x, y, r], x in vec(m) implies y in vec(m) implies r in vec(m) implies (dist(s1))((vadd(m))(x, r), (vadd(m))(y, r)) = (dist(s1))(x, y)) implies forall([x, y, r], x in vec(m) implies y in vec(m) implies r in vec(m) implies (dist(s2))((vadd(m))(x, r), (vadd(m))(y, r)) = (dist(s2))(x, y)) implies forall([sq in fun(nn, vec(m))], converges-to(s1, sq, vzero(m)) implies converges-to(s2, sq, vzero(m))) implies is-uniformly-continuous(s1, s2, id-fun(vec(m)))))

- `frm-v-neg-add-rev` — forall([m], is-module(m) implies forall([x in vec(m), y in vec(m)], (vadd(m))((vneg(m))(x), (vneg(m))(y)) = (vneg(m))((vadd(m))(x, y))))
- `frm-v-neg-neg` — forall([m], is-module(m) implies forall([x in vec(m)], (vneg(m))((vneg(m))(x)) = x))

- `frm-v-right-inv` — forall([m], is-module(m) implies forall([x in vec(m)], (vadd(m))(x, (vneg(m))(x)) = vzero(m)))

- `hbs-sl-intro-pos` — forall([m], is-module(m) implies scal(m) = normed-field-as-commutative-ring(rr-normed-field) implies forall([q in fun(vec(m), rr)], forall([x_ in vec(m), y_ in vec(m)], q((vadd(m))(x_, y_)) <= q(x_) + q(y_)) implies forall([t_ in rr], 0 < t_ implies forall([x_ in vec(m)], q((act(m))(t_, x_)) <= t_ * q(x_))) implies q(vzero(m)) = 0 implies is-sublinear(m, q)))

- `hbs-tilt-props` — forall([m], is-module(m) implies scal(m) = normed-field-as-commutative-ring(rr-normed-field) implies forall([q], is-sublinear(m, q) implies forall([y_ in vec(m), rt in fun(vec(m), rr)], forall([x_ in vec(m), t_ in rr], 0 <= t_ implies rt(x_) <= q((vadd(m))(x_, (act(m))(t_, y_))) - t_ * q(y_)) implies forall([x_ in vec(m), b_ in rr], forall([t_ in rr], 0 <= t_ implies b_ <= q((vadd(m))(x_, (act(m))(t_, y_))) - t_ * q(y_)) implies b_ <= rt(x_)) implies is-sublinear(m, rt) and forall([x_ in vec(m)], rt(x_) <= q(x_)) and rt((act(m))(-1, y_)) <= -q(y_))))

- `module-vector-ag` — into `abelian-group`: (vec vadd vzero vneg) ↦ (carr opr iden inv)

### monoid
<a id="monoid"></a>

*Declared in* [`structure-library/monoid.scm`](../structure-library/monoid.scm).

*Kind.* Shape structure — `declare-structure` with slot clauses.

*Slots* (3): `carr` carrier; `opr` operation cartesian(carr, carr) -> carr; `iden` constant in carr.

*Default arrows* (`is-hom-monoid-def`): forall([a, b, f], is-hom-monoid(a, b, f) iff is-monoid(a) and is-monoid(b) and f in fun(carr(a), carr(b)) and forall([x1_ in carr(a), x2_ in carr(a)], f((opr(a))(x1_, x2_)) = (opr(b))(f(x1_), f(x2_))) and f(iden(a)) = iden(b))

*Declaration.*

```scheme
(declare-structure monoid
  (carriers carr)
  (op opr (cartesian carr carr) carr)
  (constant iden carr)
  (property is-associative opr carr)
  (property is-identity opr iden carr))
```

*Defining predicate* (as stored):

- `is-monoid` — forall([s], is-monoid(s) iff length(s) = 3 and carr(s) in set and opr(s) in fun(cartesian(carr(s), carr(s)), carr(s)) and iden(s) in carr(s) and is-associative(opr(s), carr(s)) and is-identity(opr(s), iden(s), carr(s)))

*Theorems quantifying over `is-monoid`.*

- `hom-monoid-id` — forall([a], is-monoid(a) implies is-hom-monoid(a, a, id-fun(carr(a))))
- `monoid-assoc` — forall([s], is-monoid(s) implies forall([a in carr(s), b in carr(s), c in carr(s)], (opr(s))((opr(s))(a, b), c) = (opr(s))(a, (opr(s))(b, c))))
- `monoid-assoc-rev` — forall([s], is-monoid(s) implies forall([a in carr(s), b in carr(s), c in carr(s)], (opr(s))(a, (opr(s))(b, c)) = (opr(s))((opr(s))(a, b), c)))
- `monoid-carrier-is-set` — forall([m_], is-monoid(m_) implies carr(m_) in set)
- `monoid-identity-in` — forall([m], is-monoid(m) implies iden(m) in carr(m))
- `monoid-left-id` — forall([s], is-monoid(s) implies forall([a in carr(s)], (opr(s))(iden(s), a) = a))
- `monoid-left-id-rev` — forall([s], is-monoid(s) implies forall([a in carr(s)], a = (opr(s))(iden(s), a)))
- `monoid-right-id` — forall([s], is-monoid(s) implies forall([a in carr(s)], (opr(s))(a, iden(s)) = a))
- `monoid-right-id-rev` — forall([s], is-monoid(s) implies forall([a in carr(s)], a = (opr(s))(a, iden(s))))
- `mpow-add` — forall([m], is-monoid(m) implies forall([x in carr(m), j in nn, k in nn], mpow(m, x, j + k) = (opr(m))(mpow(m, x, j), mpow(m, x, k))))
- `mpow-add-rev` — forall([m], is-monoid(m) implies forall([x in carr(m), j in nn, k in nn], (opr(m))(mpow(m, x, j), mpow(m, x, k)) = mpow(m, x, j + k)))
- `mpow-one` — forall([m], is-monoid(m) implies forall([x in carr(m)], mpow(m, x, 1) = x))
- `mpow-one-rev` — forall([m], is-monoid(m) implies forall([x in carr(m)], x = mpow(m, x, 1)))
- `mpow-type` — forall([m], is-monoid(m) implies forall([x in carr(m), n in nn], mpow(m, x, n) in carr(m)))
- `prod-ord-singleton` — forall([m], is-monoid(m) implies forall([f in fun(nn, carr(m))], prod-ord(m, f, 1) = f(0)))
- `prod-ord-singleton-rev` — forall([m], is-monoid(m) implies forall([f in fun(nn, carr(m))], f(0) = prod-ord(m, f, 1)))
- `prod-ord-type` — forall([m], is-monoid(m) implies forall([f in fun(nn, carr(m)), n in nn], prod-ord(m, f, n) in carr(m)))

*Views into `monoid`.*

- `abelian-group-as-monoid` — from `abelian-group`: (carr opr iden) ↦ (carr opr iden)
- `ring-multiplicative-monoid` — from `ring`: (carr mul one) ↦ (carr opr iden)

### normed-ag
<a id="normed-ag"></a>

*Declared in* [`structure-library/normed-ag.scm`](../structure-library/normed-ag.scm).

*Kind.* Shape structure — `declare-structure` with slot clauses.

*Slots* (5): `carr` carrier; `opr` operation cartesian(carr, carr) -> carr; `iden` constant in carr; `inv` operation carr -> carr; `nrm` operation carr -> rr.

*Default arrows* (`is-hom-normed-ag-def`): forall([a, b, f], is-hom-normed-ag(a, b, f) iff is-normed-ag(a) and is-normed-ag(b) and f in fun(carr(a), carr(b)) and forall([x1_ in carr(a), x2_ in carr(a)], f((opr(a))(x1_, x2_)) = (opr(b))(f(x1_), f(x2_))) and f(iden(a)) = iden(b) and forall([x1_ in carr(a)], f((inv(a))(x1_)) = (inv(b))(f(x1_))) and forall([x1_ in carr(a)], (nrm(a))(x1_) = (nrm(b))(f(x1_))))

*Declaration.*

```scheme
(declare-structure normed-ag
  (carriers carr)
  (op opr (cartesian carr carr) carr)
  (constant iden carr)
  (op inv carr carr)
  (op nrm carr rr)
  (property is-associative opr carr)
  (property is-identity opr iden carr)
  (property has-inverses opr iden inv carr)
  (property is-commutative opr carr)
  (property is-group-norm nrm opr inv iden carr))
```

*Defining predicate* (as stored):

- `is-normed-ag` — forall([s], is-normed-ag(s) iff length(s) = 5 and carr(s) in set and opr(s) in fun(cartesian(carr(s), carr(s)), carr(s)) and iden(s) in carr(s) and inv(s) in fun(carr(s), carr(s)) and nrm(s) in fun(carr(s), rr) and is-associative(opr(s), carr(s)) and is-identity(opr(s), iden(s), carr(s)) and has-inverses(opr(s), iden(s), inv(s), carr(s)) and is-commutative(opr(s), carr(s)) and is-group-norm(nrm(s), opr(s), inv(s), iden(s), carr(s)))

*Theorems quantifying over `is-normed-ag`.*

- `abelian-group-as-monoid-is-monoid-normed-ag-as-abelian-group` — forall([r], is-normed-ag(r) implies is-monoid(abelian-group-as-monoid(normed-ag-as-abelian-group(r))))
- `hom-normed-ag-id` — forall([a], is-normed-ag(a) implies is-hom-normed-ag(a, a, id-fun(carr(a))))
- `nag-metric-carrier` — forall([nag], is-normed-ag(nag) implies pts(nag-metric-space(nag)) == carr(nag))
- `nag-metric-carrier-rev` — forall([nag], is-normed-ag(nag) implies carr(nag) == pts(nag-metric-space(nag)))
- `nag-metric-distance` — forall([nag], is-normed-ag(nag) implies forall([u in carr(nag), v in carr(nag)], (dist(nag-metric-space(nag)))(u, v) = (nrm(nag))((opr(nag))(u, (inv(nag))(v)))))
- `nag-metric-distance-rev` — forall([nag], is-normed-ag(nag) implies forall([u in carr(nag), v in carr(nag)], (nrm(nag))((opr(nag))(u, (inv(nag))(v))) = (dist(nag-metric-space(nag)))(u, v)))
- `nag-metric-space-is-metric-space` — forall([nag], is-normed-ag(nag) implies is-metric-space(nag-metric-space(nag)))
- `normed-ag-as-abelian-group-is-abelian-group` — forall([r], is-normed-ag(r) implies is-abelian-group(normed-ag-as-abelian-group(r)))
- `normed-ag-nrm-definite` — forall([s], is-normed-ag(s) implies forall([a in carr(s)], (nrm(s))(a) = 0 iff a = iden(s)))
- `normed-ag-nrm-definite-rev` — forall([s], is-normed-ag(s) implies forall([a in carr(s)], a = iden(s) iff (nrm(s))(a) = 0))
- `normed-ag-nrm-nonneg` — forall([s], is-normed-ag(s) implies forall([a in carr(s)], 0 <= (nrm(s))(a)))
- `normed-ag-nrm-subadditive` — forall([s], is-normed-ag(s) implies forall([a in carr(s), b in carr(s)], (nrm(s))((opr(s))(a, b)) <= (nrm(s))(a) + (nrm(s))(b)))
- `normed-ag-nrm-type` — forall([s], is-normed-ag(s) implies nrm(s) in fun(carr(s), rr))
- `rb2-dist-subset` — forall([grp], is-normed-ag(grp) implies forall([f, g, dd], forall([z_ in dd], f(z_) in carr(grp)) implies forall([z_ in dd], g(z_) = (nrm(grp))(f(z_))) implies forall([a_ in set], card(a_) in nn implies forall([b_ in set], card(b_) in nn implies a_ subset b_ implies b_ subset dd implies (dist(nag-metric-space(grp)))(finsum(normed-ag-as-abelian-group(grp), f, a_), finsum(normed-ag-as-abelian-group(grp), f, b_)) <= finsum(rr-pos-star-add-monoid, g, b_) - finsum(rr-pos-star-add-monoid, g, a_)))))
- `rb2-dist-union` — forall([grp], is-normed-ag(grp) implies forall([f, g, dd], forall([z_ in dd], f(z_) in carr(grp)) implies forall([z_ in dd], g(z_) = (nrm(grp))(f(z_))) implies forall([a_ in set], card(a_) in nn implies forall([c_ in set], card(c_) in nn implies forall([z_ in c_], not(z_ in a_)) implies a_ subset dd implies c_ subset dd implies finsum(rr-pos-star-add-monoid, g, union(a_, c_)) = finsum(rr-pos-star-add-monoid, g, a_) + finsum(rr-pos-star-add-monoid, g, c_) and (dist(nag-metric-space(grp)))(finsum(normed-ag-as-abelian-group(grp), f, a_), finsum(normed-ag-as-abelian-group(grp), f, union(a_, c_))) <= finsum(rr-pos-star-add-monoid, g, c_)))))
- `rb2-nrm-finsum-le` — forall([grp], is-normed-ag(grp) implies forall([f, g, dd], forall([z_ in dd], f(z_) in carr(grp)) implies forall([z_ in dd], g(z_) = (nrm(grp))(f(z_))) implies forall([w_ in set], card(w_) in nn implies w_ subset dd implies finsum(rr-pos-star-add-monoid, g, w_) in rr and (nrm(grp))(finsum(normed-ag-as-abelian-group(grp), f, w_)) <= finsum(rr-pos-star-add-monoid, g, w_))))
- `sums-to-unique` — forall([grp], is-normed-ag(grp) implies forall([f in fun(dom(f), carr(grp)), r1 in carr(grp), r2 in carr(grp)], sums-to(grp, f, r1) and sums-to(grp, f, r2) implies r1 = r2))
- `sums-to-unique-rev` — forall([grp], is-normed-ag(grp) implies forall([f in fun(dom(f), carr(grp)), r1 in carr(grp), r2 in carr(grp)], sums-to(grp, f, r1) and sums-to(grp, f, r2) implies r2 = r1))

*Views into `normed-ag`.*

- `normed-vector-space-as-normed-ag` — from `normed-vector-space`: (vec vadd vzero vneg vnrm) ↦ (carr opr iden inv nrm)

*Views from `normed-ag`.*

- `normed-ag-as-abelian-group` — into `abelian-group`: (carr opr iden inv) ↦ (carr opr iden inv)

### normed-field
<a id="normed-field"></a>

*Declared in* [`structure-library/normed-field.scm`](../structure-library/normed-field.scm).

*Kind.* Shape structure — `declare-structure` with slot clauses.

*Slots* (7): `carr` carrier; `add` operation cartesian(carr, carr) -> carr; `mul` operation cartesian(carr, carr) -> carr; `neg` operation carr -> carr; `zero` constant in carr; `one` constant in carr; `fnrm` operation carr -> rr.

*Default arrows* (`is-hom-normed-field-def`): forall([a, b, f], is-hom-normed-field(a, b, f) iff is-normed-field(a) and is-normed-field(b) and f in fun(carr(a), carr(b)) and forall([x1_ in carr(a), x2_ in carr(a)], f((add(a))(x1_, x2_)) = (add(b))(f(x1_), f(x2_))) and forall([x1_ in carr(a), x2_ in carr(a)], f((mul(a))(x1_, x2_)) = (mul(b))(f(x1_), f(x2_))) and forall([x1_ in carr(a)], f((neg(a))(x1_)) = (neg(b))(f(x1_))) and f(zero(a)) = zero(b) and f(one(a)) = one(b) and forall([x1_ in carr(a)], (fnrm(a))(x1_) = (fnrm(b))(f(x1_))))

*Declaration.*

```scheme
(declare-structure normed-field
  (instance-var s)
  (carriers carr)
  (op add (cartesian carr carr) carr)
  (op mul (cartesian carr carr) carr)
  (op neg carr carr)
  (constant zero carr)
  (constant one carr)
  (op fnrm carr rr)
  (property is-associative add carr)
  (property is-commutative add carr)
  (property is-identity add zero carr)
  (property has-inverses add zero neg carr)
  (property is-associative mul carr)
  (property is-commutative mul carr)
  (property is-identity mul one carr)
  (property is-distributive add mul carr)
  (property is-norm fnrm add mul zero carr)
  (law "not(zero(s) = one(s))")
  (law "forall([a_ in carr(s)],
          not(a_ = zero(s)) implies
            forsome([b_ in carr(s)], not(b_ = zero(s)) and mul(s)(a_, b_) = one(s)))"))
```

*Defining predicate* (as stored):

- `is-normed-field` — forall([s], is-normed-field(s) iff length(s) = 7 and carr(s) in set and add(s) in fun(cartesian(carr(s), carr(s)), carr(s)) and mul(s) in fun(cartesian(carr(s), carr(s)), carr(s)) and neg(s) in fun(carr(s), carr(s)) and zero(s) in carr(s) and one(s) in carr(s) and fnrm(s) in fun(carr(s), rr) and is-associative(add(s), carr(s)) and is-commutative(add(s), carr(s)) and is-identity(add(s), zero(s), carr(s)) and has-inverses(add(s), zero(s), neg(s), carr(s)) and is-associative(mul(s), carr(s)) and is-commutative(mul(s), carr(s)) and is-identity(mul(s), one(s), carr(s)) and is-distributive(add(s), mul(s), carr(s)) and is-norm(fnrm(s), add(s), mul(s), zero(s), carr(s)) and not(zero(s) = one(s)) and forall([a_ in carr(s)], not(a_ = zero(s)) implies forsome([b_ in carr(s)], not(b_ = zero(s)) and (mul(s))(a_, b_) = one(s))))

*Theorems quantifying over `is-normed-field`.*

- `abelian-group-as-monoid-is-monoid-commutative-ring-additive-ag-normed-field-as-commutative-ring` — forall([r], is-normed-field(r) implies is-monoid(abelian-group-as-monoid(commutative-ring-additive-ag(normed-field-as-commutative-ring(r)))))
- `abelian-group-as-monoid-is-monoid-normed-field-additive-ag` — forall([r], is-normed-field(r) implies is-monoid(abelian-group-as-monoid(normed-field-additive-ag(r))))
- `commutative-ring-additive-ag-is-abelian-group-normed-field-as-commutative-ring` — forall([r], is-normed-field(r) implies is-abelian-group(commutative-ring-additive-ag(normed-field-as-commutative-ring(r))))
- `commutative-ring-multiplicative-cm-is-comm-monoid-normed-field-as-commutative-ring` — forall([r], is-normed-field(r) implies is-comm-monoid(commutative-ring-multiplicative-cm(normed-field-as-commutative-ring(r))))
- `deriv-on-of-is-diff-on` — forall([drok_], is-normed-field(drok_) implies forall([d2e_], pos-rr(d2e_) implies forsome([d2h_ in carr(drok_)], not(d2h_ = zero(drok_)) and (fnrm(drok_))(d2h_) < d2e_)) implies forall([drou_, drof_, droa_, drol_], is-diff-on(drok_, drou_, drof_, droa_, drol_) implies deriv-on(drok_, drou_, drof_, droa_) = drol_))
- `deriv-on-of-is-diff-on-rev` — forall([drok_], is-normed-field(drok_) implies forall([d2e_], pos-rr(d2e_) implies forsome([d2h_ in carr(drok_)], not(d2h_ = zero(drok_)) and (fnrm(drok_))(d2h_) < d2e_)) implies forall([drou_, drof_, droa_, drol_], is-diff-on(drok_, drou_, drof_, droa_, drol_) implies drol_ = deriv-on(drok_, drou_, drof_, droa_)))
- `diff-on-chain` — forall([k], is-normed-field(k) implies forall([u, d2s_, d2f_, d2g_, d2a_, d2l_, d2m_], is-diff-on(k, u, d2f_, d2a_, d2l_) implies is-diff-on(k, d2s_, d2g_, d2f_(d2a_), d2m_) implies forall([d2y_ in u], d2f_(d2y_) in d2s_) implies is-diff-on(k, u, vnb-lambda(d2x_, u, d2g_(d2f_(d2x_))), d2a_, (mul(k))(d2m_, d2l_))))
- `diff-on-const` — forall([k], is-normed-field(k) implies forall([u], is-open(nf-metric-space(k), u) implies forall([d2c_ in carr(k), d2a_ in u], is-diff-on(k, u, vnb-lambda(d2x_, u, d2c_), d2a_, zero(k)))))
- `diff-on-identity` — forall([k], is-normed-field(k) implies forall([u], is-open(nf-metric-space(k), u) implies forall([d2a_ in u], is-diff-on(k, u, vnb-lambda(d2x_, u, d2x_), d2a_, one(k)))))
- `diff-on-product` — forall([k], is-normed-field(k) implies forall([u, d2f_, d2g_, d2a_, d2l_, d2m_], is-diff-on(k, u, d2f_, d2a_, d2l_) implies is-diff-on(k, u, d2g_, d2a_, d2m_) implies is-diff-on(k, u, vnb-lambda(d2x_, u, (mul(k))(d2f_(d2x_), d2g_(d2x_))), d2a_, (add(k))((mul(k))(d2l_, d2g_(d2a_)), (mul(k))(d2m_, d2f_(d2a_))))))
- `diff-on-sum` — forall([k], is-normed-field(k) implies forall([u, d2f_, d2g_, d2a_, d2l_, d2m_], is-diff-on(k, u, d2f_, d2a_, d2l_) implies is-diff-on(k, u, d2g_, d2a_, d2m_) implies is-diff-on(k, u, vnb-lambda(d2x_, u, (add(k))(d2f_(d2x_), d2g_(d2x_))), d2a_, (add(k))(d2l_, d2m_))))
- `diff-on-unique` — forall([k], is-normed-field(k) implies forall([d2e_], pos-rr(d2e_) implies forsome([d2h_ in carr(k)], not(d2h_ = zero(k)) and (fnrm(k))(d2h_) < d2e_)) implies forall([u, d2f_, d2a_, d2l_, d2m_], is-diff-on(k, u, d2f_, d2a_, d2l_) implies is-diff-on(k, u, d2f_, d2a_, d2m_) implies d2l_ = d2m_))
- `diff-on-unique-rev` — forall([k], is-normed-field(k) implies forall([d2e_], pos-rr(d2e_) implies forsome([d2h_ in carr(k)], not(d2h_ = zero(k)) and (fnrm(k))(d2h_) < d2e_)) implies forall([u, d2f_, d2a_, d2l_, d2m_], is-diff-on(k, u, d2f_, d2a_, d2l_) implies is-diff-on(k, u, d2f_, d2a_, d2m_) implies d2m_ = d2l_))
- `frm-nf-bracket` — forall([fld], is-normed-field(fld) implies forall([c in carr(fld), b in carr(fld)], 1 < (fnrm(fld))(c) implies (mul(fld))(c, b) = one(fld) implies forall([u in rr, v in rr], 0 < u implies 0 < v implies forsome([fbs_ in carr(fld)], (fnrm(fld))(fbs_) * v < u and u <= (fnrm(fld))(c) * ((fnrm(fld))(fbs_) * v)))))
- `frm-nf-norm-one` — forall([fld], is-normed-field(fld) implies forall([c in carr(fld)], 0 < (fnrm(fld))(c) implies (fnrm(fld))(one(fld)) = 1))
- `frm-nf-norm-one-rev` — forall([fld], is-normed-field(fld) implies forall([c in carr(fld)], 0 < (fnrm(fld))(c) implies 1 = (fnrm(fld))(one(fld))))
- `hom-normed-field-id` — forall([a], is-normed-field(a) implies is-hom-normed-field(a, a, id-fun(carr(a))))
- `nf-add-in-carr` — forall([k], is-normed-field(k) implies forall([u_, v_], u_ in carr(k) implies v_ in carr(k) implies (add(k))(u_, v_) in carr(k)))
- `nf-lam-comp-z-in-fun` — forall([k], is-normed-field(k) implies forall([u in set, d2s_, d2f_, d2g_ in fun(d2s_, carr(k))], forall([d2y_ in u], d2f_(d2y_) in d2s_) implies vnb-lambda(msz_, u, d2g_(d2f_(msz_))) in fun(u, carr(k))))
- `nf-lam-const-in-fun` — forall([k], is-normed-field(k) implies forall([u in set, d2c_ in carr(k)], vnb-lambda(d2x_, u, d2c_) in fun(u, carr(k))))

### normed-vector-space
<a id="normed-vector-space"></a>

*Declared in* [`structure-library/normed-vector-space.scm`](../structure-library/normed-vector-space.scm).

*Kind.* Shape structure — `declare-structure` with slot clauses.

*Slots* (7): `scal` substructure, a ring; `vec` carrier; `vadd` operation cartesian(vec, vec) -> vec; `vzero` constant in vec; `vneg` operation vec -> vec; `act` operation cartesian(carr(scal), vec) -> vec; `vnrm` operation vec -> rr.

*Default arrows* (`is-hom-normed-vector-space-def`): forall([a, b, f], is-hom-normed-vector-space(a, b, f) iff is-normed-vector-space(a) and is-normed-vector-space(b) and f in fun(vec(a), vec(b)) and scal(a) = scal(b) and forall([x1_ in vec(a), x2_ in vec(a)], f((vadd(a))(x1_, x2_)) = (vadd(b))(f(x1_), f(x2_))) and f(vzero(a)) = vzero(b) and forall([x1_ in vec(a)], f((vneg(a))(x1_)) = (vneg(b))(f(x1_))) and forall([x1_ in carr(scal(a)), x2_ in vec(a)], f((act(a))(x1_, x2_)) = (act(b))(x1_, f(x2_))) and forall([x1_ in vec(a)], (vnrm(a))(x1_) = (vnrm(b))(f(x1_))))

*Declaration.*

```scheme
(declare-structure normed-vector-space
  (instance-var s)
  (substructure scal ring)
  (carriers vec)
  (op vadd (cartesian vec vec) vec)
  (constant vzero vec)
  (op vneg vec vec)
  (op act (cartesian (carr scal) vec) vec)
  (op vnrm vec rr)
  (property is-associative vadd vec)
  (property is-commutative vadd vec)
  (property is-identity vadd vzero vec)
  (property has-inverses vadd vzero vneg vec)
  (law "scal(s) = normed-field-as-commutative-ring(rr-normed-field)")
  (law "forall([r_ in carr(scal(s)), x_ in vec(s), y_ in vec(s)],
          act(s)(r_, vadd(s)(x_, y_)) = vadd(s)(act(s)(r_, x_), act(s)(r_, y_)))")
  (law "forall([r_ in carr(scal(s)), s_ in carr(scal(s)), x_ in vec(s)],
          act(s)(add(scal(s))(r_, s_), x_) = vadd(s)(act(s)(r_, x_), act(s)(s_, x_)))")
  (law "forall([r_ in carr(scal(s)), s_ in carr(scal(s)), x_ in vec(s)],
          act(s)(mul(scal(s))(r_, s_), x_) = act(s)(r_, act(s)(s_, x_)))")
  (law "forall([x_ in vec(s)], act(s)(one(scal(s)), x_) = x_)")
  (law "forall([x_ in vec(s)], 0 <= vnrm(s)(x_))")
  (law "forall([x_ in vec(s)], (vnrm(s)(x_) = 0) iff (x_ = vzero(s)))")
  (law "forall([r_ in carr(scal(s)), x_ in vec(s)],
          vnrm(s)(act(s)(r_, x_)) = abs(r_) * vnrm(s)(x_))")
  (law "forall([x_ in vec(s), y_ in vec(s)],
          vnrm(s)(vadd(s)(x_, y_)) <= vnrm(s)(x_) + vnrm(s)(y_))"))
```

- `is-normed-vector-space` — forall([s], is-normed-vector-space(s) iff length(s) = 7 and is-ring(scal(s)) and vec(s) in set and vadd(s) in fun(cartesian(vec(s), vec(s)), vec(s)) and vzero(s) in vec(s) and vneg(s) in fun(vec(s), vec(s)) and act(s) in fun(cartesian(carr(scal(s)), vec(s)), vec(s)) and vnrm(s) in fun(vec(s), rr) and is-associative(vadd(s), vec(s)) and is-commutative(vadd(s), vec(s)) and is-identity(vadd(s), vzero(s), vec(s)) and has-inverses(vadd(s), vzero(s), vneg(s), vec(s)) and scal(s) = normed-field-as-commutative-ring(rr-normed-field) and forall([r_ in carr(scal(s)), x_ in vec(s), y_ in vec(s)], (act(s))(r_, (vadd(s))(x_, y_)) = (vadd(s))((act(s))(r_, x_), (act(s))(r_, y_))) and forall([r_ in carr(scal(s)), s_ in carr(scal(s)), x_ in vec(s)], (act(s))((add(scal(s)))(r_, s_), x_) = (vadd(s))((act(s))(r_, x_), (act(s))(s_, x_))) and forall([r_ in carr(scal(s)), s_ in carr(scal(s)), x_ in vec(s)], (act(s))((mul(scal(s)))(r_, s_), x_) = (act(s))(r_, (act(s))(s_, x_))) and forall([x_ in vec(s)], (act(s))(one(scal(s)), x_) = x_) and forall([x_ in vec(s)], 0 <= (vnrm(s))(x_)) and forall([x_ in vec(s)], (vnrm(s))(x_) = 0 iff x_ = vzero(s)) and forall([r_ in carr(scal(s)), x_ in vec(s)], (vnrm(s))((act(s))(r_, x_)) = abs(r_) * (vnrm(s))(x_)) and forall([x_ in vec(s), y_ in vec(s)], (vnrm(s))((vadd(s))(x_, y_)) <= (vnrm(s))(x_) + (vnrm(s))(y_)))

- `nvs-act-collect-rev` — forall([m], is-normed-vector-space(m) implies forall([r_ in rr, s_ in rr, x_ in vec(m), y_ in vec(m)], (vadd(m))(y_, (act(m))(r_ + s_, x_)) = (vadd(m))((vadd(m))(y_, (act(m))(r_, x_)), (act(m))(s_, x_))))

- `nvs-act-in-vec` — forall([m], is-normed-vector-space(m) implies forall([r_ in rr, x_ in vec(m)], (act(m))(r_, x_) in vec(m)))
- `nvs-act-mul-compat` — forall([m], is-normed-vector-space(m) implies forall([r_ in carr(scal(m)), s_ in carr(scal(m)), x_ in vec(m)], (act(m))((mul(scal(m)))(r_, s_), x_) = (act(m))(r_, (act(m))(s_, x_))))

- `nvs-act-one` — forall([m], is-normed-vector-space(m) implies forall([x_ in vec(m), y_ in vec(m)], (vadd(m))(y_, (act(m))(1, x_)) = (vadd(m))(y_, x_)))
- `nvs-act-one-rev` — forall([m], is-normed-vector-space(m) implies forall([x_ in vec(m), y_ in vec(m)], (vadd(m))(y_, x_) = (vadd(m))(y_, (act(m))(1, x_))))

- `nvs-act-zero` — forall([m], is-normed-vector-space(m) implies forall([x_ in vec(m), y_ in vec(m)], (vadd(m))(y_, (act(m))(0, x_)) = y_))

- `nvs-vadd-cancel` — forall([m], is-normed-vector-space(m) implies forall([x_ in vec(m), z_ in vec(m), w_ in vec(m)], (vadd(m))(x_, z_) = (vadd(m))(x_, w_) implies z_ = w_))

- `nvs-vadd-cancel-right-rev` — forall([m], is-normed-vector-space(m) implies forall([x_ in vec(m), z_ in vec(m), w_ in vec(m)], (vadd(m))(z_, x_) = (vadd(m))(w_, x_) implies w_ = z_))
- `nvs-vadd-cancel-zero` — forall([m], is-normed-vector-space(m) implies forall([x_ in vec(m), z_ in vec(m)], (vadd(m))(x_, z_) = x_ implies z_ = vzero(m)))
- `nvs-vadd-cancel-zero-rev` — forall([m], is-normed-vector-space(m) implies forall([x_ in vec(m), z_ in vec(m)], (vadd(m))(x_, z_) = x_ implies vzero(m) = z_))

- `nvs-vadd-in-vec` — forall([m], is-normed-vector-space(m) implies forall([x_ in vec(m), y_ in vec(m)], (vadd(m))(x_, y_) in vec(m)))
- `nvs-vadd-shuffle` — forall([m], is-normed-vector-space(m) implies forall([a_ in vec(m), b_ in vec(m), c_ in vec(m), d_ in vec(m)], (vadd(m))((vadd(m))(a_, c_), (vadd(m))(b_, d_)) = (vadd(m))((vadd(m))(a_, b_), (vadd(m))(c_, d_))))

- `nvs-vneg-left` — forall([m], is-normed-vector-space(m) implies forall([u_ in vec(m)], (vadd(m))((vneg(m))(u_), u_) = vzero(m)))
- `nvs-vneg-left-rev` — forall([m], is-normed-vector-space(m) implies forall([u_ in vec(m)], vzero(m) = (vadd(m))((vneg(m))(u_), u_)))
- `nvs-vneg-right` — forall([m], is-normed-vector-space(m) implies forall([u_ in vec(m)], (vadd(m))(u_, (vneg(m))(u_)) = vzero(m)))
- `nvs-vneg-right-rev` — forall([m], is-normed-vector-space(m) implies forall([u_ in vec(m)], vzero(m) = (vadd(m))(u_, (vneg(m))(u_))))
- `nvs-vneg-vadd` — forall([m], is-normed-vector-space(m) implies forall([x_ in vec(m), y_ in vec(m)], (vneg(m))((vadd(m))(x_, y_)) = (vadd(m))((vneg(m))(x_), (vneg(m))(y_))))

- `nvs-vnrm-homog` — forall([m], is-normed-vector-space(m) implies forall([r_ in rr, x_ in vec(m)], (vnrm(m))((act(m))(r_, x_)) = abs(r_) * (vnrm(m))(x_)))
- `nvs-vnrm-homog-c` — forall([m], is-normed-vector-space(m) implies forall([r_ in carr(scal(m)), x_ in vec(m)], (vnrm(m))((act(m))(r_, x_)) = abs(r_) * (vnrm(m))(x_)))
- `nvs-vnrm-homog-c-rev` — forall([m], is-normed-vector-space(m) implies forall([r_ in carr(scal(m)), x_ in vec(m)], abs(r_) * (vnrm(m))(x_) = (vnrm(m))((act(m))(r_, x_))))
- `nvs-vnrm-homog-rev` — forall([m], is-normed-vector-space(m) implies forall([r_ in rr, x_ in vec(m)], abs(r_) * (vnrm(m))(x_) = (vnrm(m))((act(m))(r_, x_))))
- `nvs-vnrm-triangle` — forall([m], is-normed-vector-space(m) implies forall([x_ in vec(m), y_ in vec(m)], (vnrm(m))((vadd(m))(x_, y_)) <= (vnrm(m))(x_) + (vnrm(m))(y_)))
- `nvs-vnrm-vzero` — forall([m], is-normed-vector-space(m) implies (vnrm(m))(vzero(m)) = 0)
- `nvs-vnrm-vzero-rev` — forall([m], is-normed-vector-space(m) implies 0 = (vnrm(m))(vzero(m)))

- `nvs-vzero-left` — forall([m], is-normed-vector-space(m) implies forall([u_ in vec(m)], (vadd(m))(vzero(m), u_) = u_))
- `nvs-vzero-left-rev` — forall([m], is-normed-vector-space(m) implies forall([u_ in vec(m)], u_ = (vadd(m))(vzero(m), u_)))

- `nvs-vzero-right-rev` — forall([m], is-normed-vector-space(m) implies forall([u_ in vec(m)], u_ = (vadd(m))(u_, vzero(m))))
- `nvs-zero-act` — forall([m], is-normed-vector-space(m) implies forall([x_ in vec(m)], (act(m))(0, x_) = vzero(m)))
- `nvs-zero-act-rev` — forall([m], is-normed-vector-space(m) implies forall([x_ in vec(m)], vzero(m) = (act(m))(0, x_)))
- `seg-curve-diff-at` — forall([m], is-normed-vector-space(m) implies forall([f in fun(vec(m), rr), b_ in vec(m), eta in vec(m), s_ in rr, k_], is-dir-diff-at(m, f, (vadd(m))(b_, (act(m))(s_, eta)), eta, k_) implies is-diff-at(seg-curve(m, f, b_, eta), s_, k_)))
- `submodule-nvs-module-view` — forall([m_], is-normed-vector-space(m_) implies forall([t_], is-submodule(m_, t_) implies is-submodule(normed-vector-space-as-module(m_), t_)))
- `vec-is-set` — forall([m], is-normed-vector-space(m) implies vec(m) in set)

*Views from `normed-vector-space`.*

- `normed-vector-space-as-normed-ag` — into `normed-ag`: (vec vadd vzero vneg vnrm) ↦ (carr opr iden inv nrm)
- `normed-vector-space-as-module` — into `module`: (scal vec vadd vzero vneg act) ↦ (scal vec vadd vzero vneg act)

### pid
<a id="pid"></a>

*Declared in* [`structure-library/ideal.scm`](../structure-library/ideal.scm).

*Kind.* Refinement — `declare-structure` with `(same-shape-as integral-domain)`; its `IS-X <=> IS-PARENT and <laws>` axiom is generated, not hand-written.  Subtype of [`integral-domain`](#integral-domain).  Shape inherited from `integral-domain` — slots (carr add mul neg zero one).

*Declaration.*

```scheme
(declare-structure pid
  (instance-var s)
  (same-shape-as integral-domain)
  (law "forall([i], is-ideal(s, i) implies
          forsome([a in carr(s)], i = principal-ideal(s, a)))"))
```

*Defining predicate* (as stored):

- `is-pid-def` — forall([s], is-pid(s) iff is-integral-domain(s) and forall([i], is-ideal(s, i) implies forsome([a in carr(s)], i = principal-ideal(s, a))))

*Theorems quantifying over `is-pid`.*

- `hom-pid-id` — forall([a], is-pid(a) implies is-hom-pid(a, a, id-fun(carr(a))))

### pk-space
<a id="pk-space"></a>

*Declared in* [`structure-library/nsl.scm`](../structure-library/nsl.scm).

*Kind.* Shape structure — `declare-structure` with slot clauses.

*Slots* (3): `pk-inf` substructure, a infiltrated-space; `names` carrier; `pubkey` operation names -> keys(ispace(pk-inf)).

*Default arrows* (`is-hom-pk-space-def`): forall([a, b, f], is-hom-pk-space(a, b, f) iff is-pk-space(a) and is-pk-space(b) and f in fun(names(a), names(b)) and pk-inf(a) = pk-inf(b) and forall([x1_ in names(a)], (pubkey(a))(x1_) = (pubkey(b))(f(x1_))))

*Declaration.*

```scheme
(declare-structure pk-space
  (instance-var s)
  (substructure pk-inf infiltrated-space)
  (carriers names)
  (op pubkey names (keys (ispace pk-inf)))
  (law (forall nlx_ (implies (in nlx_ (names s)) (in nlx_ (tokens (ispace (pk-inf s)))))))
  (law (forall nlx_ (implies (in nlx_ (names s)) (not (in nlx_ (keys (ispace (pk-inf s))))))))
  (law (forall nlx_ (forall nly_ (implies (in nlx_ (names s)) (implies (in nly_ (names s)) (implies (= ((pubkey s) nlx_) ((pubkey s) nly_)) (= nlx_ nly_))))))))
```

*Defining predicate* (as stored):

- `is-pk-space` — forall([s], is-pk-space(s) iff length(s) = 3 and is-infiltrated-space(pk-inf(s)) and names(s) in set and pubkey(s) in fun(names(s), keys(ispace(pk-inf(s)))) and forall([nlx_ in names(s)], nlx_ in tokens(ispace(pk-inf(s)))) and forall([nlx_ in names(s)], not(nlx_ in keys(ispace(pk-inf(s))))) and forall([nlx_, nly_], nlx_ in names(s) implies nly_ in names(s) implies (pubkey(s))(nlx_) = (pubkey(s))(nly_) implies nlx_ = nly_))

*Theorems quantifying over `is-pk-space`.*

- `pk-space-parts` — forall([nsp], is-pk-space(nsp) implies is-infiltrated-space(pk-inf(nsp)) and is-strand-space(ispace(pk-inf(nsp))) and names(nsp) in set and pubkey(nsp) in fun(names(nsp), keys(ispace(pk-inf(nsp)))) and forall([x in names(nsp)], x in tokens(ispace(pk-inf(nsp)))) and forall([x in names(nsp)], not(x in keys(ispace(pk-inf(nsp))))) and forall([x, y], x in names(nsp) implies y in names(nsp) implies (pubkey(nsp))(x) = (pubkey(nsp))(y) implies x = y))

### pre-hilbert-space
<a id="pre-hilbert-space"></a>

*Declared in* [`structure-library/sip-vocab.scm`](../structure-library/sip-vocab.scm).

*Kind.* Refinement — `declare-structure` with `(same-shape-as semi-inner-product-space)`; its `IS-X <=> IS-PARENT and <laws>` axiom is generated, not hand-written.  Subtype of [`semi-inner-product-space`](#semi-inner-product-space).  Shape inherited from `semi-inner-product-space` — slots (scal vec vadd vzero vneg act rip).

*Declaration.*

```scheme
(declare-structure pre-hilbert-space
  (instance-var s)
  (same-shape-as semi-inner-product-space)
  (law "forall([x_ in vec(s)], rip(s)(x_, x_) = 0 implies x_ = vzero(s))"))
```

*Defining predicate* (as stored):

- `is-pre-hilbert-space-def` — forall([s], is-pre-hilbert-space(s) iff is-semi-inner-product-space(s) and forall([x_ in vec(s)], (rip(s))(x_, x_) = 0 implies x_ = vzero(s)))

*Theorems quantifying over `is-pre-hilbert-space`.*

- `complexify-closed` — forall([v], is-pre-hilbert-space(v) implies forall([kk], is-closed(sip-metric-space(v), kk) implies is-closed(ip-metric-space(csip-cips(complexify(v))), cartesian(kk, kk))))
- `complexify-closure-comp1` — forall([v], is-pre-hilbert-space(v) implies forall([kk], kk subset vec(v) implies forall([z_ in closure(ip-metric-space(csip-cips(complexify(v))), cartesian(kk, kk))], nth(1, z_) in closure(sip-metric-space(v), kk))))
- `complexify-closure-comp2` — forall([v], is-pre-hilbert-space(v) implies forall([kk], kk subset vec(v) implies forall([z_ in closure(ip-metric-space(csip-cips(complexify(v))), cartesian(kk, kk))], nth(2, z_) in closure(sip-metric-space(v), kk))))
- `complexify-is-cphs` — forall([v], is-pre-hilbert-space(v) implies is-complex-pre-hilbert-space(complexify(v)))
- `complexify-submodule` — forall([v], is-pre-hilbert-space(v) implies forall([kk], is-submodule(v, kk) implies is-submodule(csip-cips(complexify(v)), cartesian(kk, kk))))
- `cxw-convex-midpoint-closed` — forall([v], is-pre-hilbert-space(v) implies forall([cs], is-convex-subset(v, cs) implies forall([hm1_ in cartesian(cs, cs), hm2_ in cartesian(cs, cs)], (act(csip-cips(complexify(v))))(num(1/2), (vadd(csip-cips(complexify(v))))(hm1_, hm2_)) in cartesian(cs, cs))))
- `cxw-dist` — forall([v], is-pre-hilbert-space(v) implies forall([px_, qx_], px_ in cartesian(vec(v), vec(v)) implies qx_ in cartesian(vec(v), vec(v)) implies (dist(ip-metric-space(csip-cips(complexify(v)))))(px_, qx_) = sqrt((rip(v))((vadd(v))(nth(1, px_), (vneg(v))(nth(1, qx_))), (vadd(v))(nth(1, px_), (vneg(v))(nth(1, qx_)))) + (rip(v))((vadd(v))(nth(2, px_), (vneg(v))(nth(2, qx_))), (vadd(v))(nth(2, px_), (vneg(v))(nth(2, qx_)))))))
- `cxw-dist-comp1` — forall([v], is-pre-hilbert-space(v) implies forall([px_, qx_], px_ in cartesian(vec(v), vec(v)) implies qx_ in cartesian(vec(v), vec(v)) implies (dist(sip-metric-space(v)))(nth(1, px_), nth(1, qx_)) <= (dist(ip-metric-space(csip-cips(complexify(v)))))(px_, qx_)))
- `cxw-dist-comp2` — forall([v], is-pre-hilbert-space(v) implies forall([px_, qx_], px_ in cartesian(vec(v), vec(v)) implies qx_ in cartesian(vec(v), vec(v)) implies (dist(sip-metric-space(v)))(nth(2, px_), nth(2, qx_)) <= (dist(ip-metric-space(csip-cips(complexify(v)))))(px_, qx_)))
- `cxw-dist-le-sum` — forall([v], is-pre-hilbert-space(v) implies forall([px_, qx_], px_ in cartesian(vec(v), vec(v)) implies qx_ in cartesian(vec(v), vec(v)) implies (dist(ip-metric-space(csip-cips(complexify(v)))))(px_, qx_) <= (dist(sip-metric-space(v)))(nth(1, px_), nth(1, qx_)) + (dist(sip-metric-space(v)))(nth(2, px_), nth(2, qx_))))
- `cxw-dist-rev` — forall([v], is-pre-hilbert-space(v) implies forall([px_, qx_], px_ in cartesian(vec(v), vec(v)) implies qx_ in cartesian(vec(v), vec(v)) implies sqrt((rip(v))((vadd(v))(nth(1, px_), (vneg(v))(nth(1, qx_))), (vadd(v))(nth(1, px_), (vneg(v))(nth(1, qx_)))) + (rip(v))((vadd(v))(nth(2, px_), (vneg(v))(nth(2, qx_))), (vadd(v))(nth(2, px_), (vneg(v))(nth(2, qx_))))) = (dist(ip-metric-space(csip-cips(complexify(v)))))(px_, qx_)))
- `cxw-dist-sq` — forall([v], is-pre-hilbert-space(v) implies forall([px_, qx_], px_ in cartesian(vec(v), vec(v)) implies qx_ in cartesian(vec(v), vec(v)) implies (ip(csip-cips(complexify(v))))((vadd(csip-cips(complexify(v))))(px_, (vneg(csip-cips(complexify(v))))(qx_)), (vadd(csip-cips(complexify(v))))(px_, (vneg(csip-cips(complexify(v))))(qx_))) = (rip(v))((vadd(v))(nth(1, px_), (vneg(v))(nth(1, qx_))), (vadd(v))(nth(1, px_), (vneg(v))(nth(1, qx_)))) + (rip(v))((vadd(v))(nth(2, px_), (vneg(v))(nth(2, qx_))), (vadd(v))(nth(2, px_), (vneg(v))(nth(2, qx_))))))
- `cxw-dist-sq-rev` — forall([v], is-pre-hilbert-space(v) implies forall([px_, qx_], px_ in cartesian(vec(v), vec(v)) implies qx_ in cartesian(vec(v), vec(v)) implies (rip(v))((vadd(v))(nth(1, px_), (vneg(v))(nth(1, qx_))), (vadd(v))(nth(1, px_), (vneg(v))(nth(1, qx_)))) + (rip(v))((vadd(v))(nth(2, px_), (vneg(v))(nth(2, qx_))), (vadd(v))(nth(2, px_), (vneg(v))(nth(2, qx_)))) = (ip(csip-cips(complexify(v))))((vadd(csip-cips(complexify(v))))(px_, (vneg(csip-cips(complexify(v))))(qx_)), (vadd(csip-cips(complexify(v))))(px_, (vneg(csip-cips(complexify(v))))(qx_)))))
- `cxw-is-cips` — forall([v], is-pre-hilbert-space(v) implies is-complex-inner-product-space(csip-cips(complexify(v))))
- `phs-definite` — forall([v], is-pre-hilbert-space(v) implies forall([x_ in vec(v)], (rip(v))(x_, x_) = 0 implies x_ = vzero(v)))
- `phs-definite-rev` — forall([v], is-pre-hilbert-space(v) implies forall([x_ in vec(v)], (rip(v))(x_, x_) = 0 implies vzero(v) = x_))
- `phs-is-sip` — forall([v], is-pre-hilbert-space(v) implies is-semi-inner-product-space(v))
- `phs-square-of-antisym-isometric` — forall([v], is-pre-hilbert-space(v) implies forall([jo in fun(vec(v), vec(v))], is-antisymmetric-op(v, jo) implies is-isometric-op(v, jo) implies is-square-minus-id(v, jo)))
- `sip-metric-space-dist` — forall([v], is-pre-hilbert-space(v) implies forall([x_ in vec(v), y_ in vec(v)], (dist(sip-metric-space(v)))(x_, y_) = sqrt((rip(v))((vadd(v))(x_, (vneg(v))(y_)), (vadd(v))(x_, (vneg(v))(y_))))))
- `sip-metric-space-dist-rev` — forall([v], is-pre-hilbert-space(v) implies forall([x_ in vec(v), y_ in vec(v)], sqrt((rip(v))((vadd(v))(x_, (vneg(v))(y_)), (vadd(v))(x_, (vneg(v))(y_)))) = (dist(sip-metric-space(v)))(x_, y_)))
- `sip-metric-space-is-metric-space` — forall([v], is-pre-hilbert-space(v) implies is-metric-space(sip-metric-space(v)))
- `sip-metric-space-pts` — forall([v], is-pre-hilbert-space(v) implies pts(sip-metric-space(v)) == vec(v))
- `sip-metric-space-pts-rev` — forall([v], is-pre-hilbert-space(v) implies vec(v) == pts(sip-metric-space(v)))
- `sip-normed-ag-is-normed-ag` — forall([v], is-pre-hilbert-space(v) implies is-normed-ag(sip-normed-ag(v)))

### pseudometric-space
<a id="pseudometric-space"></a>

*Declared in* [`structure-library/pseudometric.scm`](../structure-library/pseudometric.scm).

*Kind.* Shape structure — `declare-structure` with slot clauses.

*Slots* (2): `pts` carrier; `dist` operation cartesian(pts, pts) -> rr.

*Default arrows* (`is-hom-pseudometric-space-def`): forall([a, b, f], is-hom-pseudometric-space(a, b, f) iff is-pseudometric-space(a) and is-pseudometric-space(b) and f in fun(pts(a), pts(b)) and forall([x1_ in pts(a), x2_ in pts(a)], (dist(a))(x1_, x2_) = (dist(b))(f(x1_), f(x2_))))

*Declaration.*

```scheme
(declare-structure pseudometric-space
  (carriers pts)
  (op dist (cartesian pts pts) rr)
  (property is-pseudometric dist pts))
```

*Defining predicate* (as stored):

- `is-pseudometric-space` — forall([s], is-pseudometric-space(s) iff length(s) = 2 and pts(s) in set and dist(s) in fun(cartesian(pts(s), pts(s)), rr) and is-pseudometric(dist(s), pts(s)))

*Theorems quantifying over `is-pseudometric-space`.*

- `hom-pseudometric-space-id` — forall([a], is-pseudometric-space(a) implies is-hom-pseudometric-space(a, a, id-fun(pts(a))))

### ring
<a id="ring"></a>

*Declared in* [`structure-library/ring.scm`](../structure-library/ring.scm).

*Kind.* Shape structure — `declare-structure` with slot clauses.

*Slots* (6): `carr` carrier; `add` operation cartesian(carr, carr) -> carr; `mul` operation cartesian(carr, carr) -> carr; `neg` operation carr -> carr; `zero` constant in carr; `one` constant in carr.

*Default arrows* (`is-hom-ring-def`): forall([a, b, f], is-hom-ring(a, b, f) iff is-ring(a) and is-ring(b) and f in fun(carr(a), carr(b)) and forall([x1_ in carr(a), x2_ in carr(a)], f((add(a))(x1_, x2_)) = (add(b))(f(x1_), f(x2_))) and forall([x1_ in carr(a), x2_ in carr(a)], f((mul(a))(x1_, x2_)) = (mul(b))(f(x1_), f(x2_))) and forall([x1_ in carr(a)], f((neg(a))(x1_)) = (neg(b))(f(x1_))) and f(zero(a)) = zero(b) and f(one(a)) = one(b))

*Declaration.*

```scheme
(declare-structure ring
  (carriers carr)
  (op add (cartesian carr carr) carr)
  (op mul (cartesian carr carr) carr)
  (op neg carr carr)
  (constant zero carr)
  (constant one carr)
  (property is-associative add carr)
  (property is-commutative add carr)
  (property is-identity add zero carr)
  (property has-inverses add zero neg carr)
  (property is-associative mul carr)
  (property is-identity mul one carr)
  (property is-distributive add mul carr))
```

*Defining predicate* (as stored):

- `is-ring` — forall([s], is-ring(s) iff length(s) = 6 and carr(s) in set and add(s) in fun(cartesian(carr(s), carr(s)), carr(s)) and mul(s) in fun(cartesian(carr(s), carr(s)), carr(s)) and neg(s) in fun(carr(s), carr(s)) and zero(s) in carr(s) and one(s) in carr(s) and is-associative(add(s), carr(s)) and is-commutative(add(s), carr(s)) and is-identity(add(s), zero(s), carr(s)) and has-inverses(add(s), zero(s), neg(s), carr(s)) and is-associative(mul(s), carr(s)) and is-identity(mul(s), one(s), carr(s)) and is-distributive(add(s), mul(s), carr(s)))

- `abelian-group-idempotent-is-id-rev-ring-additive-ag` — forall([r], is-ring(r) implies forall([a in carr(r)], (add(r))(a, a) = a implies zero(r) = a))
- `abelian-group-idempotent-is-id-rev-ring-additive-ag-rev` — forall([r], is-ring(r) implies forall([a in carr(r)], (add(r))(a, a) = a implies a = zero(r)))
- `abelian-group-idempotent-is-id-ring-additive-ag` — forall([r], is-ring(r) implies forall([a in carr(r)], (add(r))(a, a) = a implies a = zero(r)))
- `abelian-group-idempotent-is-id-ring-additive-ag-rev` — forall([r], is-ring(r) implies forall([a in carr(r)], (add(r))(a, a) = a implies zero(r) = a))

- `abelian-group-opr-comm-rev-ring-additive-ag` — forall([r], is-ring(r) implies forall([a in carr(r), b in carr(r)], (add(r))(b, a) = (add(r))(a, b)))
- `abelian-group-opr-comm-rev-ring-additive-ag-rev` — forall([r], is-ring(r) implies forall([a in carr(r), b in carr(r)], (add(r))(a, b) = (add(r))(b, a)))
- `abelian-group-opr-comm-ring-additive-ag` — forall([r], is-ring(r) implies forall([a in carr(r), b in carr(r)], (add(r))(a, b) = (add(r))(b, a)))
- `abelian-group-opr-comm-ring-additive-ag-rev` — forall([r], is-ring(r) implies forall([a in carr(r), b in carr(r)], (add(r))(b, a) = (add(r))(a, b)))
- `ag-cancel-right-rev-ring-additive-ag` — forall([r], is-ring(r) implies forall([a in carr(r), b in carr(r), c in carr(r)], (add(r))(a, c) = (add(r))(b, c) implies b = a))
- `ag-cancel-right-rev-ring-additive-ag-rev` — forall([r], is-ring(r) implies forall([a in carr(r), b in carr(r), c in carr(r)], (add(r))(a, c) = (add(r))(b, c) implies a = b))
- `ag-cancel-right-ring-additive-ag` — forall([r], is-ring(r) implies forall([a in carr(r), b in carr(r), c in carr(r)], (add(r))(a, c) = (add(r))(b, c) implies a = b))
- `ag-cancel-right-ring-additive-ag-rev` — forall([r], is-ring(r) implies forall([a in carr(r), b in carr(r), c in carr(r)], (add(r))(a, c) = (add(r))(b, c) implies b = a))
- `border-identity` — forall([a], is-ring(a) implies forall([p in nn], border(a, one(a), identmat(a, p), p, p) = identmat(a, succ(p))))
- `border-identity-rev` — forall([a], is-ring(a) implies forall([p in nn], identmat(a, succ(p)) = border(a, one(a), identmat(a, p), p, p)))
- `border-invertible` — forall([a], is-ring(a) implies forall([n, u], n in nn implies is-invertible-mat(a, n, u) implies is-invertible-mat(a, succ(n), border(a, one(a), u, n, n))))
- `border-is-diagonal` — forall([a], is-ring(a) implies forall([b, e, p, q], p in nn implies q in nn implies is-diagonal(a, p, q, e) implies b in carr(a) implies e in mat(p, q, carr(a)) implies is-diagonal(a, succ(p), succ(q), border(a, b, e, p, q))))
- `border-mult` — forall([a], is-ring(a) implies forall([p, q, r, x, y, b, d], p in nn implies q in nn implies r in nn implies x in mat(p, q, carr(a)) implies y in mat(q, r, carr(a)) implies b in carr(a) implies d in carr(a) implies (q = 0 implies p = 0 or r = 0) implies matmul(a, border(a, b, x, p, q), border(a, d, y, q, r)) = border(a, (mul(a))(b, d), matmul(a, x, y), p, r)))

- `border-staircase` — forall([a], is-ring(a) implies forall([b, w, p, q, kp], p in nn implies q in nn implies kp in nn implies b in carr(a) implies not(b = zero(a)) implies smith-staircase(a, p, q, w, kp) implies w in mat(p, q, carr(a)) implies smith-staircase(a, succ(p), succ(q), border(a, b, w, p, q), succ(kp))))

- `ter-red-type` — forall([a], is-ring(a) implies forall([m, n, k, l, p, q, r, row, col, x], p in mat(m, n, carr(a)) implies q in mat(n, k, carr(a)) implies r in mat(k, l, carr(a)) implies row in interval(1, m) implies col in interval(1, l) implies x in interval(1, n) implies vnb-lambda(c, interval(1, k), (mul(a))((mul(a))(entry(p, row, x), entry(q, x, c)), entry(r, c, col))) in fun(interval(1, k), carr(ring-additive-ag(a)))))
- `ter-tout-type` — forall([a], is-ring(a) implies forall([m, n, k, l, p, q, r, row, col], p in mat(m, n, carr(a)) implies q in mat(n, k, carr(a)) implies r in mat(k, l, carr(a)) implies row in interval(1, m) implies col in interval(1, l) implies vnb-lambda(j, interval(1, n), finsum(ring-additive-ag(a), vnb-lambda(c, interval(1, k), (vnb-lambda(z, cartesian(interval(1, k), interval(1, n)), (mul(a))((mul(a))(entry(p, row, nth(2, z)), entry(q, nth(2, z), nth(1, z))), entry(r, nth(1, z), col))))([c, j])), interval(1, k))) in fun(interval(1, n), carr(ring-additive-ag(a)))))
- `triple-entry-left` — forall([a], is-ring(a) implies forall([m, n, k, l, p, q, r, row, col], p in mat(m, n, carr(a)) implies q in mat(n, k, carr(a)) implies r in mat(k, l, carr(a)) implies 1 <= n implies 1 <= k implies row in interval(1, m) implies col in interval(1, l) implies entry(matmul(a, matmul(a, p, q), r), row, col) = finsum(ring-additive-ag(a), vnb-lambda(c, interval(1, k), finsum(ring-additive-ag(a), vnb-lambda(j, interval(1, n), (vnb-lambda(z, cartesian(interval(1, k), interval(1, n)), (mul(a))((mul(a))(entry(p, row, nth(2, z)), entry(q, nth(2, z), nth(1, z))), entry(r, nth(1, z), col))))([c, j])), interval(1, n))), interval(1, k))))
- `triple-entry-left-rev` — forall([a], is-ring(a) implies forall([m, n, k, l, p, q, r, row, col], p in mat(m, n, carr(a)) implies q in mat(n, k, carr(a)) implies r in mat(k, l, carr(a)) implies 1 <= n implies 1 <= k implies row in interval(1, m) implies col in interval(1, l) implies finsum(ring-additive-ag(a), vnb-lambda(c, interval(1, k), finsum(ring-additive-ag(a), vnb-lambda(j, interval(1, n), (vnb-lambda(z, cartesian(interval(1, k), interval(1, n)), (mul(a))((mul(a))(entry(p, row, nth(2, z)), entry(q, nth(2, z), nth(1, z))), entry(r, nth(1, z), col))))([c, j])), interval(1, n))), interval(1, k)) = entry(matmul(a, matmul(a, p, q), r), row, col)))
- `triple-entry-right` — forall([a], is-ring(a) implies forall([m, n, k, l, p, q, r, row, col], p in mat(m, n, carr(a)) implies q in mat(n, k, carr(a)) implies r in mat(k, l, carr(a)) implies 1 <= n implies 1 <= k implies row in interval(1, m) implies col in interval(1, l) implies entry(matmul(a, p, matmul(a, q, r)), row, col) = finsum(ring-additive-ag(a), vnb-lambda(j, interval(1, n), finsum(ring-additive-ag(a), vnb-lambda(c, interval(1, k), (vnb-lambda(z, cartesian(interval(1, k), interval(1, n)), (mul(a))((mul(a))(entry(p, row, nth(2, z)), entry(q, nth(2, z), nth(1, z))), entry(r, nth(1, z), col))))([c, j])), interval(1, k))), interval(1, n))))
- `triple-entry-right-rev` — forall([a], is-ring(a) implies forall([m, n, k, l, p, q, r, row, col], p in mat(m, n, carr(a)) implies q in mat(n, k, carr(a)) implies r in mat(k, l, carr(a)) implies 1 <= n implies 1 <= k implies row in interval(1, m) implies col in interval(1, l) implies finsum(ring-additive-ag(a), vnb-lambda(j, interval(1, n), finsum(ring-additive-ag(a), vnb-lambda(c, interval(1, k), (vnb-lambda(z, cartesian(interval(1, k), interval(1, n)), (mul(a))((mul(a))(entry(p, row, nth(2, z)), entry(q, nth(2, z), nth(1, z))), entry(r, nth(1, z), col))))([c, j])), interval(1, k))), interval(1, n)) = entry(matmul(a, p, matmul(a, q, r)), row, col)))
- `unitrow-type` — forall([a], is-ring(a) implies forall([n, i], n in nn implies unitrow(a, n, i) in mat(1, n, carr(a))))
- `zeromat-type` — forall([a], is-ring(a) implies forall([m, n], m in nn implies n in nn implies zeromat(a, m, n) in mat(m, n, carr(a))))
- `zz-act-assoc-ring-additive-ag` — forall([r], is-ring(r) implies forall([j in zz, k in zz, a in carr(r)], zz-act(ring-additive-ag(r), j * k, a) = zz-act(ring-additive-ag(r), j, zz-act(ring-additive-ag(r), k, a))))
- `zz-act-assoc-ring-additive-ag-rev` — forall([r], is-ring(r) implies forall([j in zz, k in zz, a in carr(r)], zz-act(ring-additive-ag(r), j, zz-act(ring-additive-ag(r), k, a)) = zz-act(ring-additive-ag(r), j * k, a)))
- `zz-act-distrib-ring-additive-ag` — forall([r], is-ring(r) implies forall([k in zz, a in carr(r), b in carr(r)], zz-act(ring-additive-ag(r), k, (add(r))(a, b)) = (add(r))(zz-act(ring-additive-ag(r), k, a), zz-act(ring-additive-ag(r), k, b))))
- `zz-act-distrib-ring-additive-ag-rev` — forall([r], is-ring(r) implies forall([k in zz, a in carr(r), b in carr(r)], (add(r))(zz-act(ring-additive-ag(r), k, a), zz-act(ring-additive-ag(r), k, b)) = zz-act(ring-additive-ag(r), k, (add(r))(a, b))))

*Views into `ring`.*

- `ringoid-as-ring` — from `ringoid`: (carr add mul neg zero one) ↦ (carr add mul neg zero one)

*Views from `ring`.*

- `ring-additive-ag` — into `abelian-group`: (carr add zero neg) ↦ (carr opr iden inv)
- `ring-multiplicative-monoid` — into `monoid`: (carr mul one) ↦ (carr opr iden)

### ringoid
<a id="ringoid"></a>

*Declared in* [`structure-library/ringoid.scm`](../structure-library/ringoid.scm).

*Kind.* Shape structure — `declare-structure` with slot clauses.

*Slots* (7): `carr` carrier; `add` operation cartesian(carr, carr) -> carr; `mul` operation cartesian(carr, carr) -> carr; `neg` operation carr -> carr; `zero` constant in carr; `one` constant in carr; `idl` constant in power(carr).

*Default arrows* (`is-hom-ringoid-def`, replaced by `declare-hom!`): forall([a, b, f], is-hom-ringoid(a, b, f) iff is-ringoid(a) and is-ringoid(b) and f in fun(carr(a), carr(b)) and is-hom-ring(ringoid-as-ring(a), ringoid-as-ring(b), f) and forall([x_ in idl(a)], f(x_) in idl(b)))

*Declaration.*

```scheme
(declare-structure ringoid
  (carriers carr)
  (op add (cartesian carr carr) carr)
  (op mul (cartesian carr carr) carr)
  (op neg carr carr)
  (constant zero carr)
  (constant one carr)
  (constant idl (power carr))
  (property is-associative add carr)
  (property is-commutative add carr)
  (property is-identity add zero carr)
  (property has-inverses add zero neg carr)
  (property is-associative mul carr)
  (property is-identity mul one carr)
  (property is-distributive add mul carr)
  (property is-ideal-in idl carr add mul neg zero))
```

*Defining predicate* (as stored):

- `is-ringoid` — forall([s], is-ringoid(s) iff length(s) = 7 and carr(s) in set and add(s) in fun(cartesian(carr(s), carr(s)), carr(s)) and mul(s) in fun(cartesian(carr(s), carr(s)), carr(s)) and neg(s) in fun(carr(s), carr(s)) and zero(s) in carr(s) and one(s) in carr(s) and idl(s) in power(carr(s)) and is-associative(add(s), carr(s)) and is-commutative(add(s), carr(s)) and is-identity(add(s), zero(s), carr(s)) and has-inverses(add(s), zero(s), neg(s), carr(s)) and is-associative(mul(s), carr(s)) and is-identity(mul(s), one(s), carr(s)) and is-distributive(add(s), mul(s), carr(s)) and is-ideal-in(idl(s), carr(s), add(s), mul(s), neg(s), zero(s)))

*Theorems quantifying over `is-ringoid`.*

- `hom-ringoid-id` — forall([a], is-ringoid(a) implies is-hom-ringoid(a, a, id-fun(carr(a))))
- `r6b-add-respects2` — forall([r], is-ringoid(r) implies respects2(ringoid-setoid(r), vnb-lambda([a, b], cartesian(carr(r), carr(r)), class(ringoid-setoid(r), (add(r))(a, b)))))
- `r6b-radd-interchange` — forall([r], is-ringoid(r) implies forall([a, b, c, d], a in carr(r) implies b in carr(r) implies c in carr(r) implies d in carr(r) implies (add(r))((add(r))(a, b), (add(r))(c, d)) = (add(r))((add(r))(a, c), (add(r))(b, d))))
- `r6b-radd-interchange-rev` — forall([r], is-ringoid(r) implies forall([a, b, c, d], a in carr(r) implies b in carr(r) implies c in carr(r) implies d in carr(r) implies (add(r))((add(r))(a, c), (add(r))(b, d)) = (add(r))((add(r))(a, b), (add(r))(c, d))))
- `r6b-radd-right-id` — forall([r], is-ringoid(r) implies forall([a in carr(r)], (add(r))(a, zero(r)) = a))
- `r6b-radd-right-id-rev` — forall([r], is-ringoid(r) implies forall([a in carr(r)], a = (add(r))(a, zero(r))))
- `r6b-radd-type` — forall([r], is-ringoid(r) implies forall([a, b], a in carr(r) implies b in carr(r) implies (add(r))(a, b) in carr(r)))
- `r6b-rdiff-add` — forall([r], is-ringoid(r) implies forall([a, b, u, v], a in carr(r) implies b in carr(r) implies u in carr(r) implies v in carr(r) implies (add(r))((add(r))(a, b), (neg(r))((add(r))(u, v))) = (add(r))((add(r))(a, (neg(r))(u)), (add(r))(b, (neg(r))(v)))))
- `r6b-rdiff-add-rev` — forall([r], is-ringoid(r) implies forall([a, b, u, v], a in carr(r) implies b in carr(r) implies u in carr(r) implies v in carr(r) implies (add(r))((add(r))(a, (neg(r))(u)), (add(r))(b, (neg(r))(v))) = (add(r))((add(r))(a, b), (neg(r))((add(r))(u, v)))))
- `r6b-related-in` — forall([r], is-ringoid(r) implies forall([a, b], a in carr(r) implies b in carr(r) implies (add(r))(a, (neg(r))(b)) in idl(r) implies related(ringoid-setoid(r), a, b)))
- `r6b-related-out` — forall([r], is-ringoid(r) implies forall([a, b], a in carr(r) implies b in carr(r) implies related(ringoid-setoid(r), a, b) implies (add(r))(a, (neg(r))(b)) in idl(r)))
- `r6b-rinv-unique` — forall([r], is-ringoid(r) implies forall([a, b], a in carr(r) implies b in carr(r) implies (add(r))(a, b) = zero(r) implies b = (neg(r))(a)))
- `r6b-rinv-unique-rev` — forall([r], is-ringoid(r) implies forall([a, b], a in carr(r) implies b in carr(r) implies (add(r))(a, b) = zero(r) implies (neg(r))(a) = b))
- `r6b-rneg-add` — forall([r], is-ringoid(r) implies forall([a, b], a in carr(r) implies b in carr(r) implies (neg(r))((add(r))(a, b)) = (add(r))((neg(r))(a), (neg(r))(b))))
- `r6b-rneg-add-rev` — forall([r], is-ringoid(r) implies forall([a, b], a in carr(r) implies b in carr(r) implies (add(r))((neg(r))(a), (neg(r))(b)) = (neg(r))((add(r))(a, b))))
- `r6b-rneg-neg` — forall([r], is-ringoid(r) implies forall([a in carr(r)], (neg(r))((neg(r))(a)) = a))
- `r6b-rneg-neg-rev` — forall([r], is-ringoid(r) implies forall([a in carr(r)], a = (neg(r))((neg(r))(a))))
- `r6b-rneg-type` — forall([r], is-ringoid(r) implies forall([a in carr(r)], (neg(r))(a) in carr(r)))
- `r6b-rq-add-lam-type` — forall([r], is-ringoid(r) implies vnb-lambda([a, b], cartesian(carr(r), carr(r)), class(ringoid-setoid(r), (add(r))(a, b))) in fun(cartesian(pts(ringoid-setoid(r)), pts(ringoid-setoid(r))), quotient(ringoid-setoid(r))))
- `r6b-setoid-rel` — forall([r], is-ringoid(r) implies rel(ringoid-setoid(r)) == ringoid-rel(r))
- `r6b-setoid-rel-rev` — forall([r], is-ringoid(r) implies ringoid-rel(r) == rel(ringoid-setoid(r)))
- `r7n-mul-respects2` — forall([r], is-ringoid(r) implies respects2(ringoid-setoid(r), vnb-lambda([a, b], cartesian(carr(r), carr(r)), class(ringoid-setoid(r), (mul(r))(a, b)))))
- `r7n-neg-respects` — forall([r], is-ringoid(r) implies respects(ringoid-setoid(r), vnb-lambda(a, carr(r), class(ringoid-setoid(r), (neg(r))(a)))))
- `r7n-radd-idem-zero` — forall([r], is-ringoid(r) implies forall([a in carr(r)], (add(r))(a, a) = a implies a = zero(r)))
- `r7n-radd-idem-zero-rev` — forall([r], is-ringoid(r) implies forall([a in carr(r)], (add(r))(a, a) = a implies zero(r) = a))
- `r7n-rdiff-mul` — forall([r], is-ringoid(r) implies forall([a, b, u, v], a in carr(r) implies b in carr(r) implies u in carr(r) implies v in carr(r) implies (add(r))((mul(r))(a, b), (neg(r))((mul(r))(u, v))) = (add(r))((mul(r))((add(r))(a, (neg(r))(u)), b), (mul(r))(u, (add(r))(b, (neg(r))(v))))))
- `r7n-rdiff-mul-rev` — forall([r], is-ringoid(r) implies forall([a, b, u, v], a in carr(r) implies b in carr(r) implies u in carr(r) implies v in carr(r) implies (add(r))((mul(r))((add(r))(a, (neg(r))(u)), b), (mul(r))(u, (add(r))(b, (neg(r))(v)))) = (add(r))((mul(r))(a, b), (neg(r))((mul(r))(u, v)))))
- `r7n-rmul-neg-right` — forall([r], is-ringoid(r) implies forall([a, b], a in carr(r) implies b in carr(r) implies (mul(r))(a, (neg(r))(b)) = (neg(r))((mul(r))(a, b))))
- `r7n-rmul-neg-right-rev` — forall([r], is-ringoid(r) implies forall([a, b], a in carr(r) implies b in carr(r) implies (neg(r))((mul(r))(a, b)) = (mul(r))(a, (neg(r))(b))))
- `r7n-rmul-type` — forall([r], is-ringoid(r) implies forall([a, b], a in carr(r) implies b in carr(r) implies (mul(r))(a, b) in carr(r)))
- `r7n-rmul-zero-right` — forall([r], is-ringoid(r) implies forall([a in carr(r)], (mul(r))(a, zero(r)) = zero(r)))
- `r7n-rmul-zero-right-rev` — forall([r], is-ringoid(r) implies forall([a in carr(r)], zero(r) = (mul(r))(a, zero(r))))
- `r7n-rneg-mul-left` — forall([r], is-ringoid(r) implies forall([a, b], a in carr(r) implies b in carr(r) implies (mul(r))((neg(r))(a), b) = (neg(r))((mul(r))(a, b))))
- `r7n-rneg-mul-left-rev` — forall([r], is-ringoid(r) implies forall([a, b], a in carr(r) implies b in carr(r) implies (neg(r))((mul(r))(a, b)) = (mul(r))((neg(r))(a), b)))
- `r7n-rone-type` — forall([r], is-ringoid(r) implies one(r) in carr(r))
- `r7n-rq-mul-lam-type` — forall([r], is-ringoid(r) implies vnb-lambda([a, b], cartesian(carr(r), carr(r)), class(ringoid-setoid(r), (mul(r))(a, b))) in fun(cartesian(pts(ringoid-setoid(r)), pts(ringoid-setoid(r))), quotient(ringoid-setoid(r))))
- `r7n-rq-neg-lam-type` — forall([r], is-ringoid(r) implies vnb-lambda(a, carr(r), class(ringoid-setoid(r), (neg(r))(a))) in fun(pts(ringoid-setoid(r)), quotient(ringoid-setoid(r))))

### semi-inner-product-space
<a id="semi-inner-product-space"></a>

*Declared in* [`structure-library/sip-vocab.scm`](../structure-library/sip-vocab.scm).

*Kind.* Shape structure — `declare-structure` with slot clauses.

*Slots* (7): `scal` substructure, a ring; `vec` carrier; `vadd` operation cartesian(vec, vec) -> vec; `vzero` constant in vec; `vneg` operation vec -> vec; `act` operation cartesian(carr(scal), vec) -> vec; `rip` operation cartesian(vec, vec) -> rr.

*Default arrows* (`is-hom-semi-inner-product-space-def`): forall([a, b, f], is-hom-semi-inner-product-space(a, b, f) iff is-semi-inner-product-space(a) and is-semi-inner-product-space(b) and f in fun(vec(a), vec(b)) and scal(a) = scal(b) and forall([x1_ in vec(a), x2_ in vec(a)], f((vadd(a))(x1_, x2_)) = (vadd(b))(f(x1_), f(x2_))) and f(vzero(a)) = vzero(b) and forall([x1_ in vec(a)], f((vneg(a))(x1_)) = (vneg(b))(f(x1_))) and forall([x1_ in carr(scal(a)), x2_ in vec(a)], f((act(a))(x1_, x2_)) = (act(b))(x1_, f(x2_))) and forall([x1_ in vec(a), x2_ in vec(a)], (rip(a))(x1_, x2_) = (rip(b))(f(x1_), f(x2_))))

*Declaration.*

```scheme
(declare-structure semi-inner-product-space
  (instance-var s)
  (substructure scal ring)
  (carriers vec)
  (op vadd (cartesian vec vec) vec)
  (constant vzero vec)
  (op vneg vec vec)
  (op act (cartesian (carr scal) vec) vec)
  (op rip (cartesian vec vec) rr)
  (property is-associative vadd vec)
  (property is-commutative vadd vec)
  (property is-identity vadd vzero vec)
  (property has-inverses vadd vzero vneg vec)
  (law "scal(s) = normed-field-as-commutative-ring(rr-normed-field)")
  (law "carr(scal(s)) = rr")
  (law "forall([r_ in carr(scal(s)), x_ in vec(s), y_ in vec(s)],
          act(s)(r_, vadd(s)(x_, y_)) = vadd(s)(act(s)(r_, x_), act(s)(r_, y_)))")
  (law "forall([r_ in carr(scal(s)), s_ in carr(scal(s)), x_ in vec(s)],
          act(s)(add(scal(s))(r_, s_), x_) = vadd(s)(act(s)(r_, x_), act(s)(s_, x_)))")
  (law "forall([r_ in carr(scal(s)), s_ in carr(scal(s)), x_ in vec(s)],
          act(s)(mul(scal(s))(r_, s_), x_) = act(s)(r_, act(s)(s_, x_)))")
  (law "forall([x_ in vec(s)], act(s)(one(scal(s)), x_) = x_)")
  (law "forall([x_ in vec(s), y_ in vec(s)], rip(s)(x_, y_) = rip(s)(y_, x_))")
  (law "forall([x_ in vec(s), y_ in vec(s), z_ in vec(s)],
          rip(s)(vadd(s)(x_, y_), z_) = rip(s)(x_, z_) + rip(s)(y_, z_))")
  (law "forall([a_ in rr, x_ in vec(s), y_ in vec(s)],
          rip(s)(act(s)(a_, x_), y_) = a_ * rip(s)(x_, y_))")
  (law "forall([x_ in vec(s)], 0 <= rip(s)(x_, x_))"))
```

*Defining predicate* (as stored):

- `is-semi-inner-product-space` — forall([s], is-semi-inner-product-space(s) iff length(s) = 7 and is-ring(scal(s)) and vec(s) in set and vadd(s) in fun(cartesian(vec(s), vec(s)), vec(s)) and vzero(s) in vec(s) and vneg(s) in fun(vec(s), vec(s)) and act(s) in fun(cartesian(carr(scal(s)), vec(s)), vec(s)) and rip(s) in fun(cartesian(vec(s), vec(s)), rr) and is-associative(vadd(s), vec(s)) and is-commutative(vadd(s), vec(s)) and is-identity(vadd(s), vzero(s), vec(s)) and has-inverses(vadd(s), vzero(s), vneg(s), vec(s)) and scal(s) = normed-field-as-commutative-ring(rr-normed-field) and carr(scal(s)) = rr and forall([r_ in carr(scal(s)), x_ in vec(s), y_ in vec(s)], (act(s))(r_, (vadd(s))(x_, y_)) = (vadd(s))((act(s))(r_, x_), (act(s))(r_, y_))) and forall([r_ in carr(scal(s)), s_ in carr(scal(s)), x_ in vec(s)], (act(s))((add(scal(s)))(r_, s_), x_) = (vadd(s))((act(s))(r_, x_), (act(s))(s_, x_))) and forall([r_ in carr(scal(s)), s_ in carr(scal(s)), x_ in vec(s)], (act(s))((mul(scal(s)))(r_, s_), x_) = (act(s))(r_, (act(s))(s_, x_))) and forall([x_ in vec(s)], (act(s))(one(scal(s)), x_) = x_) and forall([x_ in vec(s), y_ in vec(s)], (rip(s))(x_, y_) = (rip(s))(y_, x_)) and forall([x_ in vec(s), y_ in vec(s), z_ in vec(s)], (rip(s))((vadd(s))(x_, y_), z_) = (rip(s))(x_, z_) + (rip(s))(y_, z_)) and forall([a_ in rr, x_ in vec(s), y_ in vec(s)], (rip(s))((act(s))(a_, x_), y_) = a_ * (rip(s))(x_, y_)) and forall([x_ in vec(s)], 0 <= (rip(s))(x_, x_)))
