- `sipq-rip-homog-left` — forall([v], is-semi-inner-product-space(v) implies forall([a_ in rr, x_ in vec(sip-quotient(v)), y_ in vec(sip-quotient(v))], (rip(sip-quotient(v)))((act(sip-quotient(v)))(a_, x_), y_) = a_ * (rip(sip-quotient(v)))(x_, y_)))
- `sipq-rip-homog-left-rev` — forall([v], is-semi-inner-product-space(v) implies forall([a_ in rr, x_ in vec(sip-quotient(v)), y_ in vec(sip-quotient(v))], a_ * (rip(sip-quotient(v)))(x_, y_) = (rip(sip-quotient(v)))((act(sip-quotient(v)))(a_, x_), y_)))
- `sipq-rip-rev` — forall([v], is-semi-inner-product-space(v) implies sip-qrip(v) == rip(sip-quotient(v)))
- `sipq-rip-self-nonneg` — forall([v], is-semi-inner-product-space(v) implies forall([x_ in vec(sip-quotient(v))], 0 <= (rip(sip-quotient(v)))(x_, x_)))
- `sipq-rip-sym` — forall([v], is-semi-inner-product-space(v) implies forall([x_ in vec(sip-quotient(v)), y_ in vec(sip-quotient(v))], (rip(sip-quotient(v)))(x_, y_) = (rip(sip-quotient(v)))(y_, x_)))
- `sipq-rip-sym-rev` — forall([v], is-semi-inner-product-space(v) implies forall([x_ in vec(sip-quotient(v)), y_ in vec(sip-quotient(v))], (rip(sip-quotient(v)))(y_, x_) = (rip(sip-quotient(v)))(x_, y_)))
- `sipq-scal` — forall([v], is-semi-inner-product-space(v) implies scal(sip-quotient(v)) == scal(v))
- `sipq-scal-carr` — forall([v], is-semi-inner-product-space(v) implies carr(scal(sip-quotient(v))) = rr)
- `sipq-scal-carr-rev` — forall([v], is-semi-inner-product-space(v) implies rr = carr(scal(sip-quotient(v))))
- `sipq-scal-pin` — forall([v], is-semi-inner-product-space(v) implies scal(sip-quotient(v)) = normed-field-as-commutative-ring(rr-normed-field))
- `sipq-scal-pin-rev` — forall([v], is-semi-inner-product-space(v) implies normed-field-as-commutative-ring(rr-normed-field) = scal(sip-quotient(v)))
- `sipq-scal-rev` — forall([v], is-semi-inner-product-space(v) implies scal(v) == scal(sip-quotient(v)))
- `sipq-scal-ring` — forall([v], is-semi-inner-product-space(v) implies is-ring(scal(sip-quotient(v))))
- `sipq-scale-computes` — forall([v], is-semi-inner-product-space(v) implies forall([r_ in rr, a_ in vec(v)], (sip-qscale(v, r_))(class(sip-setoid(v), a_)) = class(sip-setoid(v), (act(v))(r_, a_))))
- `sipq-scale-computes-rev` — forall([v], is-semi-inner-product-space(v) implies forall([r_ in rr, a_ in vec(v)], class(sip-setoid(v), (act(v))(r_, a_)) = (sip-qscale(v, r_))(class(sip-setoid(v), a_))))
- `sipq-scale-fun` — forall([v], is-semi-inner-product-space(v) implies forall([r_ in rr], sip-qscale(v, r_) in fun(quotient(sip-setoid(v)), quotient(sip-setoid(v)))))
- `sipq-unfold` — forall([v], is-semi-inner-product-space(v) implies sip-quotient(v) == [scal(v), quotient(sip-setoid(v)), sip-qvadd(v), class(sip-setoid(v), vzero(v)), sip-qvneg(v), sip-qact(v), sip-qrip(v)])
- `sipq-unfold-rev` — forall([v], is-semi-inner-product-space(v) implies [scal(v), quotient(sip-setoid(v)), sip-qvadd(v), class(sip-setoid(v), vzero(v)), sip-qvneg(v), sip-qact(v), sip-qrip(v)] == sip-quotient(v))
- `sipq-vadd` — forall([v], is-semi-inner-product-space(v) implies vadd(sip-quotient(v)) == sip-qvadd(v))
- `sipq-vadd-computes` — forall([v], is-semi-inner-product-space(v) implies forall([a_ in vec(v), b_ in vec(v)], (vadd(sip-quotient(v)))(class(sip-setoid(v), a_), class(sip-setoid(v), b_)) = class(sip-setoid(v), (vadd(v))(a_, b_))))
- `sipq-vadd-computes-rev` — forall([v], is-semi-inner-product-space(v) implies forall([a_ in vec(v), b_ in vec(v)], class(sip-setoid(v), (vadd(v))(a_, b_)) = (vadd(sip-quotient(v)))(class(sip-setoid(v), a_), class(sip-setoid(v), b_))))
- `sipq-vadd-fun` — forall([v], is-semi-inner-product-space(v) implies vadd(sip-quotient(v)) in fun(cartesian(vec(sip-quotient(v)), vec(sip-quotient(v))), vec(sip-quotient(v))))
- `sipq-vadd-rev` — forall([v], is-semi-inner-product-space(v) implies sip-qvadd(v) == vadd(sip-quotient(v)))
- `sipq-vec` — forall([v], is-semi-inner-product-space(v) implies vec(sip-quotient(v)) == quotient(sip-setoid(v)))
- `sipq-vec-rev` — forall([v], is-semi-inner-product-space(v) implies quotient(sip-setoid(v)) == vec(sip-quotient(v)))
- `sipq-vec-set` — forall([v], is-semi-inner-product-space(v) implies vec(sip-quotient(v)) in set)
- `sipq-vneg` — forall([v], is-semi-inner-product-space(v) implies vneg(sip-quotient(v)) == sip-qvneg(v))
- `sipq-vneg-computes` — forall([v], is-semi-inner-product-space(v) implies forall([a_ in vec(v)], (vneg(sip-quotient(v)))(class(sip-setoid(v), a_)) = class(sip-setoid(v), (vneg(v))(a_))))
- `sipq-vneg-computes-rev` — forall([v], is-semi-inner-product-space(v) implies forall([a_ in vec(v)], class(sip-setoid(v), (vneg(v))(a_)) = (vneg(sip-quotient(v)))(class(sip-setoid(v), a_))))
- `sipq-vneg-fun` — forall([v], is-semi-inner-product-space(v) implies vneg(sip-quotient(v)) in fun(vec(sip-quotient(v)), vec(sip-quotient(v))))
- `sipq-vneg-rev` — forall([v], is-semi-inner-product-space(v) implies sip-qvneg(v) == vneg(sip-quotient(v)))
- `sipq-vzero` — forall([v], is-semi-inner-product-space(v) implies vzero(sip-quotient(v)) == class(sip-setoid(v), vzero(v)))
- `sipq-vzero-in` — forall([v], is-semi-inner-product-space(v) implies vzero(sip-quotient(v)) in vec(sip-quotient(v)))
- `sipq-vzero-rev` — forall([v], is-semi-inner-product-space(v) implies class(sip-setoid(v), vzero(v)) == vzero(sip-quotient(v)))
- `span-is-submodule-sip-as-module` — forall([r], is-semi-inner-product-space(r) implies forall([n, u in mat(n, 1, vec(r))], is-submodule(sip-as-module(r), span(sip-as-module(r), n, u))))
- `spans-span-sip-as-module` — forall([r], is-semi-inner-product-space(r) implies forall([n, u in mat(n, 1, vec(r))], spans(sip-as-module(r), n, u, span(sip-as-module(r), n, u))))
- `spans-transport-sip-as-module` — forall([r], is-semi-inner-product-space(r) implies forall([n, u, pm, sm], u in mat(n, 1, vec(r)) implies is-invertible-mat(scal(r), n, pm) implies is-submodule(sip-as-module(r), sm) implies spans(sip-as-module(r), n, u, sm) implies spans(sip-as-module(r), n, matact(sip-as-module(r), pm, u), sm)))
- `submodule-finsum-closed-sip-as-module` — forall([r], is-semi-inner-product-space(r) implies forall([sm], is-submodule(sip-as-module(r), sm) implies forall([s in set], card(s) in nn implies forall([f in fun(s, carr(module-vector-ag(sip-as-module(r))))], forall([z in s], f(z) in sm) implies finsum(module-vector-ag(sip-as-module(r)), f, s) in sm))))
- `submodule-intersection-sip-as-module` — forall([r], is-semi-inner-product-space(r) implies forall([s1], is-submodule(sip-as-module(r), s1) implies forall([s2], is-submodule(sip-as-module(r), s2) implies is-submodule(sip-as-module(r), intersection(s1, s2)))))
- `whole-module-is-submodule-sip-as-module` — forall([r], is-semi-inner-product-space(r) implies is-submodule(sip-as-module(r), vec(r)))

*Views into `semi-inner-product-space`.*

- `csip-as-sip` — from `complex-semi-inner-product-space`: (scal vec vadd vzero vneg act rip) ↦ (scal vec vadd vzero vneg act rip)

*Views from `semi-inner-product-space`.*

- `sip-as-module` — into `module`: (scal vec vadd vzero vneg act) ↦ (scal vec vadd vzero vneg act)

### semigroup
<a id="semigroup"></a>

*Declared in* [`structure-library/semigroup.scm`](../structure-library/semigroup.scm).

*Kind.* Shape structure — `declare-structure` with slot clauses.

*Slots* (2): `carr` carrier; `opr` operation cartesian(carr, carr) -> carr.

*Default arrows* (`is-hom-semigroup-def`): forall([a, b, f], is-hom-semigroup(a, b, f) iff is-semigroup(a) and is-semigroup(b) and f in fun(carr(a), carr(b)) and forall([x1_ in carr(a), x2_ in carr(a)], f((opr(a))(x1_, x2_)) = (opr(b))(f(x1_), f(x2_))))

*Declaration.*

```scheme
(declare-structure semigroup
  (carriers carr)
  (op opr (cartesian carr carr) carr)
  (property is-associative opr carr))
```

*Defining predicate* (as stored):

- `is-semigroup` — forall([s], is-semigroup(s) iff length(s) = 2 and carr(s) in set and opr(s) in fun(cartesian(carr(s), carr(s)), carr(s)) and is-associative(opr(s), carr(s)))

*Theorems quantifying over `is-semigroup`.*

- `hom-semigroup-id` — forall([a], is-semigroup(a) implies is-hom-semigroup(a, a, id-fun(carr(a))))
- `semigroup-assoc` — forall([s], is-semigroup(s) implies forall([a in carr(s), b in carr(s), c in carr(s)], (opr(s))((opr(s))(a, b), c) = (opr(s))(a, (opr(s))(b, c))))
- `semigroup-assoc-rev` — forall([s], is-semigroup(s) implies forall([a in carr(s), b in carr(s), c in carr(s)], (opr(s))(a, (opr(s))(b, c)) = (opr(s))((opr(s))(a, b), c)))

### setoid
<a id="setoid"></a>

*Declared in* [`structure-library/setoid.scm`](../structure-library/setoid.scm).

*Kind.* Shape structure — `declare-structure` with slot clauses.

*Slots* (2): `pts` carrier; `rel` relation on pts.

*Default arrows* (`is-hom-setoid-def`): forall([a, b, f], is-hom-setoid(a, b, f) iff is-setoid(a) and is-setoid(b) and f in fun(pts(a), pts(b)) and forall([x1_ in pts(a), x2_ in pts(a)], [x1_, x2_] in rel(a) implies [f(x1_), f(x2_)] in rel(b)))

*Declaration.*

```scheme
(declare-structure setoid
  (carriers pts)
  (relation rel pts)
  (property is-equivalence rel pts))
```

*Defining predicate* (as stored):

- `is-setoid` — forall([s], is-setoid(s) iff length(s) = 2 and pts(s) in set and rel(s) in power(cartesian(pts(s), pts(s))) and is-equivalence(rel(s), pts(s)))

*Theorems quantifying over `is-setoid`.*

- `class-disjoint` — forall([s], is-setoid(s) implies forall([a in pts(s), b in pts(s)], class(s, a) = class(s, b) or intersection(class(s, a), class(s, b)) = empty-set))
- `class-eq-iff` — forall([s], is-setoid(s) implies forall([a in pts(s), b in pts(s)], class(s, a) = class(s, b) iff related(s, a, b)))
- `class-eq-iff-rev` — forall([s], is-setoid(s) implies forall([a in pts(s), b in pts(s)], related(s, a, b) iff class(s, a) = class(s, b)))
- `class-in-quotient` — forall([s], is-setoid(s) implies forall([a in pts(s)], class(s, a) in quotient(s)))
- `class-is-set` — forall([s], is-setoid(s) implies forall([a in pts(s)], class(s, a) in set))
- `class-self` — forall([s], is-setoid(s) implies forall([a in pts(s)], a in class(s, a)))
- `class-subset-carrier` — forall([s], is-setoid(s) implies forall([a in pts(s)], class(s, a) subset pts(s)))
- `descend-computes` — forall([s], is-setoid(s) implies forall([z, f], f in fun(pts(s), z) and respects(s, f) implies forall([a in pts(s)], (descend(s, f))(class(s, a)) = f(a))))
- `descend-computes-rev` — forall([s], is-setoid(s) implies forall([z, f], f in fun(pts(s), z) and respects(s, f) implies forall([a in pts(s)], f(a) = (descend(s, f))(class(s, a)))))
- `descend-in-fun` — forall([s], is-setoid(s) implies forall([z, f], f in fun(pts(s), z) and respects(s, f) implies descend(s, f) in fun(quotient(s), z)))
- `descend2-computes` — forall([s], is-setoid(s) implies forall([z, f in fun(cartesian(pts(s), pts(s)), z)], respects2(s, f) implies forall([a, b], a in pts(s) implies b in pts(s) implies (descend2(s, f))(class(s, a), class(s, b)) = f(a, b))))
- `descend2-computes-rev` — forall([s], is-setoid(s) implies forall([z, f in fun(cartesian(pts(s), pts(s)), z)], respects2(s, f) implies forall([a, b], a in pts(s) implies b in pts(s) implies f(a, b) = (descend2(s, f))(class(s, a), class(s, b)))))
- `descend2-in-fun` — forall([s], is-setoid(s) implies forall([z, f in fun(cartesian(pts(s), pts(s)), z)], respects2(s, f) implies descend2(s, f) in fun(cartesian(quotient(s), quotient(s)), z)))
- `hom-setoid-id` — forall([a], is-setoid(a) implies is-hom-setoid(a, a, id-fun(pts(a))))
- `proj-in-fun` — forall([s], is-setoid(s) implies proj(s) in fun(pts(s), quotient(s)))
- `quotient-is-set` — forall([s], is-setoid(s) implies quotient(s) in set)
- `quotient-rep` — forall([s], is-setoid(s) implies forall([x in quotient(s)], forsome([a in pts(s)], x = class(s, a))))
- `quotient-universal` — forall([s], is-setoid(s) implies forall([z, f], f in fun(pts(s), z) and respects(s, f) implies forsome([g in fun(quotient(s), z)], forall([a in pts(s)], g(class(s, a)) = f(a)) and forall([g_], g_ in fun(quotient(s), z) and forall([a in pts(s)], g_(class(s, a)) = f(a)) implies g_ = g))))
- `rko2-refl` — forall([s], is-setoid(s) implies forall([a_ in pts(s)], related(s, a_, a_)))
- `rko2-sym` — forall([s], is-setoid(s) implies forall([a_, b_], a_ in pts(s) implies b_ in pts(s) implies related(s, a_, b_) implies related(s, b_, a_)))
- `rko2-trans` — forall([s], is-setoid(s) implies forall([a_, b_, c_], a_ in pts(s) implies b_ in pts(s) implies c_ in pts(s) implies related(s, a_, b_) implies related(s, b_, c_) implies related(s, a_, c_)))

### strand-space
<a id="strand-space"></a>

*Declared in* [`structure-library/strand-spaces.scm`](../structure-library/strand-spaces.scm).

*Kind.* Shape structure — `declare-structure` with slot clauses.

*Slots* (5): `tokens` carrier; `ids` carrier; `keys` carrier; `strand-map` operation ids -> strands(tokens, keys); `kinv` operation keys -> keys.

*Default arrows* (`is-hom-strand-space-def`): forall([a, b, f1, f2, f3], is-hom-strand-space(a, b, f1, f2, f3) iff is-strand-space(a) and is-strand-space(b) and f1 in fun(tokens(a), tokens(b)) and f2 in fun(ids(a), ids(b)) and f3 in fun(keys(a), keys(b)) and forall([x1_ in ids(a)], (strand-map(a))(x1_) = (strand-map(b))(f2(x1_))) and forall([x1_ in keys(a)], f3((kinv(a))(x1_)) = (kinv(b))(f3(x1_))))

*Declaration.*

```scheme
(declare-structure strand-space
  (instance-var s)
  (carriers tokens ids keys)
  (op strand-map ids (strands tokens keys))
  (op kinv keys keys)
  (law (is-token-set (tokens s)))
  (law (forall ssx_ (implies (in ssx_ (keys s)) (in ssx_ (tokens s)))))
  (law (forall ssx_ (implies (in ssx_ (keys s)) (= ((kinv s) ((kinv s) ssx_)) ssx_)))))
```

*Defining predicate* (as stored):

- `is-strand-space` — forall([s], is-strand-space(s) iff length(s) = 5 and tokens(s) in set and ids(s) in set and keys(s) in set and strand-map(s) in fun(ids(s), strands(tokens(s), keys(s))) and kinv(s) in fun(keys(s), keys(s)) and is-token-set(tokens(s)) and forall([ssx_ in keys(s)], ssx_ in tokens(s)) and forall([ssx_ in keys(s)], (kinv(s))((kinv(s))(ssx_)) = ssx_))

*Theorems quantifying over `is-strand-space`.*

- `keys-in-tokens` — forall([spa], is-strand-space(spa) implies forall([x in keys(spa)], x in tokens(spa)))
- `kinv-in-fun` — forall([spa], is-strand-space(spa) implies kinv(spa) in fun(keys(spa), keys(spa)))
- `kinv-injective` — forall([spa], is-strand-space(spa) implies forall([y, z], y in keys(spa) implies z in keys(spa) implies (kinv(spa))(y) = (kinv(spa))(z) implies y = z))
- `kinv-injective-rev` — forall([spa], is-strand-space(spa) implies forall([y, z], y in keys(spa) implies z in keys(spa) implies (kinv(spa))(y) = (kinv(spa))(z) implies z = y))
- `kinv-involution` — forall([spa], is-strand-space(spa) implies forall([y in keys(spa)], (kinv(spa))((kinv(spa))(y)) = y))
- `kinv-involution-rev` — forall([spa], is-strand-space(spa) implies forall([y in keys(spa)], y = (kinv(spa))((kinv(spa))(y))))
- `strand-space-parts` — forall([spa], is-strand-space(spa) implies is-token-set(tokens(spa)) and tokens(spa) in set and ids(spa) in set and keys(spa) in set and strand-map(spa) in fun(ids(spa), strands(tokens(spa), keys(spa))))

### top-space
<a id="top-space"></a>

*Declared in* [`structure-library/top-space.scm`](../structure-library/top-space.scm).

*Kind.* Shape structure — `declare-structure` with slot clauses.

*Slots* (2): `pts` carrier; `opens` family of subsets of pts.

*Default arrows* (`is-hom-top-space-def`): forall([s, t, f], is-hom-top-space(s, t, f) iff is-top-space(s) and is-top-space(t) and f in fun(pts(s), pts(t)) and forall([u in opens(t)], preimage(s, f, u) in opens(s)))

*Declaration.*

```scheme
(declare-structure top-space
  (instance-var s)
  (carriers pts)
  (family opens pts)
  (law (in empty-set (opens s)))
  (law (in (pts s) (opens s)))
  (law (forall u (implies (in u (opens s)) (forall v (implies (in v (opens s)) (in (intersection u v) (opens s)))))))
  (law (forall fam (implies (subset fam (opens s)) (in (big-union u fam u) (opens s))))))
```

*Defining predicate* (as stored):

- `is-top-space` — forall([s], is-top-space(s) iff length(s) = 2 and pts(s) in set and opens(s) in power(power(pts(s))) and empty-set in opens(s) and pts(s) in opens(s) and forall([u in opens(s), v in opens(s)], intersection(u, v) in opens(s)) and forall([fam], fam subset opens(s) implies big-union(u, fam, u) in opens(s)))

*Theorems quantifying over `is-top-space`.*

- `bounded-metrizable-iff-gauge-countable` — forall([s], is-top-space(s) implies forsome([md], is-bounded-metric-space(md) and metric-top(md) == s) iff is-hausdorff(s) and is-gauge-countable(s))
- `bounded-metrizable-iff-gauge-countable-rev` — forall([s], is-top-space(s) implies is-hausdorff(s) and is-gauge-countable(s) iff forsome([md], is-bounded-metric-space(md) and metric-top(md) == s))
- `hom-top-space-id` — forall([s], is-top-space(s) implies is-hom-top-space(s, s, id-fun(pts(s))))
- `metrizable-iff-bounded-metrizable` — forall([s], is-top-space(s) implies is-metrizable-top-space(s) iff forsome([md], is-bounded-metric-space(md) and metric-top(md) == s))
- `metrizable-iff-bounded-metrizable-rev` — forall([s], is-top-space(s) implies forsome([md], is-bounded-metric-space(md) and metric-top(md) == s) iff is-metrizable-top-space(s))
- `metrizable-iff-gauge-countable` — forall([s], is-top-space(s) implies is-metrizable-top-space(s) iff is-hausdorff(s) and is-gauge-countable(s))
- `metrizable-iff-gauge-countable-rev` — forall([s], is-top-space(s) implies is-hausdorff(s) and is-gauge-countable(s) iff is-metrizable-top-space(s))
- `rb3-mgc-fwd` — forall([s], is-top-space(s) implies is-metrizable-top-space(s) implies is-hausdorff(s) and is-gauge-countable(s))

### vector-space
<a id="vector-space"></a>

*Declared in* [`structure-library/finite-dimensional.scm`](../structure-library/finite-dimensional.scm).

*Kind.* Refinement — `declare-structure` with `(same-shape-as module)`; its `IS-X <=> IS-PARENT and <laws>` axiom is generated, not hand-written.  Subtype of [`module`](#module).  Shape inherited from `module` — slots (scal vec vadd vzero vneg act).

*Declaration.*

```scheme
(declare-structure vector-space
  (instance-var s)
  (same-shape-as module)
  (law "is-field-ring(scal(s))"))
```

*Defining predicate* (as stored):

- `is-vector-space-def` — forall([s], is-vector-space(s) iff is-module(s) and is-field-ring(scal(s)))

*Theorems quantifying over `is-vector-space`.*

- `hom-vector-space-id` — forall([a], is-vector-space(a) implies is-hom-vector-space(a, a, id-fun(vec(a))))
- `vspace-vec-is-set` — forall([m], is-vector-space(m) implies vec(m) in set)

### zz-ring
<a id="zz-ring"></a>

*Declared in* [`structure-library/numeric-instances.scm`](../structure-library/numeric-instances.scm).

*Kind.* Instance — a constant tuple (an *element* of the class, not a predicate).  Subtype of [`euclidean-ring`](#euclidean-ring).

*Member of:*

- `is-ring` (via `zz-is-ring`)
- `is-commutative-ring` (via `zz-is-commutative-ring`)
- `is-integral-domain` (via `zz-is-integral-domain`)
- `is-euclidean-ring` (via `zz-is-euclidean-ring`)

*Components* (mapped to inherited `euclidean-ring` slots):

- `carr` = `zz`
- `add` = `vnb-lambda([x_, y_], cartesian(zz, zz), x_ + y_)`
- `mul` = `vnb-lambda([x_, y_], cartesian(zz, zz), x_ * y_)`
- `neg` = `vnb-lambda(x_, zz, -x_)`
- `zero` = `0`
- `one` = `1`

## View-as declarations (alphabetical)

A view-as is a *forgetful functor* between structure categories, in exactly two cases: it forgets **shape** (keeps a sub-list of the source's slots) and/or forgets **properties** (the target's laws are fewer than the source's).  The slot map below is written in the *target's* coordinates: position k shows which source accessor fills the target's k-th slot.  Non-forgetful functors (quotient, completion) are constructions, not views.

- `abelian-group-as-monoid` — `abelian-group` → `monoid`: (carr opr iden) ↦ (carr opr iden)
- `commutative-ring-additive-ag` — `commutative-ring` → `abelian-group`: (carr add zero neg) ↦ (carr opr iden inv)
- `commutative-ring-multiplicative-cm` — `commutative-ring` → `comm-monoid`: (carr mul one) ↦ (carr opr iden)
- `complex-inner-product-space-as-module` — `complex-inner-product-space` → `module`: (scal vec vadd vzero vneg act) ↦ (scal vec vadd vzero vneg act)
- `csip-as-sip` — `complex-semi-inner-product-space` → `semi-inner-product-space`: (scal vec vadd vzero vneg act rip) ↦ (scal vec vadd vzero vneg act rip)
- `field-additive-ag` — `field` → `abelian-group`: (carr add zero neg) ↦ (carr opr iden inv)
- `field-as-euclidean-ring` — `field` → `euclidean-ring`: (carr add mul neg zero one) ↦ (carr add mul neg zero one)
- `field-as-integral-domain` — `field` → `integral-domain`: (carr add mul neg zero one) ↦ (carr add mul neg zero one)
- `field-multiplicative-group` — `field` → `group`: (non-zero mul one mul-inv) ↦ (carr opr iden inv)
- `measure-space-as-measurable-space` — `measure-space` → `measurable-space`: (pts sigma) ↦ (pts sigma)
- `module-vector-ag` — `module` → `abelian-group`: (vec vadd vzero vneg) ↦ (carr opr iden inv)
- `normed-ag-as-abelian-group` — `normed-ag` → `abelian-group`: (carr opr iden inv) ↦ (carr opr iden inv)
- `normed-field-additive-ag` — `normed-field` → `abelian-group`: (carr add zero neg) ↦ (carr opr iden inv)
- `normed-field-as-commutative-ring` — `normed-field` → `commutative-ring`: (carr add mul neg zero one) ↦ (carr add mul neg zero one)
- `normed-field-as-integral-domain` — `normed-field` → `integral-domain`: (carr add mul neg zero one) ↦ (carr add mul neg zero one)
- `normed-vector-space-as-module` — `normed-vector-space` → `module`: (scal vec vadd vzero vneg act) ↦ (scal vec vadd vzero vneg act)
- `normed-vector-space-as-normed-ag` — `normed-vector-space` → `normed-ag`: (vec vadd vzero vneg vnrm) ↦ (carr opr iden inv nrm)
- `ring-additive-ag` — `ring` → `abelian-group`: (carr add zero neg) ↦ (carr opr iden inv)
- `ring-multiplicative-monoid` — `ring` → `monoid`: (carr mul one) ↦ (carr opr iden)
- `ringoid-as-ring` — `ringoid` → `ring`: (carr add mul neg zero one) ↦ (carr add mul neg zero one)
- `sip-as-module` — `semi-inner-product-space` → `module`: (scal vec vadd vzero vneg act) ↦ (scal vec vadd vzero vneg act)
